using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Albums;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Files;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;
using WinCapture.Services;

namespace WinCapture.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/admin")]
[Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.Admin")]
public sealed class AdminController(IFileService fileService, IAlbumService albumService) : ControllerBase
{
    // Gets the mapped WinCapture user ID for retrieving the current admin's own resources.
    private int UserId
    {
        get
        {
            var value = User.FindFirst("wincapture_user_id")?.Value;

            if (!int.TryParse(value, out var userId) || userId <= 0)
            {
                throw new UnauthorizedException("The authenticated user ID is missing or invalid.");
            }

            return userId;
        }
    }

    // Returns files uploaded by all users for admin management.
    [HttpGet("files")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<AdminFileResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<AdminFileResponse>>> GetFiles(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await fileService.GetAllPageAsync(pagination));
    }

    // Returns albums across all users for admin management.
    [HttpGet("albums")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>> GetAlbums(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await albumService.GetAllForAdminPageAsync(pagination));
    }

    // Returns only the albums owned by the current admin.
    [HttpGet("albums/mine")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>> GetMyAlbums(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await albumService.GetOwnedByUserPageAsync(UserId, pagination));
    }

    // Returns only the files uploaded by the current admin.
    [HttpGet("files/mine")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<AdminFileResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<AdminFileResponse>>> GetMyFiles(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await fileService.GetAdminFilesByUploaderIdPageAsync(UserId, pagination));
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Albums;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Files;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;
using WinCapture.Models;
using WinCapture.Services;
using WinCapture.Validators;

namespace WinCapture.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/albums")]
[Authorize]
public sealed class AlbumsController(IAlbumService albumService, IAlbumFileService albumFileService) : ControllerBase
{
    // Allows space for multipart form data beyond the maximum file size.
    private const long RequestOverhead = 1024L * 1024L;

    private const long MaximumRequestSize = FileValidator.MaximumFileSize + RequestOverhead;

    // Gets the local WinCapture user ID mapped to the authenticated Entra account.
    private int UserId
    {
        get
        {
            var value = User.FindFirst("wincapture_user_id")?.Value;

            if (!int.TryParse(value, out var userId) || userId <= 0)
            {
                throw new UnauthorizedException("The authenticated user ID is missing or invalid.");
            }

            return userId;
        }
    }

    // Maps the authenticated Entra app role to the role used by WinCapture services.
    private UserRole CurrentUserRole
    {
        get
        {
            if (User.IsInRole("WinCapture.Admin"))
            {
                return UserRole.Admin;
            }

            if (User.IsInRole("WinCapture.User"))
            {
                return UserRole.User;
            }

            throw new ForbiddenException("The authenticated user role is missing or invalid.");
        }
    }

    // Builds the user context required by the album services from the authenticated claims.
    private User CurrentUser =>
        new()
        {
            Id = UserId,
            Name = User.FindFirst("name")?.Value ?? string.Empty,
            Email = User.FindFirst("email")?.Value
                ?? User.FindFirst("preferred_username")?.Value
                ?? string.Empty,
            Role = CurrentUserRole
        };

    // Creates an album for the current user and returns its location.
    [HttpPost]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AlbumResponse>> Create(CreateAlbumRequest request)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;
        var response = await albumService.CreateAsync(request, user);

        return Created($"/api/albums/{response.Id}", response);
    }

    // Returns the albums the current user is allowed to access, including shared albums.
    [HttpGet]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>> GetAll(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        return Ok(await albumService.GetAccessiblePageAsync(user, pagination));
    }

    // Returns only the albums owned by the current user.
    [HttpGet("mine")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>> GetOwnedByCurrentUser(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await albumService.GetOwnedByUserPageAsync(UserId, pagination));
    }

    [HttpGet("shared")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>> GetSharedWithCurrentUser(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await albumService.GetSharedWithUserPageAsync(UserId, pagination));
    }

    // Retrieves an album using the current user's identity and role for access checks.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AlbumResponse>> Get(long id)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        return Ok(await albumService.GetAsync(id, user, CurrentUserRole));
    }

    // Passes the current user's identity and role to the service when updating an album.
    [HttpPut("{id:long}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AlbumResponse>> Update(long id, UpdateAlbumRequest request)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        return Ok(await albumService.UpdateAsync(id, request, user, CurrentUserRole));
    }

    // Delegates album deletion and access checks to the album service.
    [HttpDelete("{id:long}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(long id)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        await albumService.DeleteAsync(id, user, CurrentUserRole);

        return NoContent();
    }

    // Uploads a file to the specified album within the configured request-size limit.
    [HttpPost("{id:long}/files")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [Consumes("multipart/form-data")]
    [RequestSizeLimit(MaximumRequestSize)]
    [RequestFormLimits(MultipartBodyLengthLimit = MaximumRequestSize)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<FileResponse>> UploadFile(long id, IFormFile file)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;
        var response = await albumFileService.UploadAsync(id, file, user, CurrentUserRole);

        return Created(response.Url, response);
    }

    // Replaces a file in the specified album while enforcing the configured request-size limit.
    [HttpPut("{id:long}/files/{fileId:long}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [Consumes("multipart/form-data")]
    [RequestSizeLimit(MaximumRequestSize)]
    [RequestFormLimits(MultipartBodyLengthLimit = MaximumRequestSize)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<FileResponse>> ReplaceFile(long id, long fileId, IFormFile file)
    {
        var response = await albumFileService.ReplaceAsync(id, fileId, file, CurrentUser, CurrentUserRole);

        return Ok(response);
    }

    // Returns the files in an album after applying the current user's access rules.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}/files")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<FileResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<FileResponse>>> GetFiles(
        long id,
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        return Ok(await albumFileService.GetPageAsync(id, user, CurrentUserRole, pagination));
    }

    // Retrieves a specific file using the current user's access context.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}/files/{fileId:long}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<FileResponse>> GetFile(long id, long fileId)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        return Ok(await albumFileService.GetOneAsync(id, fileId, user, CurrentUserRole));
    }

    // Returns the file content for in-browser preview without setting a download filename.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}/files/{fileId:long}/preview")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> PreviewFile(long id, long fileId)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;
        var result = await albumFileService.PreviewAsync(id, fileId, user, CurrentUserRole);

        return File(result.Content, result.ContentType);
    }

    // Returns the file content with its download filename.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}/files/{fileId:long}/download")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DownloadFile(long id, long fileId)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;
        var result = await albumFileService.DownloadAsync(id, fileId, user, CurrentUserRole);

        return File(result.Content, result.ContentType, result.DownloadName);
    }

    // Delegates file deletion and album access checks to the album file service.
    [HttpDelete("{id:long}/files/{fileId:long}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteFile(long id, long fileId)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        var storageCleanupComplete = await albumFileService.DeleteAsync(id, fileId, user, CurrentUserRole);
        return Ok(new { storageCleanupComplete });
    }

    [HttpDelete("{id:long}/files/{fileId:long}/association")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveFileFromAlbum(long id, long fileId)
    {
        await albumFileService.RemoveFromAlbumAsync(id, fileId, CurrentUser, CurrentUserRole);
        return NoContent();
    }

    // Returns the members of an album according to the current user's access permissions.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}/members")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<AlbumMemberResponse>>> GetMembers(long id)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        return Ok(await albumService.GetMembersAsync(id, user, CurrentUserRole));
    }

    // Adds an album member using the current user's identity and role.
    [HttpPost("{id:long}/members")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AddMember(long id, AddAlbumMemberRequest request)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        await albumService.AddMemberAsync(id, request, user, CurrentUserRole);

        return NoContent();
    }

    // Updates an album member's permissions through the album service.
    [HttpPut("{id:long}/members/{userId:int}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateMember(long id, int userId, UpdateAlbumMemberRequest request)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        await albumService.UpdateMemberAsync(id, userId, request, user, CurrentUserRole);

        return NoContent();
    }

    // Removes an album member through the album service.
    [HttpDelete("{id:long}/members/{userId:int}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveMember(long id, int userId)
    {
        var user = CurrentUser;

        await albumService.RemoveMemberAsync(id, userId, user, CurrentUserRole);

        return NoContent();
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Auth;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;

namespace WinCapture.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/auth")]
public sealed class AuthController : ControllerBase
{
    // Returns the authenticated WinCapture user's details after validating the mapped user ID and role.
    [Authorize]
    [HttpGet("me")]
    [ProducesResponseType<CurrentUserResponse>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    public ActionResult<CurrentUserResponse> Me()
    {
        var userId = User.FindFirst("wincapture_user_id")?.Value;

        if (!int.TryParse(userId, out var parsedUserId) || parsedUserId <= 0)
        {
            throw new UnauthorizedException("The authenticated WinCapture user ID is missing or invalid.");
        }

        var role = GetWinCaptureRole();

        if (role is null)
        {
            throw new ForbiddenException("The Microsoft account is authenticated but is not assigned to a WinCapture role.");
        }

        var name = User.FindFirst("name")?.Value ?? string.Empty;
        var email = User.FindFirst("preferred_username")?.Value
            ?? User.FindFirst("email")?.Value
            ?? User.FindFirst("upn")?.Value
            ?? string.Empty;

        return Ok(new CurrentUserResponse(
            parsedUserId,
            name,
            email,
            role,
            "Microsoft Entra ID"));
    }

    // Maps the Microsoft Entra app role to the role name returned by WinCapture.
    private string? GetWinCaptureRole()
    {
        if (User.IsInRole("WinCapture.Admin"))
        {
            return "Admin";
        }

        if (User.IsInRole("WinCapture.User"))
        {
            return "User";
        }

        return null;
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Files;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;
using WinCapture.Models;
using WinCapture.Services;
using WinCapture.Validators;

namespace WinCapture.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/files")]
[Authorize]
public sealed class FilesController(IFileService fileService) : ControllerBase
{
    // Allows space for multipart form data beyond the maximum file size.
    private const long RequestOverhead = 1024L * 1024L;

    private const long MaximumRequestSize = FileValidator.MaximumFileSize + RequestOverhead;

    // Gets the mapped WinCapture user ID from the authenticated user's claims.
    private int UserId
    {
        get
        {
            var value = User.FindFirst("wincapture_user_id")?.Value;

            if (!int.TryParse(value, out var userId) || userId <= 0)
            {
                throw new UnauthorizedException("The authenticated user ID is missing or invalid.");
            }

            return userId;
        }
    }

    // Gets the WinCapture role from the authenticated user's Entra app roles.
    private UserRole CurrentUserRole
    {
        get
        {
            if (User.IsInRole("WinCapture.Admin"))
            {
                return UserRole.Admin;
            }

            if (User.IsInRole("WinCapture.User"))
            {
                return UserRole.User;
            }

            throw new ForbiddenException("The authenticated user role is missing or invalid.");
        }
    }

    // Uploads a new file for the current user and returns its location.
    [HttpPost]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [Consumes("multipart/form-data")]
    [RequestSizeLimit(MaximumRequestSize)]
    [RequestFormLimits(MultipartBodyLengthLimit = MaximumRequestSize)]
    [ProducesResponseType<FileResponse>(StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status413PayloadTooLarge)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<FileResponse>> Upload(IFormFile file)
    {
        var response = await fileService.UploadAsync(file, UserId);

        return Created($"/api/files/{response.Id}", response);
    }

    // Returns the files available in the current user's gallery.
    [HttpGet]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<FileResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<FileResponse>>> GetGallery(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await fileService.GetGalleryPageAsync(UserId, pagination));
    }

    // Returns the current user's favorite files according to their WinCapture role.
    [HttpGet("favorites")]
    [ProducesResponseType<PagedResponse<FileResponse>>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<FileResponse>>> GetFavorites(
        [FromQuery] PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return Ok(await fileService.GetFavoritesPageAsync(UserId, CurrentUserRole, pagination));
    }

    // Adds the specified file to the current user's favorites.
    [HttpPut("{id:long}/favorite")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AddFavorite(long id)
    {
        await fileService.AddFavoriteAsync(id, UserId, CurrentUserRole);

        return NoContent();
    }

    // Removes the specified file from the current user's favorites.
    [HttpDelete("{id:long}/favorite")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveFavorite(long id)
    {
        await fileService.RemoveFavoriteAsync(id, UserId);

        return NoContent();
    }

    // Retrieves a file after the service checks the current user's access permissions.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}")]
    [ProducesResponseType<FileResponse>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<FileResponse>> Get(long id)
    {
        return Ok(await fileService.GetAsync(id, UserId, CurrentUserRole));
    }

    // Replaces an existing file while enforcing the configured request-size limit.
    [HttpPut("{id:long}")]
    [Authorize(Roles = "WinCapture.User,WinCapture.Admin")]
    [Consumes("multipart/form-data")]
    [RequestSizeLimit(MaximumRequestSize)]
    [RequestFormLimits(MultipartBodyLengthLimit = MaximumRequestSize)]
    [ProducesResponseType<FileResponse>(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status413PayloadTooLarge)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<FileResponse>> Replace(long id, IFormFile file)
    {
        var response = await fileService.ReplaceAsync(id, file, UserId, CurrentUserRole);

        return Ok(response);
    }

    // Returns the file content with its download filename.
    [HttpGet("{id:long}/download")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Download(long id)
    {
        var result = await fileService.DownloadAsync(id, UserId, CurrentUserRole);

        return File(result.Content, result.ContentType, result.DownloadName);
    }

    // Deletes the specified file after the service checks the current user's permissions.
    [HttpDelete("{id:long}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(long id)
    {
        var storageCleanupComplete = await fileService.DeleteAsync(id, UserId, CurrentUserRole);
        return Ok(new { storageCleanupComplete });
    }
}

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using WinCapture.Models;

namespace WinCapture.Data;

public sealed class WinCaptureDbContext(DbContextOptions<WinCaptureDbContext> options) : DbContext(options)
{
    public DbSet<User> Users => Set<User>();

    public DbSet<FileMetadata> Files => Set<FileMetadata>();

    public DbSet<Album> Albums => Set<Album>();

    public DbSet<AlbumAccess> AlbumAccess => Set<AlbumAccess>();

    public DbSet<Favorite> Favorites => Set<Favorite>();

    // Configures the database tables, constraints, indexes, and relationships used by WinCapture.
    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<User>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("Users");

            entity.HasKey(user => user.Id);

            entity.Property(user => user.Id)
                .ValueGeneratedOnAdd();

            entity.Property(user => user.Name)
                .HasMaxLength(200)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(user => user.Email)
                .HasMaxLength(320)
                .IsRequired();

            // Prevents multiple user accounts from using the same email address or Entra object ID.
            entity.HasIndex(user => user.Email)
                .IsUnique();

            entity.Property(user => user.EntraObjectId)
                .HasMaxLength(100)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.HasIndex(user => user.EntraObjectId)
                .IsUnique();

            entity.Property(user => user.Role)
                .HasConversion<string>()
                .HasMaxLength(20)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(user => user.CreatedAt)
                .HasColumnType("datetime2")
                .IsRequired();
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<FileMetadata>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("Files");

            entity.HasKey(file => file.Id);

            entity.Property(file => file.Id)
                .ValueGeneratedOnAdd();

            entity.Property(file => file.OriginalFileName)
                .HasMaxLength(260)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(file => file.StoredFileName)
                .HasMaxLength(260)
                .IsRequired();

            // Ensures each file has a unique name in storage.
            entity.HasIndex(file => file.StoredFileName)
                .IsUnique();

            entity.Property(file => file.ContentType)
                .HasMaxLength(100)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(file => file.FileSize)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(file => file.UploadedBy)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(file => file.AlbumId);

            entity.Property(file => file.UploadedAt)
                .HasColumnType("datetime2")
                .IsRequired();

            entity.HasIndex(file => file.UploadedBy);

            entity.HasIndex(file => file.AlbumId);

            entity.HasIndex(file => file.UploadedAt);

            // Prevents deleting a user while their uploaded files still reference them.
            entity.HasOne(file => file.User)
                .WithMany(user => user.Files)
                .HasForeignKey(file => file.UploadedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            // Removes the associated file records when an album is deleted.
            entity.HasOne(file => file.Album)
                .WithMany(album => album.Files)
                .HasForeignKey(file => file.AlbumId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Album>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("Albums");

            entity.HasKey(album => album.Id);

            entity.Property(album => album.Id)
                .ValueGeneratedOnAdd();

            entity.Property(album => album.AlbumName)
                .HasMaxLength(200)
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(album => album.CreatedAt)
                .HasColumnType("datetime2")
                .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(album => album.UpdatedAt)
                .HasColumnType("datetime2");

            entity.HasIndex(album => album.CreatedBy);

            // Prevents deleting the album owner while their albums still reference them.
            entity.HasOne(album => album.Owner)
                .WithMany(user => user.Albums)
                .HasForeignKey(album => album.CreatedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne<User>()
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(album => album.UpdatedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<AlbumAccess>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("AlbumAccess");

            entity.HasKey(access => access.Id);

            entity.Property(access => access.Id)
                .ValueGeneratedOnAdd();

            entity.Property(access => access.GrantedAt)
                .HasColumnType("datetime2")
                .IsRequired();

            // Prevents the same user from receiving duplicate access entries for an album.
            entity.HasIndex(access => new
                {
                    access.AlbumId,
                    access.UserId
                })
                .IsUnique();

            entity.HasIndex(access => access.UserId);

            // Removes album access entries when the associated album is deleted.
            entity.HasOne(access => access.Album)
                .WithMany(album => album.Access)
                .HasForeignKey(access => access.AlbumId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasOne(access => access.User)
                .WithMany(user => user.AlbumAccess)
                .HasForeignKey(access => access.UserId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne<User>()
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(access => access.GrantedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Favorite>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("Favorites");

            // Uses the user and file IDs together to prevent duplicate favorites.
            entity.HasKey(favorite => new
            {
                favorite.UserId,
                favorite.FileId
            });

            entity.Property(favorite => favorite.FavoritedAt)
                .HasColumnType("datetime2")
                .IsRequired();

            entity.HasIndex(favorite => favorite.FileId);

            // Removes a user's favorites when that user is deleted.
            entity.HasOne(favorite => favorite.User)
                .WithMany(user => user.Favorites)
                .HasForeignKey(favorite => favorite.UserId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            // Removes favorite entries when the associated file is deleted.
            entity.HasOne(favorite => favorite.File)
                .WithMany(file => file.Favorites)
                .HasForeignKey(favorite => favorite.FileId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });
    }
}

using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace WinCapture.DTOs.Common;

public sealed class PaginationQuery
{
    [Range(1, int.MaxValue)]
    public int Page { get; set; } = 1;

    [Range(1, 100)]
    public int PageSize { get; set; } = 10;

    public string? Search { get; set; }

    [RegularExpression("^(All|Images|Documents|Videos|Audio|Archives)$")]
    public string? Category { get; set; }

    public int Offset => (int)Math.Min((long)(Page - 1) * PageSize, int.MaxValue);
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Middleware\ExceptionMiddleware.cs
============================================================

using System.Text.Json;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;

namespace WinCapture.Middleware;

public sealed class ExceptionMiddleware(RequestDelegate next, ILogger<ExceptionMiddleware> logger)
{
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);
    // Catches exceptions from the request pipeline and passes them to the central handler.
    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        try
        {
            await next(context);
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            await HandleExceptionAsync(context, exception);
        }
    }

    // Converts exceptions into HTTP responses and logs unexpected server errors.
    private async Task HandleExceptionAsync(HttpContext context, Exception exception)
    {
        var statusCode = exception switch
        {
            BadRequestException => StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest,
            ConflictException => StatusCodes.Status409Conflict,
            UnauthorizedException => StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized,
            PayloadTooLargeException => StatusCodes.Status413PayloadTooLarge,
            ForbiddenException => StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden,
            NotFoundException => StatusCodes.Status404NotFound,
            StorageException => StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError,
            _ => StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError
        };

        if (statusCode >= StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError)
        {
            logger.LogError(
                exception,
                "Unhandled exception for {Method} {Path}. RequestId: {RequestId}",
                context.Request.Method,
                context.Request.Path,
                context.TraceIdentifier);
        }

        context.Response.StatusCode = statusCode;
        context.Response.ContentType = "application/json";

        var message = statusCode == StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError
            ? "An unexpected error occurred."
            : exception.Message;

        var response = new ErrorResponse(statusCode, message);

        await context.Response.WriteAsync(JsonSerializer.Serialize(response, JsonOptions));
    }
}

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using WinCapture.Data;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.Models;

namespace WinCapture.Repositories;

public sealed class AlbumRepository(WinCaptureDbContext db) : IAlbumRepository
{
    // Adds a new album to the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task AddAsync(Album album)
    {
        db.Albums.Add(album);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Gets an album by ID and includes its owner details.
    public async Task<Album?> GetByIdAsync(long albumId)
    {
        return await db.Albums
            .Include(album => album.Owner)
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(album => album.Id == albumId);
    }

    // Returns albums owned by the user or shared with them with view permission.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<Album>> GetAccessibleByUserIdAsync(int userId)
    {
        return await db.Albums
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(album => album.Owner)
            .Where(album =>
                album.CreatedBy == userId ||
                album.Access.Any(access =>
                    access.UserId == userId &&
                    access.CanView))
            .OrderByDescending(album => album.CreatedAt)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedAccessibleByUserIdAsync(
        int userId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        IQueryable<Album> query = db.Albums
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(album => album.Owner)
            .Where(album =>
                album.CreatedBy == userId ||
                album.Access.Any(access =>
                    access.UserId == userId &&
                    access.CanView));

        var search = pagination.Search?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(search))
        {
            query = query.Where(album =>
                album.AlbumName.Contains(search) ||
                album.Id.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.CreatedBy.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.Owner.Name.Contains(search));
        }

        return await query
            .OrderByDescending(album => album.CreatedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(album => album.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    // Returns only the albums owned by the specified user, newest first.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<Album>> GetOwnedByUserIdAsync(int userId)
    {
        return await db.Albums
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(album => album.Owner)
            .Where(album => album.CreatedBy == userId)
            .OrderByDescending(album => album.CreatedAt)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedOwnedByUserIdAsync(
        int userId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        IQueryable<Album> query = db.Albums
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(album => album.Owner)
            .Where(album => album.CreatedBy == userId);

        var search = pagination.Search?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(search))
        {
            query = query.Where(album =>
                album.AlbumName.Contains(search) ||
                album.Id.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.CreatedBy.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.Owner.Name.Contains(search));
        }

        return await query
            .OrderByDescending(album => album.CreatedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(album => album.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedSharedWithUserIdAsync(
        int userId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        IQueryable<Album> query = db.Albums
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(album => album.Owner)
            .Where(album => album.CreatedBy != userId &&
                album.Access.Any(access => access.UserId == userId && access.CanView));

        var search = pagination.Search?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(search))
        {
            query = query.Where(album =>
                album.AlbumName.Contains(search) ||
                album.Id.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.CreatedBy.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.Owner.Name.Contains(search));
        }

        return await query
            .OrderByDescending(album => album.CreatedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(album => album.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    // Returns all albums with their owner details, newest first.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<Album>> GetAllAsync()
    {
        return await db.Albums
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(album => album.Owner)
            .OrderByDescending(album => album.CreatedAt)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedAllAsync(PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        IQueryable<Album> query = db.Albums
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(album => album.Owner);

        var search = pagination.Search?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(search))
        {
            query = query.Where(album =>
                album.AlbumName.Contains(search) ||
                album.Id.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.CreatedBy.ToString().Contains(search) ||
                album.Owner.Name.Contains(search));
        }

        return await query
            .OrderByDescending(album => album.CreatedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(album => album.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    // Updates the album in the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task UpdateAsync(Album album)
    {
        db.Albums.Update(album);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Removes the album from the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task DeleteAsync(Album album)
    {
        db.Albums.Remove(album);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Gets a user's access entry for the specified album, if one exists.
    public async Task<AlbumAccess?> GetAccessAsync(long albumId, int userId)
    {
        return await db.AlbumAccess
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(access =>
                access.AlbumId == albumId &&
                access.UserId == userId);
    }

    // Returns the access entries for an album with user details, ordered by name.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AlbumAccess>> GetAccessListAsync(long albumId)
    {
        return await db.AlbumAccess
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(access => access.User)
            .Where(access => access.AlbumId == albumId)
            .OrderBy(access => access.User.Name)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    // Grants a user access to an album and saves the changes.
    public async Task AddAccessAsync(AlbumAccess access)
    {
        db.AlbumAccess.Add(access);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Updates an album access entry and saves the changes.
    public async Task UpdateAccessAsync(AlbumAccess access)
    {
        db.AlbumAccess.Update(access);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Removes an album access entry and saves the changes.
    public async Task DeleteAccessAsync(AlbumAccess access)
    {
        db.AlbumAccess.Remove(access);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Repositories\FileQueryExtensions.cs
============================================================

using System.Linq.Expressions;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.Models;

namespace WinCapture.Repositories;

internal static class FileQueryExtensions
{
    private static readonly string[][] CategoryExtensions =
    [
        [".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"],
        [".pdf", ".txt", ".csv", ".docx", ".xlsx", ".pptx"],
        [".mp4", ".mov", ".webm"],
        [".mp3", ".wav", ".m4a"],
        [".zip"]
    ];

    private static readonly string[] CategoryNames =
        ["Images", "Documents", "Videos", "Audio", "Archives"];

    public static IQueryable<FileMetadata> ApplyFileFilters(
        this IQueryable<FileMetadata> query,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var search = pagination.Search?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(search))
        {
            query = query.Where(file =>
                file.OriginalFileName.Contains(search) ||
                file.ContentType.Contains(search) ||
                file.Id.ToString().Contains(search));
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pagination.Category) && pagination.Category != "All")
        {
            var categoryIndex = Array.IndexOf(CategoryNames, pagination.Category);
            if (categoryIndex >= 0)
            {
                query = ApplyCategory(query, CategoryExtensions[categoryIndex]);
            }
        }

        return query;
    }

    public static IQueryable<FileMetadata> ApplyFileCategory(
        this IQueryable<FileMetadata> query,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pagination.Category) && pagination.Category != "All")
        {
            var categoryIndex = Array.IndexOf(CategoryNames, pagination.Category);
            if (categoryIndex >= 0)
            {
                query = ApplyCategory(query, CategoryExtensions[categoryIndex]);
            }
        }

        return query;
    }

    private static IQueryable<FileMetadata> ApplyCategory(
        IQueryable<FileMetadata> query,
        string[] extensions)
    {
        var file = Expression.Parameter(typeof(FileMetadata), "file");
        var fileName = Expression.Property(file, nameof(FileMetadata.OriginalFileName));
        var endsWith = typeof(string).GetMethod(nameof(string.EndsWith), [typeof(string)])!;
        Expression? categoryMatch = null;

        foreach (var extension in extensions)
        {
            var match = Expression.Call(fileName, endsWith, Expression.Constant(extension));
            categoryMatch = categoryMatch is null
                ? match
                : Expression.OrElse(categoryMatch, match);
        }

        return query.Where(Expression.Lambda<Func<FileMetadata, bool>>(categoryMatch!, file));
    }
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Repositories\FileRepository.cs

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using WinCapture.Data;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.Models;

namespace WinCapture.Repositories;

public sealed class FileRepository(WinCaptureDbContext db) : IFileRepository
{
    // Adds the file metadata to the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task AddAsync(FileMetadata file)
    {
        db.Files.Add(file);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Retrieves a file by ID without tracking it because the query is read-only.
    public async Task<FileMetadata?> GetByIdAsync(long fileId)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(file => file.Id == fileId);
    }

    // Returns the files uploaded by the specified user, newest first.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<FileMetadata>> GetByUserIdAsync(int userId)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(file => file.UploadedBy == userId)
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<FileMetadata>> GetPagedByUserIdAsync(
        int userId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(file => file.UploadedBy == userId)
            .ApplyFileFilters(pagination)
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(file => file.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    // Returns the files belonging to the specified album, newest first.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<FileMetadata>> GetByAlbumIdAsync(long albumId)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(file => file.AlbumId == albumId)
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<FileMetadata>> GetPagedByAlbumIdAsync(
        long albumId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(file => file.AlbumId == albumId)
            .ApplyFileFilters(pagination)
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(file => file.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    // Returns all files with their uploader and album details for admin management.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<FileMetadata>> GetAllAsync()
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(file => file.User)
            .Include(file => file.Album)
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<FileMetadata>> GetPagedAllAsync(PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(file => file.User)
            .Include(file => file.Album)
            .ApplyFileCategory(pagination)
            .Where(file =>
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pagination.Search) ||
                file.OriginalFileName.Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.ContentType.Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.Id.ToString().Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.UploadedBy.ToString().Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.User.Name.Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                (file.AlbumId != null && file.AlbumId.ToString()!.Contains(pagination.Search)) ||
                (file.Album != null && file.Album.AlbumName.Contains(pagination.Search)))
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(file => file.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    // Returns the specified admin's uploaded files with uploader and album details.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<FileMetadata>> GetAdminFilesByUploaderIdAsync(int userId)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(file => file.User)
            .Include(file => file.Album)
            .Where(file => file.UploadedBy == userId)
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<FileMetadata>> GetPagedAdminFilesByUploaderIdAsync(
        int userId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        return await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(file => file.User)
            .Include(file => file.Album)
            .Where(file => file.UploadedBy == userId)
            .ApplyFileCategory(pagination)
            .Where(file =>
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pagination.Search) ||
                file.OriginalFileName.Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.ContentType.Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.Id.ToString().Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.UploadedBy.ToString().Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                file.User.Name.Contains(pagination.Search) ||
                (file.AlbumId != null && file.AlbumId.ToString()!.Contains(pagination.Search)) ||
                (file.Album != null && file.Album.AlbumName.Contains(pagination.Search)))
            .OrderByDescending(file => file.UploadedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(file => file.Id)
            .ToPagedResponseAsync(pagination);
    }

    // Updates the file metadata in the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task UpdateAsync(FileMetadata file)
    {
        db.Files.Update(file);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Removes the file metadata from the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task DeleteAsync(FileMetadata file)
    {
        db.Files.Remove(file);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Returns the IDs of files favorited by the specified user.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<long>> GetFavoriteFileIdsByUserIdAsync(int userId)
    {
        return await db.Favorites
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(favorite => favorite.UserId == userId)
            .Select(favorite => favorite.FileId)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<long>> GetFavoriteFileIdsByUserIdAsync(
        int userId,
        IReadOnlyCollection<long> fileIds)
    {
        return await db.Favorites
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(favorite => favorite.UserId == userId && fileIds.Contains(favorite.FileId))
            .Select(favorite => favorite.FileId)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    // Returns the user's favorite files, ordered by when they were favorited.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<FileMetadata>> GetFavoriteFilesByUserIdAsync(int userId)
    {
        return await db.Favorites
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(favorite => favorite.File)
                .ThenInclude(file => file.Album)
                    .ThenInclude(album => album!.Access.Where(access => access.UserId == userId))
            .Where(favorite => favorite.UserId == userId)
            .OrderByDescending(favorite => favorite.FavoritedAt)
            .Select(favorite => favorite.File)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<FileMetadata>> GetPagedFavoriteFilesByUserIdAsync(
        int userId,
        UserRole userRole,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        IQueryable<Favorite> query = db.Favorites
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(favorite => favorite.UserId == userId);

        // Keep the role check out of the translated SQL predicate. Besides making the
        // access rule easier to read, this avoids provider-specific enum translation.
        if (userRole != UserRole.Admin)
        {
            query = query.Where(favorite =>
                (favorite.File.AlbumId == null && favorite.File.UploadedBy == userId) ||
                (favorite.File.Album != null &&
                    (favorite.File.Album.CreatedBy == userId ||
                     favorite.File.Album.Access.Any(access =>
                         access.UserId == userId && access.CanView))));
        }

        var search = pagination.Search?.Trim();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(search))
        {
            query = query.Where(favorite =>
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.Contains(search) ||
                favorite.File.ContentType.Contains(search) ||
                favorite.File.Id.ToString().Contains(search));
        }

        query = pagination.Category switch
        {
            "Images" => query.Where(favorite =>
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".jpg") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".jpeg") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".png") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".webp")),
            "Documents" => query.Where(favorite =>
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".pdf") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".txt") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".csv") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".docx") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".xlsx") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".pptx")),
            "Videos" => query.Where(favorite =>
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".mp4") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".mov") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".webm")),
            "Audio" => query.Where(favorite =>
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".mp3") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".wav") ||
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".m4a")),
            "Archives" => query.Where(favorite =>
                favorite.File.OriginalFileName.EndsWith(".zip")),
            _ => query
        };

        var totalItems = await query.CountAsync();
        var pageCount = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling(totalItems / (double)pagination.PageSize));
        var page = Math.Clamp(pagination.Page, 1, pageCount);
        var favoriteRows = await query
            .OrderByDescending(favorite => favorite.FavoritedAt)
            .ThenByDescending(favorite => favorite.FileId)
            .Skip((int)Math.Min((long)(page - 1) * pagination.PageSize, int.MaxValue))
            .Take(pagination.PageSize)
            .Select(favorite => new { favorite.FileId, favorite.FavoritedAt })
            .ToListAsync();

        var fileIds = favoriteRows.Select(favorite => favorite.FileId).ToArray();
        var loadedFiles = await db.Files
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(file => fileIds.Contains(file.Id))
            .Include(file => file.Album)
                .ThenInclude(album => album!.Access.Where(access => access.UserId == userId))
            .ToListAsync();
        var filesById = loadedFiles.ToDictionary(file => file.Id);
        var orderedFiles = favoriteRows
            .Where(favorite => filesById.ContainsKey(favorite.FileId))
            .Select(favorite => filesById[favorite.FileId])
            .ToList();

        return new PagedResponse<FileMetadata>(
            orderedFiles,
            totalItems,
            page,
            pagination.PageSize,
            pageCount);
    }



    // Finds the favorite entry for a specific user and file, if one exists.
    public async Task<Favorite?> GetFavoriteAsync(int userId, long fileId)
    {
        return await db.Favorites
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(favorite =>
                favorite.UserId == userId &&
                favorite.FileId == fileId);
    }

    // Adds a favorite entry to the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task AddFavoriteAsync(Favorite favorite)
    {
        db.Favorites.Add(favorite);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Removes a favorite entry from the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task DeleteFavoriteAsync(Favorite favorite)
    {
        db.Favorites.Remove(favorite);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }
}

using WinCapture.Models;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;

namespace WinCapture.Repositories;

public interface IAlbumRepository
{
    // Adds a new album to the database.
    Task AddAsync(Album album);

    // Retrieves an album by ID, including its owner details.
    Task<Album?> GetByIdAsync(long albumId);

    // Returns albums owned by the user or shared with them with view permission.
    Task<IReadOnlyList<Album>> GetAccessibleByUserIdAsync(int userId);

    Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedAccessibleByUserIdAsync(int userId, PaginationQuery pagination);

    // Returns only the albums owned by the specified user.
    Task<IReadOnlyList<Album>> GetOwnedByUserIdAsync(int userId);

    Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedOwnedByUserIdAsync(int userId, PaginationQuery pagination);

    Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedSharedWithUserIdAsync(int userId, PaginationQuery pagination);

    // Returns all albums in the database.
    Task<IReadOnlyList<Album>> GetAllAsync();

    Task<PagedResponse<Album>> GetPagedAllAsync(PaginationQuery pagination);

    // Updates an existing album in the database.
    Task UpdateAsync(Album album);

    // Deletes an album from the database.
    Task DeleteAsync(Album album);

    // Retrieves a user's access entry for an album, if one exists.
    Task<AlbumAccess?> GetAccessAsync(long albumId, int userId);

    // Returns all access entries for an album, including user details.
    Task<IReadOnlyList<AlbumAccess>> GetAccessListAsync(long albumId);

    // Adds an access entry to share an album with a user.
    Task AddAccessAsync(AlbumAccess access);

    // Updates an existing album access entry.
    Task UpdateAccessAsync(AlbumAccess access);

    // Removes an access entry to revoke a user's album access.
    Task DeleteAccessAsync(AlbumAccess access);
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Repositories\PaginationExtensions.cs
============================================================

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;

namespace WinCapture.Repositories;

internal static class PaginationExtensions
{
    public static async Task<PagedResponse<T>> ToPagedResponseAsync<T>(
        this IQueryable<T> query,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var totalItems = await query.CountAsync();
        var pageCount = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling(totalItems / (double)pagination.PageSize));
        var page = Math.Clamp(pagination.Page, 1, pageCount);
        var items = await query
            .Skip((int)Math.Min((long)(page - 1) * pagination.PageSize, int.MaxValue))
            .Take(pagination.PageSize)
            .ToListAsync();

        return new PagedResponse<T>(items, totalItems, page, pagination.PageSize, pageCount);
    }
}

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using WinCapture.Data;
using WinCapture.Models;

namespace WinCapture.Repositories;

public sealed class UserRepository(WinCaptureDbContext db) : IUserRepository
{
    // Finds a user by email address without tracking the entity.
    public async Task<User?> GetByEmailAsync(string email)
    {
        return await db.Users
            .AsNoTracking()
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(user => user.Email == email);
    }

    // Finds a user by their Microsoft Entra object ID without tracking the entity.
    public async Task<User?> GetByEntraObjectIdAsync(string entraObjectId)
    {
        return await db.Users
            .AsNoTracking()
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(user => user.EntraObjectId == entraObjectId);
    }

    // Adds a new user to the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task AddAsync(User user)
    {
        db.Users.Add(user);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    // Updates an existing user's details in the database and saves the changes.
    public async Task UpdateAsync(User user)
    {
        db.Users.Update(user);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Services\AlbumFileService.cs
============================================================

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Files;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;
using WinCapture.Models;
using WinCapture.Repositories;
using WinCapture.Validators;

namespace WinCapture.Services;

public sealed class AlbumFileService(
    FileValidator validator,
    IStorageService storage,
    IFileRepository files,
    IAlbumService albumService,
    ILogger<AlbumFileService> logger) : IAlbumFileService
{
    // Saves the replacement first so the existing file remains available if the database update fails.
    public async Task<FileResponse> ReplaceAsync(
        long albumId,
        long fileId,
        IFormFile file,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var album = await albumService.GetForUploadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        var existing = await files.GetByIdAsync(fileId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found.");

        if (existing.AlbumId != albumId)
        {
            throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found in this album.");
        }

        await validator.ValidateAsync(file);

        var oldName = existing.StoredFileName;
        var newName = await storage.SaveAsync(file);

        existing.StoredFileName = newName;
        existing.OriginalFileName = Path.GetFileName(file.FileName);
        existing.ContentType = file.ContentType;
        existing.FileSize = file.Length;
        existing.UploadedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

        try
        {
            await files.UpdateAsync(existing);
        }
        catch
        {
            await TryDeleteStoredFileAsync(newName);
            throw;
        }

        await TryDeleteStoredFileAsync(oldName);

        var isFavorite = await files.GetFavoriteAsync(currentUser.Id, fileId) is not null;
        var canManage = role == UserRole.Admin || album.CreatedBy == currentUser.Id;

        return ToResponse(existing, albumId, isFavorite, canManage);
    }

    // Saves the uploaded file and removes the stored blob if saving its metadata fails.
    public async Task<FileResponse> UploadAsync(
        long albumId,
        IFormFile file,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var album = await albumService.GetForUploadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        await validator.ValidateAsync(file);

        var storedFileName = await storage.SaveAsync(file);

        var metadata = new FileMetadata
        {
            OriginalFileName = Path.GetFileName(file.FileName),
            StoredFileName = storedFileName,
            ContentType = file.ContentType,
            FileSize = file.Length,
            UploadedBy = currentUser.Id,
            AlbumId = albumId,
            UploadedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
        };

        try
        {
            await files.AddAsync(metadata);
        }
        catch
        {
            await TryDeleteStoredFileAsync(storedFileName);
            throw;
        }

        var canManage = role == UserRole.Admin || album.CreatedBy == currentUser.Id;

        return ToResponse(metadata, albumId, false, canManage);
    }

    // Returns album files with their favorite status and management permissions.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<FileResponse>> GetAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var album = await albumService.GetAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);
        var albumFiles = await files.GetByAlbumIdAsync(albumId);
        var favoriteIds = await files.GetFavoriteFileIdsByUserIdAsync(currentUser.Id);
        var favoriteIdSet = favoriteIds.ToHashSet();

        var canManage = role == UserRole.Admin || album.OwnerId == currentUser.Id;
        var canDownload = await albumService.CanDownloadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        return albumFiles
            .Select(file => ToResponse(
                file,
                albumId,
                favoriteIdSet.Contains(file.Id),
                canManage,
                canDownload))
            .ToList();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<FileResponse>> GetPageAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var album = await albumService.GetAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);
        var page = await files.GetPagedByAlbumIdAsync(albumId, pagination);
        var ids = page.Items.Select(file => file.Id).ToArray();
        var favoriteIds = (await files.GetFavoriteFileIdsByUserIdAsync(currentUser.Id, ids)).ToHashSet();
        var canManage = role == UserRole.Admin || album.OwnerId == currentUser.Id;
        var canDownload = await albumService.CanDownloadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);
        var responses = page.Items
            .Select(file => ToResponse(file, albumId, favoriteIds.Contains(file.Id), canManage, canDownload))
            .ToList();

        return new PagedResponse<FileResponse>(
            responses,
            page.TotalItems,
            page.Page,
            page.PageSize,
            page.PageCount);
    }

    // Checks album access and verifies that the file belongs to the album before returning its details.
    public async Task<FileResponse> GetOneAsync(
        long albumId,
        long fileId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var album = await albumService.GetAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        var file = await files.GetByIdAsync(fileId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found.");

        if (file.AlbumId != albumId)
        {
            throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found in this album.");
        }

        var isFavorite = await files.GetFavoriteAsync(currentUser.Id, fileId) is not null;
        var canManage = role == UserRole.Admin || album.OwnerId == currentUser.Id;
        var canDownload = await albumService.CanDownloadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        return ToResponse(file, albumId, isFavorite, canManage, canDownload);
    }

    // Checks album access and returns the stored file content for preview.
    public async Task<FileDownloadResult> PreviewAsync(
        long albumId,
        long fileId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        await albumService.GetAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        var file = await files.GetByIdAsync(fileId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found.");

        if (file.AlbumId != albumId)
        {
            throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found in this album.");
        }

        var content = await storage.GetAsync(file.StoredFileName);

        return new FileDownloadResult(
            content,
            file.ContentType,
            file.OriginalFileName);
    }

    // Checks download permission before returning the stored file content.
    public async Task<FileDownloadResult> DownloadAsync(
        long albumId,
        long fileId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        if (!await albumService.CanDownloadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role))
        {
            throw new ForbiddenException(
                "You are not allowed to download files from this album.");
        }

        var file = await files.GetByIdAsync(fileId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found.");

        if (file.AlbumId != albumId)
        {
            throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found in this album.");
        }

        var content = await storage.GetAsync(file.StoredFileName);

        return new FileDownloadResult(
            content,
            file.ContentType,
            file.OriginalFileName);
    }

    // Removes metadata first so a database failure cannot leave a record pointing to missing content.
    public async Task<bool> DeleteAsync(
        long albumId,
        long fileId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        await albumService.GetForUploadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        var file = await files.GetByIdAsync(fileId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found.");

        if (file.AlbumId != albumId)
        {
            throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found in this album.");
        }

        await files.DeleteAsync(file);
        return await TryDeleteStoredFileAsync(file.StoredFileName);
    }

    // Detaches file metadata from this album while retaining the file and its blob.
    public async Task RemoveFromAlbumAsync(
        long albumId,
        long fileId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        await albumService.GetForUploadAsync(albumId, currentUser, role);

        var file = await files.GetByIdAsync(fileId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found.");

        if (file.AlbumId != albumId)
        {
            throw new NotFoundException("The requested file was not found in this album.");
        }

        file.AlbumId = null;
        await files.UpdateAsync(file);
    }

    // Logs cleanup failures without hiding the result of the main operation.
    private async Task<bool> TryDeleteStoredFileAsync(string storedFileName)
    {
        try
        {
            await storage.DeleteAsync(storedFileName);
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            logger.LogCritical(
                exception,
                "Database metadata was removed but blob cleanup failed. Reconciliation is required for {StoredFileName}.",
                storedFileName);
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Builds the file response with its URLs, favorite status, and management permission.
    private static FileResponse ToResponse(
        FileMetadata file,
        long albumId,
        bool isFavorite,
        bool canManage,
        bool canDownload = true) =>
        new(
            file.Id,
            file.OriginalFileName,
            file.ContentType,
            file.FileSize,
            file.UploadedAt,
            $"/api/albums/{albumId}/files/{file.Id}",
            $"/api/albums/{albumId}/files/{file.Id}/download",
            isFavorite,
            canManage,
            canDownload);
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\backend\Services\AlbumService.cs
============================================================

using WinCapture.DTOs.Albums;
using WinCapture.DTOs.Common;
using WinCapture.Exceptions;
using WinCapture.Models;
using WinCapture.Repositories;

namespace WinCapture.Services;

public sealed class AlbumService(
    IAlbumRepository albums,
    IUserRepository users,
    IFileRepository files,
    IStorageService storage,
    ILogger<AlbumService> logger) : IAlbumService
{
    // Validates the album name, saves the album, and returns its details.
    public async Task<AlbumResponse> CreateAsync(
        CreateAlbumRequest request,
        User currentUser)
    {
        ValidateAlbumName(request.AlbumName);

        var album = new Album
        {
            AlbumName = request.AlbumName.Trim(),
            CreatedBy = currentUser.Id,
            CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
        };

        await albums.AddAsync(album);

        album.Owner = currentUser;

        return ToResponse(album);
    }

    // Returns the albums owned by the user or shared with them.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AlbumResponse>> GetAccessibleAsync(
        User currentUser)
    {
        var result = await albums.GetAccessibleByUserIdAsync(currentUser.Id);

        return result
            .Select(ToResponse)
            .ToList();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> GetAccessiblePageAsync(
        User currentUser,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var page = await albums.GetPagedAccessibleByUserIdAsync(currentUser.Id, pagination);
        return MapPage(page);
    }

    // Returns only the albums owned by the specified user.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AlbumResponse>> GetOwnedByUserAsync(int userId)
    {
        var result = await albums.GetOwnedByUserIdAsync(userId);

        return result
            .Select(ToResponse)
            .ToList();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> GetOwnedByUserPageAsync(
        int userId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var page = await albums.GetPagedOwnedByUserIdAsync(userId, pagination);
        return MapPage(page);
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> GetSharedWithUserPageAsync(
        int userId,
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var page = await albums.GetPagedSharedWithUserIdAsync(userId, pagination);
        return MapPage(page);
    }

    // Returns all albums for admin management.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AlbumResponse>> GetAllForAdminAsync()
    {
        var result = await albums.GetAllAsync();

        return result
            .Select(ToResponse)
            .ToList();
    }

    public async Task<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> GetAllForAdminPageAsync(
        PaginationQuery pagination)
    {
        var page = await albums.GetPagedAllAsync(pagination);
        return MapPage(page);
    }

    private static PagedResponse<AlbumResponse> MapPage(
        PagedResponse<Album> page)
    {
        return new PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>(
            page.Items.Select(ToResponse).ToList(),
            page.TotalItems,
            page.Page,
            page.PageSize,
            page.PageCount);
    }

    // Retrieves an album after checking whether the user can view it.
    public async Task<AlbumResponse> GetAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var album = await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            true);

        return ToResponse(album);
    }

    // Validates the album name and updates the album details.
    public async Task<AlbumResponse> UpdateAsync(
        long albumId,
        UpdateAlbumRequest request,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        ValidateAlbumName(request.AlbumName);

        var album = await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            false);

        album.AlbumName = request.AlbumName.Trim();
        album.UpdatedBy = currentUser.Id;
        album.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

        await albums.UpdateAsync(album);

        return ToResponse(album);
    }

    // Deletes album metadata first, then cleans its private blobs and logs any orphan requiring reconciliation.
    public async Task DeleteAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var album = await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            false);

        var albumFiles = await files.GetByAlbumIdAsync(albumId);

        await albums.DeleteAsync(album);

        foreach (var file in albumFiles)
        {
            try
            {
                await storage.DeleteAsync(file.StoredFileName);
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                logger.LogCritical(
                    exception,
                    "Album metadata was removed but blob cleanup failed. Reconciliation is required for {StoredFileName}.",
                    file.StoredFileName);
            }
        }
    }

    // Returns the members who have access to the album and their permissions.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AlbumMemberResponse>> GetMembersAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            false);

        var access = await albums.GetAccessListAsync(albumId);

        return access
            .Select(item => new AlbumMemberResponse(
                item.UserId,
                item.User.Name,
                item.User.Email,
                item.CanView,
                item.CanDownload,
                item.GrantedAt))
            .ToList();
    }

    // Grants an existing WinCapture user access to the album with the requested permissions.
    public async Task AddMemberAsync(
        long albumId,
        AddAlbumMemberRequest request,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            false);

        var email = request.Email.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();

        if (!request.CanView && request.CanDownload)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException(
                "CanDownload cannot be true when CanView is false.");
        }

        var user = await users.GetByEmailAsync(email)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException(
                "The user must register in WinCapture before they can be added to an album.");

        if (user.Id == currentUser.Id)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException(
                "The album owner already has full access.");
        }

        var existing = await albums.GetAccessAsync(albumId, user.Id);

        if (existing is not null)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException(
                "This user already has access to the album.");
        }

        await albums.AddAccessAsync(new AlbumAccess
        {
            AlbumId = albumId,
            UserId = user.Id,
            CanView = request.CanView,
            CanDownload = request.CanView && request.CanDownload,
            GrantedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            GrantedBy = currentUser.Id
        });
    }

    // Updates a member's viewing and downloading permissions for the album.
    public async Task UpdateMemberAsync(
        long albumId,
        int userId,
        UpdateAlbumMemberRequest request,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            false);

        if (!request.CanView && request.CanDownload)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException(
                "CanDownload cannot be true when CanView is false.");
        }

        var access = await albums.GetAccessAsync(albumId, userId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("Album access was not found.");

        access.CanView = request.CanView;
        access.CanDownload = request.CanView && request.CanDownload;

        await albums.UpdateAccessAsync(access);
    }

    // Removes a member's access to the specified album.
    public async Task RemoveMemberAsync(
        long albumId,
        int userId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            false);

        var access = await albums.GetAccessAsync(albumId, userId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException("Album access was not found.");

        await albums.DeleteAccessAsync(access);
    }

    // Checks album access before allowing a file to be uploaded.
    public async Task<Album> GetForUploadAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        return await GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser,
            role,
            false);
    }

    // Checks whether the user is allowed to download files from the album.
    public async Task<bool> CanDownloadAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role)
    {
        var album = await albums.GetByIdAsync(albumId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException(
                "The requested album was not found.");

        if (role == UserRole.Admin || album.CreatedBy == currentUser.Id)
        {
            return true;
        }

        var access = await albums.GetAccessAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser.Id);

        return access is not null && access.CanView && access.CanDownload;
    }

    // Checks whether the user can view or manage the album based on ownership, role, and access permissions.
    private async Task<Album> GetAuthorizedAlbumAsync(
        long albumId,
        User currentUser,
        UserRole role,
        bool canView)
    {
        var album = await albums.GetByIdAsync(albumId)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException(
                "The requested album was not found.");

        if (role == UserRole.Admin || album.CreatedBy == currentUser.Id)
        {
            return album;
        }

        var access = await albums.GetAccessAsync(
            albumId,
            currentUser.Id);

        if (access is null || (canView && !access.CanView))
        {
            throw new ForbiddenException(
                "You are not allowed to access this album.");
        }

        if (!canView)
        {
            throw new ForbiddenException(
                "Only the album owner or an Admin can manage this album.");
        }

        return album;
    }

    // Rejects empty album names and names longer than 200 characters.
    private static void ValidateAlbumName(string name)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
        {
            throw new BadRequestException("Album name is required.");
        }

        if (name.Trim().Length > 200)
        {
            throw new BadRequestException(
                "Album name cannot exceed 200 characters.");
        }
    }

    // Maps an album entity to the response returned by the API.
    private static AlbumResponse ToResponse(Album album)
    {
        return new AlbumResponse(
            album.Id,
            album.AlbumName,
            album.CreatedBy,
            album.Owner.Name,
            album.CreatedAt,
            album.UpdatedAt);
    }
}
