export function Layout() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const angularAlbumsUrl = getAngularAlbumsUrl();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [profileMenuOpen, setProfileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const profileMenuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const profileTriggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);

  const closeMobileNavigation = () => {
    setMobileOpen(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!profileMenuOpen) return;

    const handlePointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (event.target instanceof Node && !profileMenuRef.current?.contains(event.target)) {
        setProfileMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };
    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setProfileMenuOpen(false);
        profileTriggerRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [profileMenuOpen]);

  return (
    <div className={appShellClass}>
      <div className="relative z-[1] flex min-h-screen w-full flex-col">
        {/* Full-width top header */}
        <header className="glass-chrome sticky top-0 z-40 w-full border-b border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.55)]">
          <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[82px] w-full items-center gap-5 px-8 max-[1200px]:gap-3 max-[960px]:min-h-[72px] max-[760px]:gap-3 max-[760px]:px-4">
            {/* Project logo only: no WinWire branding or WinCapture text */}
            <NavLink
              to="/dashboard"
              aria-label="Go to dashboard"
              className="flex h-[58px] w-[165px] flex-none items-center justify-start rounded-lg transition-opacity hover:opacity-85 max-[1200px]:w-[135px] max-[760px]:h-[48px] max-[760px]:w-[80px]"
              onClick={closeMobileNavigation}
            >
              <img
                src="/wincapture-logo.png"
                alt="Project logo"
                className="block max-h-[52px] max-w-full object-contain object-left max-[760px]:max-h-[44px]"
              />
            </NavLink>

            {/* Desktop navigation */}
            <nav
              aria-label="Primary navigation"
              className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1 max-[1000px]:hidden"
            >
              {navigation.map((item) => {
                const Icon = item.icon;

                if (item.external) {
                  return (
                    <a key={item.to} href={angularAlbumsUrl} className={navLinkClass(false)}>
                      <Icon aria-hidden="true" size={21} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                      <span>{item.label}</span>
                    </a>
                  );
                }

                return (
                  <NavLink
                    key={item.to}
                    to={item.to}
                    className={({ isActive }) => navLinkClass(isActive)}
                  >
                    <Icon aria-hidden="true" size={21} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                    <span>{item.label}</span>
                  </NavLink>
                );
              })}
            </nav>

            {/* Mobile navigation toggle */}
            <button
              type="button"
              className="hidden h-11 w-11 flex-none cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.65)] bg-white/45 text-[#3b5973] backdrop-blur-md transition-colors hover:bg-white/70 hover:text-[#126dd7] max-[1000px]:inline-flex"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen((current) => !current)}
              aria-label={mobileOpen ? "Close navigation" : "Open navigation"}
              aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
              aria-controls="mobile-primary-navigation"
            >
              {mobileOpen ? (
                <X aria-hidden="true" size={22} />
              ) : (
                <Menu aria-hidden="true" size={22} />
              )}
            </button>

            {/* Account controls */}
            <div
              ref={profileMenuRef}
              className="relative ml-auto flex flex-none items-center border-l border-[rgba(189,211,229,0.45)] pl-5 max-[1200px]:pl-3 max-[760px]:border-l-0 max-[760px]:pl-0"
            >
              <button
                ref={profileTriggerRef}
                type="button"
                className="inline-flex min-w-0 cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-transparent p-1 text-left transition-colors hover:border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.55)] hover:bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.45)] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--focus-ring)] max-[1200px]:gap-2"
                onClick={() => setProfileMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
                aria-label={`Profile menu for ${user?.name ?? "WinCapture User"}`}
                aria-haspopup="menu"
                aria-expanded={profileMenuOpen}
              >
                <span
                  className="grid h-10 w-10 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full bg-[linear-gradient(135deg,#54a6ff,#1b6ecf)] text-[12px] font-bold text-white shadow-[0_4px_12px_rgba(27,110,207,0.22),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)]"

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";

interface AppProvidersProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  msalInstance: IPublicClientApplication;
}

export function AppProviders({ children, msalInstance }: AppProvidersProps) {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <MsalProvider instance={msalInstance}>
          <AuthProvider>{children}</AuthProvider>
        </MsalProvider>

import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect } from "react";
import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import { getAngularAlbumsUrl } from "@/config/auth";
import { Layout } from "@/app/components/Layout";
import { RouteErrorBoundary } from "./RouteErrorBoundary";
import { NotFoundPage } from "./NotFoundPage";

const Dashboard = lazy(() =>
  import("@/features/dashboard").then((feature) => ({ default: feature.Dashboard })),
);
const AlbumDetailPage = lazy(() =>
  import("@/features/albums").then((feature) => ({ default: feature.AlbumDetailPage })),
);
const FilesPage = lazy(() =>
  import("@/features/files").then((feature) => ({ default: feature.FilesPage })),
);

function AngularAlbumsRedirect() {
  useEffect(() => {
    window.location.replace(getAngularAlbumsUrl());
  }, []);
  return null;
}

export function AppRoutes() {
  return (
    <RouteErrorBoundary>
      <Suspense
        fallback={
          <main className="p-6" role="status">
            Loading pageâ€¦
          </main>
        }
      >
        <Routes>
          <Route element={<Layout />}>
            <Route path="/" element={<Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />} />
            <Route path="/dashboard" element={<Dashboard />} />
            <Route path="/admin/*" element={<Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />} />
            <Route path="/albums" element={<AngularAlbumsRedirect />} />
            <Route path="/albums/:albumId" element={<AlbumDetailPage />} />
            <Route path="/files" element={<FilesPage />} />
            <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
          </Route>
          <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
        </Routes>
      </Suspense>
    </RouteErrorBoundary>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\app\routes\RouteErrorBoundary.tsx
============================================================

import { Component, type ReactNode } from "react";

interface RouteErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

interface RouteErrorBoundaryState {
  hasError: boolean;
}

export class RouteErrorBoundary extends Component<
  RouteErrorBoundaryProps,
  RouteErrorBoundaryState
> {
  override state: RouteErrorBoundaryState = { hasError: false };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(): RouteErrorBoundaryState {
    return { hasError: true };
  }

  private handleRetry = (): void => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false });
  };

  override render(): ReactNode {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <main
          className="mx-auto grid min-h-[50vh] max-w-xl place-content-center gap-4 p-6 text-center"
          role="alert"
        >
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">This page could not be displayed</h1>
          <p>Try again. If the problem continues, return to the dashboard.</p>
          <button
            className="primary-action justify-self-center"
            onClick={this.handleRetry}
            type="button"
          >
            Try again
          </button>
        </main>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

import type { Configuration, RedirectRequest } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { environment, validateEnvironment } from "@/config/env";

export const apiBaseUrl = environment.apiBaseUrl;
export const msalClientId = environment.msalClientId;
export const msalTenantId = environment.msalTenantId;
export const msalRedirectUri = environment.msalRedirectUri;
export const msalPostLogoutRedirectUri = environment.msalPostLogoutRedirectUri;
export const apiScope = environment.apiScope;
export { validateEnvironment };

export function getAngularAlbumsUrl(): string {
  return new URL(environment.angularAlbumsBaseUrl, window.location.origin).toString();
}

export const msalConfig: Configuration = {
  auth: {
    clientId: msalClientId,
    authority: `https://login.microsoftonline.com/${msalTenantId}`,
    redirectUri: msalRedirectUri,
    postLogoutRedirectUri: msalPostLogoutRedirectUri,
  },
  cache: {
    cacheLocation: "sessionStorage",
  },
};

export const loginRequest: RedirectRequest = {
  scopes: [apiScope],
  redirectStartPage: "/",
};

type Environment = {
  apiBaseUrl: string;
  msalClientId: string;
  msalTenantId: string;
  msalRedirectUri: string;
  msalPostLogoutRedirectUri: string;
  apiScope: string;
  angularAlbumsBaseUrl: string;
};

const rawEnvironment = import.meta.env;

export const environment: Environment = {
  apiBaseUrl: rawEnvironment.VITE_API_BASE_URL?.trim() ?? "",
  msalClientId: rawEnvironment.VITE_MSAL_CLIENT_ID?.trim() ?? "",
  msalTenantId: rawEnvironment.VITE_MSAL_TENANT_ID?.trim() ?? "",
  msalRedirectUri: rawEnvironment.VITE_MSAL_REDIRECT_URI?.trim() || "https://localhost:5173/",
  msalPostLogoutRedirectUri:
    rawEnvironment.VITE_MSAL_POST_LOGOUT_REDIRECT_URI?.trim() || "https://localhost:5173/",
  apiScope: rawEnvironment.VITE_MSAL_API_SCOPE?.trim() ?? "",
  angularAlbumsBaseUrl:
    rawEnvironment.VITE_ANGULAR_ALBUMS_URL?.trim() || "https://localhost:5173/angular/albums",
};

const REQUIRED_ENVIRONMENT_KEYS = [
  ["VITE_API_BASE_URL", environment.apiBaseUrl],
  ["VITE_MSAL_CLIENT_ID", environment.msalClientId],
  ["VITE_MSAL_TENANT_ID", environment.msalTenantId],
  ["VITE_MSAL_API_SCOPE", environment.apiScope],
] as const;

export function validateEnvironment(): string[] {
  return REQUIRED_ENVIRONMENT_KEYS.filter(([, value]) => !value).map(([key]) => key);
}

import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { callApi } from "@/shared/api/request";
import { buildPagePath } from "@/shared/api/pagination";
import { downloadResponse, uploadMultipartFile } from "@/shared/api/transfers";
import { getFilePreviewUrlByPath } from "@/shared/api/previews";
import { ApiError, type PagedResponse } from "@/shared/types/api";
import type {
  AlbumMemberResponse,
  AlbumResponse,
  CreateAlbumRequest,
  UpdateAlbumRequest,
  AddAlbumMemberRequest,
  UpdateAlbumMemberRequest,
} from "@/features/albums/types/album";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import { parseAlbumMembers } from "@/features/albums/schemas/albumMember";
import {
  AddAlbumMemberRequestSchema,
  parseAlbumResponse,
  parsePagedAlbums,
  UpdateAlbumMemberRequestSchema,
  validateAlbumNameRequest,
} from "@/features/albums/schemas/album";
import {
  parseFileResponse,
  parsePagedResponse,
  parseStorageCleanupResponse,
} from "@/features/files/schemas/fileResponse";
// Albums API
export async function getAlbums(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number,
  search?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/albums", page, pageSize, search),
    { signal },
  );

  return parsePagedAlbums(await response.json());
}

export async function getMyAlbums(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page = 1,
  pageSize = 10,
  search?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/albums/mine", page, pageSize, search),
    { signal },
  );

  return parsePagedAlbums(await response.json());
}

export async function getSharedAlbums(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,

  page = 1,
  pageSize = 10,
  search?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/albums/shared", page, pageSize, search),
    { signal },
  );

  return parsePagedAlbums(await response.json());
}

export async function getAdminAlbums(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number,
  search?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/admin/albums", page, pageSize, search),
    { signal },
  );

  return parsePagedAlbums(await response.json());
}

export async function getMyAdminAlbums(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page = 1,
  pageSize = 10,
  search?: string,
): Promise<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/admin/albums/mine", page, pageSize, search),
  );

  return parsePagedAlbums(await response.json());
}

export async function getAlbum(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
): Promise<AlbumResponse> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}`);

  return parseAlbumResponse(await response.json());
}

export async function createAlbum(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  request: CreateAlbumRequest,
): Promise<AlbumResponse> {
  const validatedRequest = validateAlbumNameRequest(request);
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, "/api/albums", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify(validatedRequest),
  });

  return parseAlbumResponse(await response.json());
}

export async function updateAlbum(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  request: UpdateAlbumRequest,
): Promise<AlbumResponse> {
  const validatedRequest = validateAlbumNameRequest(request);
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}`, {
    method: "PUT",
    body: JSON.stringify(validatedRequest),
  });

  return parseAlbumResponse(await response.json());
}

export async function deleteAlbum(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
): Promise<void> {
  await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
}

export async function getAlbumMembers(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
): Promise<AlbumMemberResponse[]> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}/members`);
  const correlationId =
    response.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? response.headers.get("request-id") ?? undefined;
  return parseAlbumMembers(await response.json(), correlationId);
}

export async function addAlbumMember(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  request: AddAlbumMemberRequest,
): Promise<void> {
  const validation = AddAlbumMemberRequestSchema.safeParse(request);
  if (!validation.success) {
    throw new ApiError("Provide a valid email address and album permissions.", 400);
  }
  await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}/members`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify(validation.data),
  });
}

export async function updateAlbumMember(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  userId: number,
  request: UpdateAlbumMemberRequest,
): Promise<void> {
  const validation = UpdateAlbumMemberRequestSchema.safeParse(request);
  if (!validation.success) {
    throw new ApiError("Album permissions are invalid.", 400);
  }
  await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}/members/${userId}`, {
    method: "PUT",
    body: JSON.stringify(validation.data),
  });
}

export async function removeAlbumMember(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  userId: number,
): Promise<void> {
  await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}/members/${userId}`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
}
// Album Files API
export async function getAlbumFiles(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number,
  category?: string,
): Promise<PagedResponse<FileResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath(`/api/albums/${albumId}/files`, page, pageSize, undefined, category),
    { cache: "no-store" },
  );

  return parsePagedResponse(await response.json(), parseFileResponse);
}

export async function getAlbumFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  fileId: number,
): Promise<FileResponse> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}`);

  return parseFileResponse(await response.json());
}

export async function uploadAlbumFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  file: File,
): Promise<FileResponse> {
  const value: unknown = await uploadMultipartFile(
    instance,
    account,
    `/api/albums/${albumId}/files`,
    "POST",
    file,
  );
  return parseFileResponse(value);
}

export async function replaceAlbumFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  fileId: number,
  file: File,
): Promise<FileResponse> {
  const value: unknown = await uploadMultipartFile(
    instance,
    account,
    `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}`,
    "PUT",
    file,
  );
  return parseFileResponse(value);
}

export async function downloadAlbumFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  fileId: number,
  fallbackFileName: string,
): Promise<void> {
  await downloadResponse(
    instance,
    account,
    `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}/download`,
    fallbackFileName,
  );
}

export async function getFilePreviewUrl(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  fileId: number,
  albumId?: number,
  canManage = false,
): Promise<string> {
  // Managers can use the download endpoint; shared-album viewers use preview.
  const path = albumId
    ? canManage
      ? `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}/download`
      : `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}/preview`
    : `/api/files/${fileId}/download`;

  return getFilePreviewUrlByPath(instance, account, path);
}

export async function deleteAlbumFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  fileId: number,
): Promise<{ storageCleanupComplete: boolean }> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
  if (response.status === 204) return { storageCleanupComplete: true };
  return parseStorageCleanupResponse(await response.json());
}

export async function removeAlbumFileAssociation(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  albumId: number,
  fileId: number,
): Promise<void> {
  await callApi(instance, account, `/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${fileId}/association`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
}

import { Upload } from "lucide-react";
import { BulkSelectionToolbar, FileCardGrid } from "@/features/files";
import { FILE_CATEGORY_FILTERS } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import type { FileCategoryFilter } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import { Pagination } from "@/shared/components/ui/Pagination";

interface AlbumFilesSectionProps {
  files: FileResponse[];
  previewUrls: Record<number, string>;
  favoritePendingIds: Set<number>;
  loadPreview: (file: FileResponse) => Promise<string>;
  canManageAlbum: boolean;
  categoryFilter: FileCategoryFilter;
  currentPage: number;
  pageCount: number;
  totalFiles: number;
  selectionMode: boolean;
  selectedIds: Set<number>;
  bulkPending: boolean;
  onCategoryChange: (category: FileCategoryFilter) => void;
  onStartSelection: () => void;
  onCancelSelection: () => void;
  onSelectAll: (ids: number[]) => void;
  onBulkAction: (action: "remove" | "delete") => void;
  onFavoriteToggle: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onReplace: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onDetails: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onPreview: (file: FileResponse, source?: string) => void;
  onDownload: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onDelete: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onSelectionToggle: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onUploadFirstFile: () => void;
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
}

export function AlbumFilesSection({
  files,
  previewUrls,
  favoritePendingIds,
  loadPreview,
  canManageAlbum,
  categoryFilter,
  currentPage,
  pageCount,
  totalFiles,
  selectionMode,
  selectedIds,
  bulkPending,
  onCategoryChange,
  onStartSelection,
  onCancelSelection,
  onSelectAll,
  onBulkAction,
  onFavoriteToggle,
  onReplace,
  onDetails,
  onPreview,
  onDownload,
  onDelete,
  onSelectionToggle,
  onUploadFirstFile,
  onPageChange,
}: AlbumFilesSectionProps) {
  return (
    <section className="min-w-0">
      <div className="mb-[15px] flex items-end justify-between gap-[18px]">
        <div>
          <p className="dashboard-panel-kicker">Collection content</p>

          <h2 className="mt-1 mb-0 text-[16px] leading-[1.25] font-[650] tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#163855]">
            Files
          </h2>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2"
        role="group"
        aria-label="Filter album files by category"
      >
        {FILE_CATEGORY_FILTERS.map((category) => (
          <button
            key={category}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={categoryFilter === category}
            onClick={() => onCategoryChange(category)}
            className={`secondary-action min-h-[32px] px-3 py-1 text-[9px] ${
              categoryFilter === category
                ? "!border-white/80 !bg-white/70 !text-[#145a99] shadow-[0_3px_12px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)]"
                : ""
            }`}
          >
            {category}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {canManageAlbum && !selectionMode && files.length > 0 && (
        <button type="button" onClick={onStartSelection} className="secondary-action mb-4">
          Select files
        </button>
      )}
      {canManageAlbum && selectionMode && (
        <BulkSelectionToolbar
          selectedCount={selectedIds.size}
          visibleCount={files.length}
          disabled={bulkPending}
          onSelectAll={() =>
            onSelectAll(files.filter((file) => file.canManage).map((file) => file.id))
          }
          onCancel={onCancelSelection}
          actions={
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={!selectedIds.size || bulkPending}
                onClick={() => onBulkAction("remove")}
                className="primary-action disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Remove from album
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={!selectedIds.size || bulkPending}
                onClick={() => onBulkAction("delete")}
                className="danger-gradient-action inline-flex min-h-[36px] items-center rounded-[10px] px-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Delete permanently
              </button>
            </>
          }
        />
      )}

      <FileCardGrid
        files={files}
        previewUrls={previewUrls}
        favoritePendingIds={favoritePendingIds}
        loadPreview={loadPreview}
        canManage={canManageAlbum}
        onFavoriteToggle={onFavoriteToggle}
        onReplace={onReplace}
        onDetails={onDetails}
        onPreview={onPreview}
        onDownload={onDownload}
        onDelete={onDelete}
        selectionMode={selectionMode && canManageAlbum}
        selectionDisabled={bulkPending}
        selectedIds={selectedIds}
        selectionEligible={(file) => file.canManage}
        onSelectionToggle={onSelectionToggle}
        empty={
          <section className="surface flex min-h-[300px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-[18px] px-[22px] py-[38px] text-center">
            <span className="grid h-[62px] w-[62px] place-items-center rounded-[19px] bg-[#e7f3ff] text-[#287dd7] shadow-[inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.72),0_7px_18px_rgba(45,111,176,0.06)]">
              <Upload aria-hidden="true" size={25} strokeWidth={1.5} />
            </span>

            <h3 className="mt-4 mb-0 text-[15px] font-[650] text-[#29445d]">
              {totalFiles === 0
                ? "No files in this album"
                : `No ${categoryFilter.toLocaleLowerCase()} found`}
            </h3>

            <p className="mt-[7px] mb-[18px] max-w-[390px] text-[10px] leading-[1.6] text-[#8497a7]">
              {totalFiles === 0
                ? "Add images, documents, videos, audio files, or ZIP archives to start building this collection."
                : `There are no ${categoryFilter.toLocaleLowerCase()} in this album. Try another category or select All.`}
            </p>

            {canManageAlbum && totalFiles === 0 && (
              <button type="button" onClick={onUploadFirstFile} className="primary-action">
                <Upload aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                Add your first file
              </button>
            )}
          </section>
        }
      />

      {totalFiles > 0 && (
        <Pagination currentPage={currentPage} pageCount={pageCount} onPageChange={onPageChange} />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

              {members.length}

          {members.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex min-h-[205px] flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">

              {members.map((member) => (
                <div
                  key={member.userId}
                  className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-[9px] border-b border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.35)] px-[11px] py-[10px] transition-colors duration-[170ms] hover:bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.45)] last:border-b-0 max-[760px]:flex-wrap"
                >
                  <div className="grid h-[31px] w-[31px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.55)] text-[9px] font-bold text-[#1b6ecf] shadow-sm">
                    {member.name?.charAt(0).toUpperCase() ?? member.email.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 gap-0.5">
                    <strong className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[14px] font-[650] text-[#3b556b]">
                      {member.name || member.email}
                    </strong>

                    <span className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[12px] text-[#71879a]">
                      {member.email}
                    </span>

                    <small className="text-[10px] text-[#71879a]">
                      Granted {formatDate(member.grantedAt)}
                    </small>

                    <span
                      className={`mt-1 inline-flex w-fit items-center rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[9px] font-semibold ${
                        member.canDownload
                          ? "bg-[#e8f2ff] text-[#245f9e]"
                          : member.canView
                            ? "bg-[#e8f7ef] text-[#216747]"
                            : "bg-[#f0f3f6] text-[#5f7080]"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {member.canDownload
                        ? "View and download"
                        : member.canView
                          ? "View only"
                          : "No access"}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-1 max-[760px]:w-full max-[760px]:justify-start max-[760px]:pl-10 max-[520px]:pl-10">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label={`${member.canView ? "View allowed" : "View off"} for ${member.email}`}
                      aria-pressed={member.canView}
                      onClick={() =>
                        void onUpdateMember(
                          member.userId,
                          !member.canView,
                          member.canView && member.canDownload ? false : member.canDownload,
                        )
                      }
                      className={`${memberPermissionClass} ${
                        member.canView
                          ? "primary-action"
                          : "border-[#d4dde5] bg-[#f2f5f8] text-[#647789] hover:bg-[#eaf0f4]"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {member.canView && <Check aria-hidden="true" size={12} strokeWidth={2} />}
                      {member.canView ? "View allowed" : "View off"}
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label={`${member.canDownload ? "Download allowed" : "Download off"} for ${member.email}`}
                      aria-pressed={member.canDownload}
                      disabled={!member.canView}
                      onClick={() =>
                        void onUpdateMember(member.userId, member.canView, !member.canDownload)
                      }
                      className={`${memberPermissionClass} ${
                        member.canDownload
                          ? "primary-action"
                          : "border-[#d4dde5] bg-[#f2f5f8] text-[#647789] hover:bg-[#eaf0f4]"
                      } ${!member.canView ? "cursor-not-allowed opacity-[0.47]" : ""}`}
                    >
                      {member.canDownload && (
                        <Download aria-hidden="true" size={12} strokeWidth={1.9} />
                      )}
                      {member.canDownload ? "Download allowed" : "Download off"}
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label={`Revoke access for ${member.email}`}
                      title="Revoke access"
                      onClick={() => void onRemoveMember(member.userId)}
                      className="danger-gradient-action inline-flex min-h-[36px] cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-[8px] px-[11px] !text-[11px] !font-semibold transition-[transform,box-shadow] duration-[160ms] max-[520px]:w-7 max-[520px]:px-0"
                    >
                      <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" size={15} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                      <span className="max-[520px]:hidden">Revoke</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </details>
  );
}

============================================================

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { createAlbum, deleteAlbum, getAlbum, getAdminAlbums, getAlbums, getMyAlbums, getSharedAlbums, updateAlbum } from "@/features/albums/api/albumsApi";
import type { CreateAlbumRequest, UpdateAlbumRequest } from "@/features/albums/types/album";

export const albumKeys = {
  all: ["albums"] as const,
  lists: () => [...albumKeys.all, "list"] as const,
  list: (accountId: string | null, filter: string, page: number, pageSize: number, search: string, adminAll: boolean) =>
    [...albumKeys.lists(), accountId, filter, page, pageSize, search, adminAll] as const,
  details: () => [...albumKeys.all, "detail"] as const,
  detail: (id: number) => [...albumKeys.details(), id] as const,
};

interface AlbumQueryContext {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  accountKey: string | null;
}

export function useAlbumListQuery(context: AlbumQueryContext & { page: number; pageSize: number; filter: "all" | "mine" | "shared"; search?: string; adminAll?: boolean }) {
  const { instance, account, accountKey, page, pageSize, filter, search, adminAll = false } = context;
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: albumKeys.list(accountKey, filter, page, pageSize, search ?? "", adminAll),
    enabled: !!account,
    queryFn: async ({ signal }) => {
      if (!account) throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      if (adminAll) return getAdminAlbums(instance, account, page, pageSize, search, signal);
      if (filter === "mine") return getMyAlbums(instance, account, page, pageSize, search, signal);
      if (filter === "shared") return getSharedAlbums(instance, account, page, pageSize, search, signal);
      return getAlbums(instance, account, page, pageSize, search, signal);
    },
  });
}

export function useAlbumDetailQuery(context: AlbumQueryContext & { albumId: number }) {
  const { instance, account, albumId } = context;
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: albumKeys.detail(albumId),
    enabled: !!account && albumId > 0,
    queryFn: () => account ? getAlbum(instance, account, albumId) : Promise.reject(new Error("An authenticated account is required.")),
  });
}

export function useCreateAlbumMutation(context: AlbumQueryContext) {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (request: CreateAlbumRequest) => context.account ? createAlbum(context.instance, context.account, request) : Promise.reject(new Error("An authenticated account is required.")),
    onSuccess: (album) => { client.setQueryData(albumKeys.detail(album.id), album); return client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: albumKeys.lists() }); },
  });
}

export function useUpdateAlbumMutation(context: AlbumQueryContext & { albumId: number }) {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (request: UpdateAlbumRequest) => context.account ? updateAlbum(context.instance, context.account, context.albumId, request) : Promise.reject(new Error("An authenticated account is required.")),
    onSuccess: (album) => { client.setQueryData(albumKeys.detail(album.id), album); return client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: albumKeys.lists() }); },
  });
}

export function useDeleteAlbumMutation(context: AlbumQueryContext) {
  const client = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (albumId: number) => context.account ? deleteAlbum(context.instance, context.account, albumId) : Promise.reject(new Error("An authenticated account is required.")),
    onSuccess: (_result, albumId) => { client.removeQueries({ queryKey: albumKeys.detail(albumId) }); return client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: albumKeys.lists() }); },
  });
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\albums\hooks\useAlbumCoverPreviews.ts
============================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";

import { getAlbumFiles, getFilePreviewUrl } from "../api/albumsApi";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "@/features/albums/types/album";
import { getAccountKey } from "@/shared/utils/accountKey";

export function useAlbumCoverPreviews(
  albums: AlbumResponse[],
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo | null,
  userId?: number,
  isAdmin = false,
): Record<number, string> {
  const [coverUrls, setCoverUrls] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const accountKey = getAccountKey(account);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    let cleanupRequested = false;
    const createdUrls: string[] = [];

    const registerUrl = (url: string) => {
      if (cleanupRequested) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      } else {
        createdUrls.push(url);
      }
    };

    setCoverUrls({});

    if (!account || albums.length === 0) {
      return () => {
        cleanupRequested = true;
      };
    }

    const load = async () => {
      const entries = await Promise.all(
        albums.map(async (album) => {
          try {
            const page = await getAlbumFiles(instance, account, album.id, 1, 100, "Images");
            const image = page.items[0];
            if (!image) return null;

            const canManage = isAdmin || album.ownerId === userId;
            const url = await getFilePreviewUrl(instance, account, image.id, album.id, canManage);
            registerUrl(url);
            return [album.id, url] as const;
          } catch {
            return null;
          }
        }),
      );

      if (!active) return;
      setCoverUrls(
        Object.fromEntries(
          entries.filter((entry): entry is readonly [number, string] => entry !== null),
        ),
      );
    };

    void load();

    return () => {
      active = false;
      cleanupRequested = true;
      createdUrls.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
    };
    // Account object refreshes with an unchanged homeAccountId do not require refetching.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [accountKey, albums, instance, isAdmin, userId]);

  return coverUrls;
}


============================================================
FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\albums\hooks\useAlbumFileActions.ts
============================================================

import { useState, type ChangeEvent } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import { useFileFavorites } from "@/features/files";
import {
  deleteAlbumFile,
  downloadAlbumFile,
  getAlbumFile,
  removeAlbumFileAssociation,
  replaceAlbumFile,
} from "@/features/albums/api/albumsApi";
import { validateUploadFile } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";

export type AlbumBulkAction = "remove" | "delete" | null;

interface ReplaceTargetRef {
  current: number | null;
}

interface UseAlbumFileActionsOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  albumId: number;
  selectedFiles: FileResponse[];
  bulkAction: AlbumBulkAction;
  replaceTargetIdRef: ReplaceTargetRef;
  reloadAlbum: () => Promise<void>;
  removeSelected: (ids: readonly number[]) => void;
  cancelSelection: () => void;
  setFiles: (update: (files: FileResponse[]) => FileResponse[]) => void;
  setFileDetails: (file: FileResponse | null) => void;
  setError: (message: string | null) => void;
  setSuccessMessage: (message: string | null) => void;
  clearBulkAction: () => void;
}

export function useAlbumFileActions({
  instance,
  account,
  albumId,
  selectedFiles,
  bulkAction,
  replaceTargetIdRef,
  reloadAlbum,
  removeSelected,
  cancelSelection,
  setFiles,
  setFileDetails,
  setError,
  setSuccessMessage,
  clearBulkAction,
}: UseAlbumFileActionsOptions) {
  const [bulkPending, setBulkPending] = useState(false);

  const { pendingIds: favoritePendingIds, toggleFavorite } = useFileFavorites({
    instance,
    account,
    onSuccess: (file, nextFavorite) => {
      setFiles((previous) =>
        previous.map((currentFile) =>
          currentFile.id === file.id ? { ...currentFile, isFavorite: nextFavorite } : currentFile,
        ),
      );
    },
    onError: setError,
  });

  const handleDownload = async (file: FileResponse): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account || !Number.isFinite(albumId)) return;

    try {
      setError(null);
      setSuccessMessage(null);
      await downloadAlbumFile(instance, account, albumId, file.id, file.originalFileName);
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Download failed.");
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (fileId: number): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account || !Number.isFinite(albumId)) return;

    try {
      setError(null);
      const result = await deleteAlbumFile(instance, account, albumId, fileId);
      removeSelected([fileId]);

      if (result?.storageCleanupComplete === false) {
        setSuccessMessage(
          "The file record was deleted, but its stored content needs cleanup reconciliation.",
        );
      }

      await reloadAlbum();
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? `${caughtError.message} The file remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.`
          : "Delete failed. The file remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.",
      );
    }
  };

  const runBulkAction = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (
      !account ||
      !Number.isFinite(albumId) ||
      !bulkAction ||
      bulkPending ||
      !selectedFiles.length
    ) {
      return;
    }

    setBulkPending(true);
    setError(null);
    const results = await Promise.all(
      selectedFiles.map(async (file) => {
        try {
          if (bulkAction === "remove") {
            await removeAlbumFileAssociation(instance, account, albumId, file.id);
            return { id: file.id, ok: true as const, cleanupComplete: true };
          }

          const deletion = await deleteAlbumFile(instance, account, albumId, file.id);
          return {
            id: file.id,
            ok: true as const,
            cleanupComplete: deletion?.storageCleanupComplete !== false,
          };
        } catch {
          return { id: file.id, ok: false as const };
        }
      }),
    );

    const succeeded = results.filter((result) => result.ok).map((result) => result.id);
    const failedCount = results.length - succeeded.length;
    const cleanupWarnings = results.filter((result) => result.ok && !result.cleanupComplete).length;
    const getFileName = (fileId: number) =>
      selectedFiles.find((file) => file.id === fileId)?.originalFileName;
    const failedNames = results
      .filter((result) => !result.ok)
      .map((result) => getFileName(result.id))
      .filter(Boolean);
    const cleanupNames = results
      .filter((result) => result.ok && !result.cleanupComplete)
      .map((result) => getFileName(result.id))
      .filter(Boolean);

    removeSelected(succeeded);
    const failureSummary = failedCount
      ? `${succeeded.length} completed; ${failedCount} failed (${failedNames.join(", ")}). Failed files that remain available are selected for review or retry.`
      : null;

    setSuccessMessage(
      succeeded.length
        ? `${succeeded.length} ${bulkAction === "remove" ? `file${succeeded.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} removed from this album. The files remain in their ownersâ€™ libraries.` : `file${succeeded.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} permanently deleted.`}${cleanupWarnings ? ` Storage cleanup needs reconciliation for: ${cleanupNames.join(", ")}. The file records are already removed.` : ""}`
        : null,
    );
    clearBulkAction();
    if (!failedCount) cancelSelection();
    await reloadAlbum();
    if (failureSummary) setError(failureSummary);
    setBulkPending(false);
  };

  const handleReplace = async (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): Promise<void> => {
    const selectedFile = event.target.files?.[0];
    const fileId = replaceTargetIdRef.current;

    if (!selectedFile || !account || !Number.isFinite(albumId) || fileId === null) return;

    const validationError = validateUploadFile(selectedFile);
    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    try {
      setError(null);
      await replaceAlbumFile(instance, account, albumId, fileId, selectedFile);
      setSuccessMessage(`${selectedFile.name} replaced successfully.`);
      await reloadAlbum();
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Replace failed.");
    } finally {
      replaceTargetIdRef.current = null;
      event.target.value = "";
    }
  };

  const handleShowFileDetails = async (file: FileResponse): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account) return;

    try {
      setError(null);
      setFileDetails(await getAlbumFile(instance, account, albumId, file.id));
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to load file details.");
    }
  };

  return {
    bulkPending,
    favoritePendingIds,
    toggleFavorite,
    handleDownload,
    handleDelete,
    runBulkAction,
    handleReplace,
    handleShowFileDetails,
  };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\albums\hooks\useAlbumsData.ts
============================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { Dispatch, SetStateAction } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { getAdminAlbums, getAlbums, getMyAlbums, getSharedAlbums } from "@/features/albums/api/albumsApi";
import { albumKeys } from "@/features/albums/hooks/albumQueries";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "@/features/albums/types/album";
import { ApiError, type PagedResponse } from "@/shared/types/api";

const PAGE_SIZE = 9;
export type AlbumFilter = "all" | "shared" | "mine";

interface UseAlbumsDataOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  accountKey: string | null;
  userId: number | undefined;
  isAdminAllContent: boolean;
  currentPage: number;
  albumFilter: AlbumFilter;
  searchQuery: string;
  setCurrentPage: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
}

export function useAlbumsData({ instance, account, accountKey, userId, isAdminAllContent, currentPage, albumFilter, searchQuery, setCurrentPage }: UseAlbumsDataOptions) {
  const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const query = useQuery<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>>({
    queryKey: albumKeys.list(accountKey, albumFilter, currentPage, PAGE_SIZE, searchQuery, isAdminAllContent),
    enabled: !!account,
    placeholderData: (previousData) => previousData,
    queryFn: async ({ signal }) => {
      if (!account) throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      if (isAdminAllContent) return getAdminAlbums(instance, account, currentPage, PAGE_SIZE, searchQuery, signal);
      if (albumFilter === "mine") return getMyAlbums(instance, account, currentPage, PAGE_SIZE, searchQuery, signal);
      if (albumFilter === "all") return getAlbums(instance, account, currentPage, PAGE_SIZE, searchQuery, signal);
      try {
        return await getSharedAlbums(instance, account, currentPage, PAGE_SIZE, searchQuery, signal);
      } catch (error) {
        if (signal.aborted || !(error instanceof ApiError) || error.status !== 404 || !userId) throw error;
        const firstPage = await getAlbums(instance, account, 1, 100, searchQuery, signal);
        const accessible = [...firstPage.items];
        for (let next = 2; next <= firstPage.pageCount; next += 1) accessible.push(...(await getAlbums(instance, account, next, 100, searchQuery, signal)).items);
        const shared = accessible.filter((album) => album.ownerId !== userId);
        const pageCount = Math.ceil(shared.length / PAGE_SIZE);
        return { items: shared.slice((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, currentPage * PAGE_SIZE), totalItems: shared.length, page: currentPage, pageSize: PAGE_SIZE, pageCount };
      }
    },
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (query.data && query.data.page !== currentPage) setCurrentPage(query.data.page);
  }, [currentPage, query.data, setCurrentPage]);
  const data = query.data;
  return {
    albums: data?.items ?? [],
    pageCount: data?.pageCount ?? 1,
    totalItems: data?.totalItems ?? 0,
    loading: query.isPending || query.isFetching,
    hasLoaded: query.isSuccess || query.isError,
    error: localError ?? (query.error instanceof Error ? query.error.message : null),
    setError: setLocalError,
  };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\albums\hooks\useAlbumSharingActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import type { Dispatch, SetStateAction } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import type { AlbumMemberResponse } from "@/features/albums/types/album";
import {
  addAlbumMember,
  getAlbumMembers,
  removeAlbumMember,
  updateAlbumMember,
} from "@/features/albums/api/albumsApi";

interface UseAlbumSharingActionsOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  albumId: number;
  setMembers: Dispatch<SetStateAction<AlbumMemberResponse[]>>;
  setMemberAccessError: (message: string | null) => void;
}

export function useAlbumSharingActions({
  instance,
  account,
  albumId,
  setMembers,
  setMemberAccessError,
}: UseAlbumSharingActionsOptions) {
  const [memberEmail, setMemberEmail] = useState("");
  const [canView, setCanView] = useState(true);
  const [canDownload, setCanDownload] = useState(true);

  const handleAddMember = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account || !Number.isFinite(albumId) || !memberEmail.trim()) {
      setMemberAccessError("An email address is required to share the album.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setMemberAccessError(null);
      await addAlbumMember(instance, account, albumId, {
        email: memberEmail.trim(),
        canView,
        canDownload,
      });

      setMemberEmail("");
      setCanView(true);
      setCanDownload(true);
      setMembers(await getAlbumMembers(instance, account, albumId));
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setMemberAccessError(
        caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to share album.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateMember = async (
    userId: number,
    nextView: boolean,
    nextDownload: boolean,
  ): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account || !Number.isFinite(albumId)) return;

    try {
      setMemberAccessError(null);
      await updateAlbumMember(instance, account, albumId, userId, {
        canView: nextView,
        canDownload: nextDownload,
      });

      // Download permission also requires view permission.
      setMembers((currentMembers) =>
        currentMembers.map((member) =>
          member.userId === userId
            ? {
                ...member,
                canView: nextView || nextDownload,
                canDownload: nextDownload,
              }
            : member,
        ),
      );
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setMemberAccessError(
        caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to update member access.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveMember = async (userId: number): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account || !Number.isFinite(albumId)) return;

    try {
      setMemberAccessError(null);
      await removeAlbumMember(instance, account, albumId, userId);
      setMembers((currentMembers) => currentMembers.filter((member) => member.userId !== userId));
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setMemberAccessError(
        caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to revoke access.",
      );
    }
  };

  return {
    memberEmail,
    setMemberEmail,
    canView,
    setCanView,
    canDownload,
    setCanDownload,
    handleAddMember,
    handleUpdateMember,
    handleRemoveMember,
  };
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowLeft, Images, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import {
  deleteAlbum,
  getAlbum,
  getAlbumFiles,
  getAlbumMembers,
  getFilePreviewUrl,
  updateAlbum,
  uploadAlbumFile,
} from "../api/albumsApi";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/shared/components/ui/ErrorBanner";
import { AlbumSharingPanel } from "@/features/albums/components/AlbumSharingPanel";
import { AlbumHeader } from "@/features/albums/components/AlbumHeader";
import { AlbumFilesSection } from "@/features/albums/components/AlbumFilesSection";
import {
  BulkDeleteDialog,
  FileDetailsDialog,
  FilePreviewModal,
  MultiFileUploadDialog,
  useFilePreviews,
} from "@/features/files";
import type { AlbumMemberResponse } from "@/features/albums";
import { useBulkSelection } from "@/shared/hooks/useBulkSelection";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth";

import type { AlbumResponse } from "@/features/albums";
import type { FileCategoryFilter, FileResponse } from "@/features/files";
import { getAccountKey } from "@/shared/utils/accountKey";
import {
  useAlbumFileActions,
  type AlbumBulkAction,
} from "@/features/albums/hooks/useAlbumFileActions";
import { useAlbumSharingActions } from "@/features/albums/hooks/useAlbumSharingActions";

const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

export function AlbumDetailPage() {
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const { account, user } = useAuth();
  const accountKey = getAccountKey(account);
  const { albumId } = useParams();

  const replaceInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const albumNameInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const loadRequestVersion = useRef(0);
  // Stores the file selected for replacement while the hidden file picker is open.
  const replaceTargetIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const [album, setAlbum] = useState<AlbumResponse | null>(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<FileResponse[]>([]);
  const [totalFiles, setTotalFiles] = useState(0);
  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(1);
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<FileCategoryFilter>("All");
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const [fileDetails, setFileDetails] = useState<FileResponse | null>(null);
  const [previewFile, setPreviewFile] = useState<FileResponse | null>(null);
  const [previewSource, setPreviewSource] = useState<string | undefined>();

  const [members, setMembers] = useState<AlbumMemberResponse[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [hasLoaded, setHasLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [memberAccessError, setMemberAccessError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [showUploadDialog, setShowUploadDialog] = useState(false);
  const [deletingAlbum, setDeletingAlbum] = useState(false);
  const {
    selectionMode,
    selectedIds,
    startSelection,
    cancelSelection,
    toggle,
    selectAll,
    remove,
    retain,
  } = useBulkSelection<number>();
  const [bulkAction, setBulkAction] = useState<AlbumBulkAction>(null);

  const [albumNameDraft, setAlbumNameDraft] = useState("");
  const [editingName, setEditingName] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (editingName) albumNameInputRef.current?.focus();
  }, [editingName]);

  const numericAlbumId = Number(albumId ?? "0");
  const {
    memberEmail,
    setMemberEmail,
    canView,
    setCanView,
    canDownload,
    setCanDownload,
    handleAddMember,
    handleUpdateMember,
    handleRemoveMember,
  } = useAlbumSharingActions({
    instance,
    account,
    albumId: numericAlbumId,
    setMembers,
    setMemberAccessError,
  });

  const canManageAlbum = Boolean(
    album && user && (user.role === "Admin" || album.ownerId === user.userId),
  );

  const loadPreview = useCallback(
    async (file: FileResponse) => {
      if (!account) {
        throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      }

      return getFilePreviewUrl(instance, account, file.id, numericAlbumId, canManageAlbum);
    },
    // Keep previews stable across MSAL object refreshes for the same identity.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [accountKey, canManageAlbum, instance, numericAlbumId],
  );

  // Keeps an image available for the album cover when it exists in the loaded files.
  const albumCoverFile = files.find((file) => file.contentType.startsWith("image/"));

  // Includes the cover in preview loading even if it is not in the current file list.
  const previewFiles = useMemo(
    () => [
      ...files,
      ...(albumCoverFile && !files.some((file) => file.id === albumCoverFile.id)
        ? [albumCoverFile]
        : []),
    ],
    [files, albumCoverFile],
  );

  const { previewUrls } = useFilePreviews(previewFiles, loadPreview);

  const albumCoverPreview =
    albumCoverFile && previewUrls[albumCoverFile.id] ? albumCoverFile : undefined;

  // Loads album details, the selected file page, and sharing settings for managers.
  const loadAlbumData = useCallback(async () => {
    const requestVersion = ++loadRequestVersion.current;
    if (!account || !Number.isFinite(numericAlbumId)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      const albumResult = await getAlbum(instance, account, numericAlbumId);
      if (requestVersion !== loadRequestVersion.current) return;

      setAlbum(albumResult);
      setAlbumNameDraft(albumResult.albumName);

      const nextCanManageAlbum = user?.role === "Admin" || albumResult.ownerId === user?.userId;

      const fileResult = await getAlbumFiles(
        instance,
        account,
        numericAlbumId,
        currentPage,
        PAGE_SIZE,
        categoryFilter,
      );
      if (requestVersion !== loadRequestVersion.current) return;

      setFiles(fileResult.items);
      retain(fileResult.items.filter((file) => file.canManage).map((file) => file.id));
      setTotalFiles(fileResult.totalItems);
      setPageCount(fileResult.pageCount);
      setCurrentPage(fileResult.page);

      if (nextCanManageAlbum) {
        try {
          const albumMembers = await getAlbumMembers(instance, account, numericAlbumId);
          if (requestVersion !== loadRequestVersion.current) return;
          setMembers(albumMembers);
        } catch {
          if (requestVersion !== loadRequestVersion.current) return;
          setMembers([]);
          setError("Album opened, but sharing settings could not be loaded.");
        }
      } else {
        setMembers([]);
      }
    } catch (caughtError) {
      if (requestVersion !== loadRequestVersion.current) return;
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to load album details.",
      );
    } finally {
      if (requestVersion === loadRequestVersion.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setHasLoaded(true);
      }
    }
    // accountKey changes only when the signed-in identity changes; token refreshes reuse data.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [
    accountKey,
    instance,
    numericAlbumId,
    currentPage,
    categoryFilter,
    user?.role,
    user?.userId,
    retain,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    cancelSelection();
  }, [accountKey, numericAlbumId, cancelSelection]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!canManageAlbum) cancelSelection();
  }, [canManageAlbum, cancelSelection]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(numericAlbumId) || numericAlbumId <= 0) {
      setAlbum(null);
      setFiles([]);
      setTotalFiles(0);
      setMembers([]);
      setError("The album ID is invalid.");
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    void loadAlbumData();
    return () => {
      loadRequestVersion.current += 1;
    };
  }, [loadAlbumData, numericAlbumId]);

  const selectedFiles = files.filter((file) => selectedIds.has(file.id));
  const {
    bulkPending,
    favoritePendingIds,
    toggleFavorite,
    handleDownload,
    handleDelete,
    runBulkAction,
    handleReplace,
    handleShowFileDetails,
  } = useAlbumFileActions({
    instance,
    account,
    albumId: numericAlbumId,
    selectedFiles,
    bulkAction,
    replaceTargetIdRef,
    reloadAlbum: loadAlbumData,
    removeSelected: remove,
    cancelSelection,
    setFiles,
    setFileDetails,
    setError,
    setSuccessMessage,
    clearBulkAction: () => setBulkAction(null),
  });
  const handleUpdateAlbum = async () => {
    if (!account || !Number.isFinite(numericAlbumId) || !albumNameDraft.trim()) {
      setError("Album name is required.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setError(null);

      const updated = await updateAlbum(instance, account, numericAlbumId, {
        albumName: albumNameDraft.trim(),
      });

      setAlbum(updated);
      setAlbumNameDraft(updated.albumName);
      setEditingName(false);
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to update album.");
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteAlbum = async () => {
    if (!account || !album || deletingAlbum) {
      return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm(`Delete "${album.albumName}"? This cannot be undone.`)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setDeletingAlbum(true);
      setError(null);

      await deleteAlbum(instance, account, album.id);
      navigate("/albums", { replace: true });
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? `${caughtError.message} The album remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.`
          : "Unable to delete album. You can retry after resolving the issue.",
      );
    } finally {
      setDeletingAlbum(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading && !hasLoaded) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section className="skeleton-panel relative flex items-center gap-[17px] overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] p-[22px] max-[760px]:flex-wrap max-[760px]:p-[18px]">
          <div className="h-[78px] w-[95px] flex-none rounded-2xl bg-[#dce9f2]" />

          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-[17px]">
          {[1, 2, 3].map((item) => (
            <div key={item} className="surface h-[315px] rounded-[17px]" />
          ))}
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!album) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section role="alert" className="surface album-not-found">
          <span className="album-not-found-icon">
            <Images aria-hidden="true" size={28} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          </span>

          <h1>Album not found</h1>

          <p>{error ?? "The requested album could not be loaded."}</p>

          <Link to="/albums" className="primary-action">
            <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            Back to albums
          </Link>
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page gap-5">
      <div className="flex min-h-[22px]">
        <Link
          to="/albums"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-[7px] text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#648199] transition-[color,transform] duration-[180ms] hover:-translate-x-px hover:text-[#176fca]"
        >
          <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
          Albums
        </Link>
      </div>

      <AlbumHeader
        album={album}
        totalFiles={totalFiles}
        albumCoverPreview={albumCoverPreview}
        coverUrl={albumCoverPreview ? previewUrls[albumCoverPreview.id] : undefined}
        canManageAlbum={canManageAlbum}
        editingName={editingName}
        albumNameDraft={albumNameDraft}
        uploading={uploading}
        deletingAlbum={deletingAlbum}
        albumNameInputRef={albumNameInputRef}
        replaceInputRef={replaceInputRef}
        onNameDraftChange={setAlbumNameDraft}
        onSaveName={() => void handleUpdateAlbum()}
        onCancelEditing={() => {
          setAlbumNameDraft(album.albumName);
          setEditingName(false);
        }}
        onEditName={() => setEditingName(true)}
        onUpload={() => setShowUploadDialog(true)}
        onDelete={() => void handleDeleteAlbum()}
        onReplaceChange={handleReplace}
      />
      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error} />}

      {successMessage && (
        <p
          role="status"
          className="glass-alert-success rounded-[11px] px-3 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium text-[#34734b]"
        >
          {successMessage}
        </p>
      )}

      <AlbumFilesSection
        files={files}
        previewUrls={previewUrls}
        favoritePendingIds={favoritePendingIds}
        loadPreview={loadPreview}
        canManageAlbum={canManageAlbum}
        categoryFilter={categoryFilter}
        currentPage={currentPage}
        pageCount={pageCount}
        totalFiles={totalFiles}
        selectionMode={selectionMode}
        selectedIds={selectedIds}
        bulkPending={bulkPending}
        onCategoryChange={(category) => {
          cancelSelection();
          setCategoryFilter(category);
          setCurrentPage(1);
        }}
        onStartSelection={startSelection}
        onCancelSelection={cancelSelection}
        onSelectAll={selectAll}
        onBulkAction={setBulkAction}
        onFavoriteToggle={(file) => void toggleFavorite(file)}
        onReplace={(file) => {
          replaceTargetIdRef.current = file.id;
          if (replaceInputRef.current) {
            replaceInputRef.current.value = "";
            replaceInputRef.current.click();
          }
        }}
        onDetails={(file) => void handleShowFileDetails(file)}
        onPreview={(file, source) => {
          setPreviewFile(file);
          setPreviewSource(source);
        }}
        onDownload={(file) => void handleDownload(file)}
        onDelete={(file) => void handleDelete(file.id)}
        onSelectionToggle={(file) => toggle(file.id)}
        onUploadFirstFile={() => setShowUploadDialog(true)}
        onPageChange={(page) => {
          cancelSelection();
          setCurrentPage(page);
        }}
      />
      {canManageAlbum && (
        <AlbumSharingPanel
          members={members}
          memberEmail={memberEmail}
          canView={canView}
          canDownload={canDownload}
          memberAccessError={memberAccessError}
          onMemberEmailChange={setMemberEmail}
          onCanViewChange={setCanView}
          onCanDownloadChange={setCanDownload}
          onAddMember={handleAddMember}
          onUpdateMember={handleUpdateMember}
          onRemoveMember={handleRemoveMember}
        />
      )}

      {!canManageAlbum && (
        <section className="surface flex items-center gap-[11px] rounded-[14px] px-[16px] py-[14px]">
          <span className="grid h-[36px] w-[36px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[11px] border border-white/70 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.55)] text-[var(--accent)] shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm">
            <Users aria-hidden="true" size={18} strokeWidth={1.8} />
          </span>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <strong className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#183955]">Shared album</strong>

            <p className="mt-[3px] text-[10px] text-[#658299]">
              You have access to this collection based on the permissions assigned to your account.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {fileDetails && <FileDetailsDialog file={fileDetails} onClose={() => setFileDetails(null)} />}

      {bulkAction && (
        <BulkDeleteDialog
          count={selectedFiles.length}
          title={
            bulkAction === "remove"
              ? `Remove ${selectedFiles.length} ${selectedFiles.length === 1 ? "file" : "files"} from this album?`
              : `Permanently delete ${selectedFiles.length} ${selectedFiles.length === 1 ? "file" : "files"}?`
          }
          description={
            bulkAction === "remove"
              ? "This removes only the album association. The file and its stored content remain in the uploader's library."
              : "This permanently deletes the selected file records and stored content, including favorites. This action cannot be undone."
          }
          confirmLabel={bulkAction === "remove" ? "Remove from album" : "Delete permanently"}
          pendingLabel={bulkAction === "remove" ? "Removing..." : "Deleting..."}
          destructive={bulkAction === "delete"}
          pending={bulkPending}
          onCancel={() => setBulkAction(null)}
          onConfirm={() => void runBulkAction()}
        />
      )}

      {/* Prefer the selected preview source; fall back to the cached preview URL. */}
      {previewFile && (
        <FilePreviewModal
          file={previewFile}
          src={previewSource ?? previewUrls[previewFile.id]}
          onClose={() => {
            setPreviewFile(null);
            setPreviewSource(undefined);
          }}
        />
      )}
      {showUploadDialog && canManageAlbum && (
        <MultiFileUploadDialog
          title="Add files to album"
          description={`Upload supported files to ${album?.albumName ?? "this album"}. Each file can be up to 10 MB.`}
          onUpload={(file) => {
            if (!account || !canManageAlbum || !Number.isFinite(numericAlbumId)) {
              throw new Error("You do not have permission to upload to this album.");
            }
            return uploadAlbumFile(instance, account, numericAlbumId, file);
          }}
          onUploadingChange={(isActive) => {
            setUploading(isActive);
            if (isActive) {
              setError(null);
              setSuccessMessage(null);
            }
          }}
          onComplete={async (successCount, failureCount) => {
            if (successCount > 0) await loadAlbumData();
            setSuccessMessage(
              `Uploads finished: ${successCount} successful, ${failureCount} failed.`,
            );
          }}
          onClose={() => setShowUploadDialog(false)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\albums\pages\AlbumsPage.tsx
