import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { CalendarDays, FolderOpen, Images, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { useCreateAlbumMutation } from "@/features/albums/hooks/albumQueries";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/shared/components/ui/ErrorBanner";
import { Pagination } from "@/shared/components/ui/Pagination";
import { SearchField } from "@/shared/components/ui/SearchField";
import { pageHeaderClass, pageHeaderSkeletonClass } from "@/shared/components/ui/uiClasses";
import { useAlbumCoverPreviews } from "@/features/albums/hooks/useAlbumCoverPreviews";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth";
import { getAccountKey } from "@/shared/utils/accountKey";
import { formatDate } from "@/shared/utils/formatters";
import { getAdminContentScope } from "@/shared/utils/adminContentScope";
import { useAlbumsData, type AlbumFilter } from "@/features/albums/hooks/useAlbumsData";

export function AlbumsPage() {
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const { account, user } = useAuth();
  const accountKey = getAccountKey(account);
  const createAlbumMutation = useCreateAlbumMutation({ instance, account, accountKey });
  const isAdminAllContent = user?.role === "Admin" && getAdminContentScope() === "all";
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [albumFilter, setAlbumFilter] = useState<AlbumFilter>("all");
  const [searchInput, setSearchInput] = useState("");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [albumName, setAlbumName] = useState("");
  const { albums, pageCount, totalItems, loading, hasLoaded, error, setError } = useAlbumsData({
    instance,
    account,
    accountKey,
    userId: user?.userId,
    isAdminAllContent,
    currentPage,
    albumFilter,
    searchQuery,
    setCurrentPage,
  });
  const coverUrls = useAlbumCoverPreviews(
    albums,
    instance,
    account,
    user?.userId,
    user?.role === "Admin",
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    // Debounce search so each keystroke does not trigger a separate API request.
    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setSearchQuery(searchInput.trim());
      setCurrentPage(1);
    }, 300);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }, [searchInput]);

  const handleCreateAlbum = async () => {
    if (!account || !albumName.trim()) {
      setError("Album name is required.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setCreating(true);
      setError(null);

      const created = await createAlbumMutation.mutateAsync({
        albumName: albumName.trim(),
      });

      setAlbumName("");

      navigate(`/albums/${created.id}`);
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to create album.");
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  // Use skeletons on the initial load; later requests keep the existing page visible.
  if (loading && !hasLoaded) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section className={pageHeaderSkeletonClass}>
          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="skeleton-field" />
            <span className="skeleton-button skeleton-button-wide" />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3 max-[1200px]:grid-cols-3 max-[900px]:grid-cols-2 max-[520px]:grid-cols-1">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((item) => (
            <article
              key={item}
              className="surface min-h-[270px] w-full min-w-0 cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] p-0 text-left text-inherit transition-[transform,box-shadow,border-color] duration-[220ms] hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[rgba(180,209,231,0.75)] hover:shadow-[0_18px_38px_rgba(49,102,148,0.13),inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(202,224,240,0.3)]"
            >
              <div className="relative h-[148px] w-full overflow-hidden bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,#e3edf5,#d7e5ef)] after:absolute after:top-0 after:bottom-0 after:w-[35%] after:translate-x-[-130%] after:bg-[linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,rgba(255,255,255,0.56),transparent)] after:animate-[skeleton-shimmer_1.55s_infinite_ease-in-out] after:content-['']" />

              <div className="p-4">
                <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
                <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
                <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-xs" />
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page">
      <section className={pageHeaderClass}>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h1 className="page-heading flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="page-kicker-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
            Albums
          </h1>

          <p className="page-description">
            Organize your images and files into collections that are easy to access and share.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-[0_1_430px] gap-2 max-[960px]:w-full max-[960px]:basis-auto max-[760px]:flex-col">
          <label htmlFor="new-album-name" className="sr-only">
            New album name
          </label>

          <input
            id="new-album-name"
            value={albumName}
            onChange={(event) => setAlbumName(event.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === "Enter" && !creating) {
                void handleCreateAlbum();
              }
            }}
            placeholder="New album name"
            className="field min-w-0 flex-1"
            maxLength={200}
          />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void handleCreateAlbum()}
            disabled={creating}
            className="primary-action flex-none disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-wait max-[760px]:w-full"
          >
            <Images aria-hidden="true" size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />

            <span>{creating ? "Creating..." : "New album"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error} />}

      <section className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 max-[760px]:items-stretch">
        {!isAdminAllContent && (
          <div
            className="glass-toolbar inline-flex flex-wrap gap-1 rounded-[11px] p-[3px]"
            role="group"
            aria-label="Album filters"
          >
            {(
              [
                ["all", "All"],
                ["shared", "Shared with me"],
                ["mine", "My albums"],
              ] as const
            ).map(([value, label]) => (
              <button
                key={value}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={albumFilter === value}
                onClick={() => {
                  setAlbumFilter(value);
                  setCurrentPage(1);
                }}
                className={`min-h-[33px] rounded-[8px] border px-3 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-[color,background,box-shadow,border-color] ${albumFilter === value ? "border-white/80 bg-white/70 text-[#145a99] shadow-[0_3px_12px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)]" : "border-transparent bg-transparent text-[#557086] hover:bg-white/30 hover:text-[#194b75]"}`}
              >
                {label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        <SearchField
          value={searchInput}
          onChange={setSearchInput}
          placeholder="Search albums"
          ariaLabel="Search albums"
          className="glass-toolbar flex min-h-[39px] w-[min(300px,100%)] items-center gap-2 rounded-[11px] px-[11px] text-[#8195a5] focus-within:border-[#87a9c2] focus-within:shadow-[0_0_0_3px_rgba(84,132,169,0.1)] max-[760px]:w-full"
          inputClassName="min-w-0 flex-1 border-0 bg-transparent text-[11px] text-[#284963] outline-none placeholder:text-[#8b9eae]"
          iconSize={15}
        />
      </section>

      <section className="min-w-0">
        {albums.length === 0 ? (
          <section className="surface flex min-h-[300px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-[18px] px-[22px] py-[38px] text-center">
            <span className="grid h-[62px] w-[62px] place-items-center rounded-[19px] border border-white/80 bg-white/50 text-[var(--accent)] shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.9),0_6px_18px_rgba(26,54,80,0.06)] backdrop-blur-md">
              <Images aria-hidden="true" size={28} strokeWidth={1.5} />
            </span>

            <h2 className="mt-4 mb-0 text-[15px] font-[650] text-[#29445d]">No albums available</h2>

            <p className="mt-[7px] mb-[18px] max-w-[390px] text-[10px] leading-[1.6] text-[#8497a7]">
              Create your first album to start organizing your WinCapture files.
            </p>

            <div className="albums-empty-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  document.getElementById("new-album-name")?.focus();
                }}
                className="primary-action"
              >
                <Images aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                Create album
              </button>
            </div>
          </section>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3 max-[1200px]:grid-cols-3 max-[900px]:grid-cols-2 max-[520px]:grid-cols-1">
            {albums.map((album) => (
              <button
                key={album.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => navigate(`/albums/${album.id}`)}
                className="surface group w-full min-w-0 cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] p-0 text-left text-inherit transition-[transform,box-shadow,border-color] duration-[220ms] hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.85)] hover:shadow-[var(--glass-shadow-raised)]"
              >
                <div className="relative h-[148px] overflow-hidden max-[760px]:h-[132px] max-[520px]:h-[148px]">
                  <div className="relative grid h-full w-full place-items-center overflow-hidden bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_25%_20%,rgba(255,255,255,0.55),transparent_35%),linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(215,238,255,0.7)_0%,rgba(180,218,250,0.5)_48%,rgba(215,235,250,0.65)_100%)]">
                    {coverUrls[album.id] ? (
                      <img
                        src={coverUrls[album.id]}
                        alt=""
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <span className="absolute right-[-22px] top-[-92px] h-[170px] w-[170px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(75,158,237,0.24)] blur-[1px]" />
                        <span className="absolute bottom-[-78px] left-[-23px] h-[125px] w-[125px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.36)] blur-[1px]" />

                        <span className="relative z-[1] grid h-[61px] w-[61px] place-items-center rounded-[18px] border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.8)] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.55)] text-[var(--accent)] shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(26,54,80,0.06),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.9)] backdrop-blur-md">
                          <Images aria-hidden="true" size={28} strokeWidth={1.45} />
                        </span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="px-[15px] pt-[15px] pb-[13px] max-[760px]:p-3">
                  <h3
                    className="m-0 overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[13px] leading-[1.3] font-[650] text-[#183b59]"
                    title={album.albumName}
                  >
                    {album.albumName}
                  </h3>

                  <div className="mt-[15px] grid grid-cols-2 gap-[9px] border-t border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.45)] pt-3 max-[760px]:grid-cols-1 max-[760px]:gap-[7px]">
                    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="grid h-7 w-7 flex-none place-items-center rounded-[9px] border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.5)] text-[#246bb1]">
                        <FolderOpen aria-hidden="true" size={14} strokeWidth={1.7} />
                      </span>

                      <span className="grid min-w-0 gap-0.5">
                        <small className="text-[11px] text-[#869cb0]">Owner</small>
                        <strong
                          className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#3b5973]"
                          title={album.ownerName}
                        >
                          {album.ownerName}
                        </strong>
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="grid h-7 w-7 flex-none place-items-center rounded-[9px] border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.5)] text-[#246bb1]">
                        <CalendarDays aria-hidden="true" size={14} strokeWidth={1.7} />
                      </span>

                      <span className="grid min-w-0 gap-0.5">
                        <small className="text-[11px] text-[#869cb0]">Created</small>
                        <strong className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#3b5973]">
                          {formatDate(album.createdAt)}
                        </strong>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-[13px] flex items-center justify-between gap-[10px] border-t border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.45)] pt-[11px] text-[8px] text-[#8299aa]">
                    <span>
                      {album.updatedAt ? `Updated ${formatDate(album.updatedAt)}` : "Not updated"}
                    </span>

                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[8px] font-semibold text-[#1a74d2]">
                      Open
                      <ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={13} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        {totalItems > 0 && (
          <Pagination
            currentPage={currentPage}
            pageCount={pageCount}
            onPageChange={setCurrentPage}
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

import { z } from "zod";
import { ApiError } from "@/shared/types/api";

const positiveId = z.number().int().safe().positive();
const validDate = z.string().min(1).refine((value) => Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)));

export const AlbumResponseSchema = z.object({
  id: positiveId,
  albumName: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  ownerId: positiveId,
  ownerName: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  createdAt: validDate,
  updatedAt: validDate.nullable(),
});

export const PagedAlbumsSchema = z.object({
  items: z.array(AlbumResponseSchema),
  totalItems: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
  page: z.number().int().safe().positive(),
  pageSize: z.number().int().safe().positive(),
  pageCount: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
});

export const AlbumNameRequestSchema = z.object({ albumName: z.string().min(1).max(200).refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0) });
export const AddAlbumMemberRequestSchema = z.object({
  email: z.email(),
  canView: z.boolean(),
  canDownload: z.boolean(),
}).refine((request) => request.canView || !request.canDownload);
export const UpdateAlbumMemberRequestSchema = z.object({
  canView: z.boolean(),
  canDownload: z.boolean(),
}).refine((request) => request.canView || !request.canDownload);

export function validateAlbumNameRequest(value: unknown) {
  const result = AlbumNameRequestSchema.safeParse(value);
  if (!result.success) throw new ApiError("Album name must contain 1 to 200 characters.", 400);
  return result.data;
}

function invalidAlbumResponse(): never {
  throw new ApiError(
    "WinCapture received invalid album information. Refresh and try again.",
    502,
    undefined,
    undefined,
    "INVALID_ALBUM_RESPONSE",
  );
}

export function parseAlbumResponse(value: unknown) {
  const result = AlbumResponseSchema.safeParse(value);
  if (!result.success) invalidAlbumResponse();
  return result.data;
}

export function parsePagedAlbums(value: unknown) {
  const result = PagedAlbumsSchema.safeParse(value);
  if (!result.success) invalidAlbumResponse();
  return result.data;
}

import { z } from "zod";
import { ApiError } from "@/shared/types/api";

export const AlbumMemberSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number().int().safe().positive(),
  name: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  email: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  canView: z.boolean(),
  canDownload: z.boolean(),
  grantedAt: z.string().min(1).refine((value) => Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value))),
}).refine((member) => !member.canDownload || member.canView);

function invalidAlbumMembers(correlationId?: string): never {
  throw new ApiError(
    "WinCapture received invalid album sharing data. Refresh the album and retry.",
    502,
    undefined,
    correlationId,
    "INVALID_ALBUM_MEMBERS_RESPONSE",
  );
}

export function parseAlbumMembers(value: unknown, correlationId?: string): z.infer<typeof AlbumMemberSchema>[] {
  const result = z.array(AlbumMemberSchema).safeParse(value);
  if (!result.success) invalidAlbumMembers(correlationId);
  return result.data;
}

import type { z } from "zod";
import type { AlbumNameRequestSchema, AlbumResponseSchema, AddAlbumMemberRequestSchema, UpdateAlbumMemberRequestSchema } from "@/features/albums/schemas/album";
import type { AlbumMemberSchema } from "@/features/albums/schemas/albumMember";

export type UpdateAlbumRequest = z.infer<typeof AlbumNameRequestSchema>;

import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { callApi } from "@/shared/api/request";
import type { CurrentUser } from "@/features/auth/types/auth";
import { parseCurrentUser } from "@/features/auth/schemas/currentUser";

export async function getCurrentUser(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
): Promise<CurrentUser> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, "/api/auth/me");

  const correlationId =
    response.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? response.headers.get("request-id") ?? undefined;
  return parseCurrentUser(await response.json(), correlationId);
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\auth\AuthContext.tsx
============================================================

import {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";

import type { AccountInfo } from "@azure/msal-browser";

import { InteractionStatus } from "@azure/msal-browser";

import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";

import { getCurrentUser } from "./api/authApi";
import { ApiError } from "@/shared/types/api";
import type { CurrentUser } from "@/features/auth/types/auth";

import { loginRequest, msalPostLogoutRedirectUri } from "@/config/auth";

interface AuthContextValue {
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  user: CurrentUser | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  login: () => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshUser: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(undefined);
const PICK_ACCOUNT_AFTER_LOGOUT_KEY = "wincapture.pick-account-after-logout";

function accountPickerPending(): boolean {
  try {
    return sessionStorage.getItem(PICK_ACCOUNT_AFTER_LOGOUT_KEY) === "true";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function setAccountPickerPending(pending: boolean): void {
  try {
    if (pending) sessionStorage.setItem(PICK_ACCOUNT_AFTER_LOGOUT_KEY, "true");
    else sessionStorage.removeItem(PICK_ACCOUNT_AFTER_LOGOUT_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Auth can continue if browser storage is unavailable.
  }
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { instance, accounts, inProgress } = useMsal();

  const [user, setUser] = useState<CurrentUser | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const requestVersion = useRef(0);
  const logoutStarted = useRef(false);
  const profileAccountId = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const refreshUserRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve());

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!instance.getActiveAccount() && accounts.length > 0) {
      const firstAccount = accounts[0];
      if (firstAccount) instance.setActiveAccount(firstAccount);
    }
  }, [instance, accounts]);

  const account = instance.getActiveAccount() ?? accounts[0] ?? null;

  const refreshUser = useCallback(async () => {
    const version = ++requestVersion.current;
    if (!account) {
      profileAccountId.current = null;
      setUser(null);
      setLoading(false);
      setError(null);
      return;
    }

    if (profileAccountId.current !== account.homeAccountId) {
      profileAccountId.current = null;
      setUser(null);
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    let profileTimeout: number | undefined;
    try {
      const currentUser = await Promise.race([
        getCurrentUser(instance, account),
        new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
          profileTimeout = window.setTimeout(
            () =>
              reject(
                new Error(
                  "WinCapture sign-in took too long. Check that the backend is running, then retry.",
                ),
              ),
            15_000,
          );
        }),
      ]);

      if (requestVersion.current === version) {
        profileAccountId.current = account.homeAccountId;
        setUser(currentUser);
      }
    } catch (caughtError) {
      if (requestVersion.current === version) {
        profileAccountId.current = null;
        setUser(null);
        setError(
          caughtError instanceof ApiError
            ? caughtError.message
            : "Microsoft authentication succeeded, but WinCapture could not load your profile. Retry the request.",
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (profileTimeout !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(profileTimeout);
      if (requestVersion.current === version) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [account, instance]);

  refreshUserRef.current = refreshUser;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (inProgress !== InteractionStatus.None || logoutStarted.current) return;

    if (accountPickerPending()) {
      setAccountPickerPending(false);
      setError(null);
      setLoading(true);
      void instance.loginRedirect({ ...loginRequest, prompt: "select_account" }).catch(() => {
        setError("Microsoft account selection could not be started.");
        setLoading(false);
      });
      return;
    }

    void refreshUserRef.current();
  }, [account?.homeAccountId, inProgress, instance]);

  const login = useCallback(async () => {
    setAccountPickerPending(false);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await instance.loginRedirect(loginRequest);
    } catch {
      setError("Microsoft sign-in could not be started. Check your connection and retry.");
    }
  }, [instance]);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    setAccountPickerPending(true);
    logoutStarted.current = true;
    requestVersion.current += 1;
    profileAccountId.current = null;
    setUser(null);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await instance.logoutRedirect({
        account: account ?? undefined,
        postLogoutRedirectUri: msalPostLogoutRedirectUri,
      });
    } catch {
      setAccountPickerPending(false);
      logoutStarted.current = false;
      setError("Microsoft logout could not be completed.");
    }
  }, [account, instance]);

  const value = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(
    () => ({
      account,
      user,
      loading: loading || inProgress !== InteractionStatus.None,
      error,
      login,
      logout,
      refreshUser,
    }),
    [account, user, loading, inProgress, error, login, logout, refreshUser],
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider.");
  }

  return context;
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\auth\schemas\currentUser.ts

import { z } from "zod";
import { ApiError } from "@/shared/types/api";
import type { CurrentUser } from "@/features/auth/types/auth";

export const CurrentUserSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number().int().safe().positive(),
  name: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  email: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  role: z.enum(["User", "Admin"]),
  authenticationType: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
});

export function parseCurrentUser(value: unknown, correlationId?: string): CurrentUser {
  const result = CurrentUserSchema.safeParse(value);
  if (!result.success) {
    throw new ApiError(
      "WinCapture received an invalid account profile. Retry, or contact support if the problem continues.",
      502,
      undefined,
      correlationId,
      "INVALID_PROFILE_RESPONSE",
    );
  }
  return result.data;
}

export type UserRole = "User" | "Admin";

export interface CurrentUser {
  userId: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: UserRole;
  authenticationType: string;
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\dashboard\hooks\useDashboardAdminActions.ts
============================================================

import type { Dispatch, SetStateAction } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { deleteAlbum } from "@/features/albums/api/albumsApi";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "@/features/albums/types/album";
import { deleteFile } from "@/features/files/api/filesApi";
import type { DashboardFile } from "@/features/dashboard/types/dashboard";

interface UseDashboardAdminActionsOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  canManageContent: boolean;
  setAlbums: Dispatch<SetStateAction<AlbumResponse[]>>;
  setFiles: Dispatch<SetStateAction<DashboardFile[]>>;
  resetAdminPages: () => void;
  refreshContent: () => void;
  setError: (message: string | null) => void;
}

export function useDashboardAdminActions({
  instance,
  account,
  canManageContent,
  setAlbums,
  setFiles,
  resetAdminPages,
  refreshContent,
  setError,
}: UseDashboardAdminActionsOptions) {
  const handleDeleteAlbum = async (album: AlbumResponse): Promise<void> => {
    if (
      !account ||
      !canManageContent ||
      !window.confirm(`Delete "${album.albumName}" and all its files? This cannot be undone.`)
    ) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setError(null);
      await deleteAlbum(instance, account, album.id);
      setAlbums((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== album.id));
      resetAdminPages();
      refreshContent();
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? `${caughtError.message} The album remains listed, so you can retry.`
          : "Unable to delete album. You can retry after resolving the issue.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteFile = async (file: DashboardFile): Promise<void> => {
    if (
      !account ||
      !canManageContent ||
      !window.confirm(`Delete "${file.originalFileName}"? This cannot be undone.`)
    ) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setError(null);
      await deleteFile(instance, account, file.id);
      setFiles((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== file.id));
      resetAdminPages();
      refreshContent();
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? `${caughtError.message} The file remains listed, so you can retry.`
          : "Unable to delete file. You can retry after resolving the issue.",
      );
    }
  };

  return { handleDeleteAlbum, handleDeleteFile };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\dashboard\hooks\useDashboardData.ts
============================================================

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { Dispatch, SetStateAction } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { getAdminAlbums, getMyAlbums } from "@/features/albums/api/albumsApi";
import type { AlbumResponse } from "@/features/albums/types/album";
import { getAdminFiles, getFiles } from "@/features/files/api/filesApi";
import {
  DASHBOARD_FILE_CATEGORIES,
  ALL_CONTENT_PAGE_SIZE,
} from "@/features/dashboard/constants/dashboard";
import type { DashboardFile } from "@/features/dashboard/types/dashboard";

interface UseDashboardDataOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  accountKey: string | null;
  canViewAllContent: boolean;
  contentScope: "mine" | "all";
  contentVersion: number;
  allAlbumsPage: number;
  allFilesPage: number;
  allAlbumsSearch: string;
  allFilesSearch: string;
  setAlbums: Dispatch<SetStateAction<AlbumResponse[]>>;
  setFiles: Dispatch<SetStateAction<DashboardFile[]>>;
  setAllAlbumsPage: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
  setAllFilesPage: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
  setAllAlbumsPageCount: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
  setAllFilesPageCount: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
  setAllAlbumsCount: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
  setAllFilesCount: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
}

export function useDashboardData({
  instance,
  account,
  accountKey,
  canViewAllContent,
  contentScope,
  contentVersion,
  allAlbumsPage,
  allFilesPage,
  allAlbumsSearch,
  allFilesSearch,
  setAlbums,
  setFiles,
  setAllAlbumsPage,
  setAllFilesPage,
  setAllAlbumsPageCount,
  setAllFilesPageCount,
  setAllAlbumsCount,
  setAllFilesCount,
}: UseDashboardDataOptions) {
  const [fileCategoryCounts, setFileCategoryCounts] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [hasLoaded, setHasLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const albumsDataKey = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const filesDataKey = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const categoryCountsCache = useRef(
    new Map<string, { version: number; counts: Record<string, number> }>(),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function load(): Promise<void> {
      if (!account) {
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const allScope = canViewAllContent && contentScope === "all";
      const requestedAlbumsKey = allScope
        ? `${accountKey}:all:${contentVersion}:${allAlbumsPage}:${allAlbumsSearch}`
        : `${accountKey}:mine:${contentVersion}`;
      const requestedFilesKey = allScope
        ? `${accountKey}:all:${contentVersion}:${allFilesPage}:${allFilesSearch}`
        : `${accountKey}:mine:${contentVersion}`;
      const effectiveContentScope = contentScope === "all" && canViewAllContent ? "all" : "mine";
      const requestedCountsKey = `${accountKey}:${effectiveContentScope}`;
      const cachedCounts = categoryCountsCache.current.get(requestedCountsKey);
      const countsAreCurrent = cachedCounts?.version === contentVersion;
      const shouldLoadAlbums = albumsDataKey.current !== requestedAlbumsKey;
      const shouldLoadFiles = filesDataKey.current !== requestedFilesKey;
      const shouldLoadCounts = !countsAreCurrent;

      if (!shouldLoadAlbums && !shouldLoadFiles && !shouldLoadCounts) {
        setFileCategoryCounts(cachedCounts.counts);
        setLoading(false);
        setHasLoaded(true);
        return;
      }

      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        setFileCategoryCounts(countsAreCurrent ? cachedCounts.counts : {});

        const albumsRequest = !shouldLoadAlbums
          ? Promise.resolve(null)
          : allScope
            ? getAdminAlbums(
                instance,
                account,
                allAlbumsPage,
                ALL_CONTENT_PAGE_SIZE,
                allAlbumsSearch.trim() || undefined,
              )
            : getMyAlbums(instance, account, 1, 4);
        const filesRequest = !shouldLoadFiles
          ? Promise.resolve(null)
          : allScope
            ? getAdminFiles(
                instance,
                account,
                allFilesPage,
                ALL_CONTENT_PAGE_SIZE,
                allFilesSearch.trim() || undefined,
              ).then((fileResult) => ({
                page: fileResult.page,
                pageCount: fileResult.pageCount,
                totalItems: fileResult.totalItems,
                items: fileResult.items.map((file): DashboardFile => ({
                  ...file,
                  url: "",
                  downloadUrl:
                    file.albumId === null
                      ? `/api/files/${file.id}/download`
                      : `/api/albums/${file.albumId}/files/${file.id}/download`,
                  isFavorite: false,
                  canManage: true,
                  canDownload: true,
                })),
              }))
            : getFiles(instance, account, 1, 4);
        const [albumResult, fileResult, categoryResults] = await Promise.all([
          albumsRequest,
          filesRequest,
          shouldLoadCounts
            ? Promise.all(
                DASHBOARD_FILE_CATEGORIES.map(({ label }) =>
                  effectiveContentScope === "all"
                    ? getAdminFiles(instance, account, 1, 1, undefined, label)
                    : getFiles(instance, account, 1, 1, undefined, label),
                ),
              )
            : Promise.resolve([]),
        ]);

        if (!active) return;

        if (albumResult) {
          setAlbums(albumResult.items);
          setAllAlbumsPage(albumResult.page ?? 1);
          setAllAlbumsPageCount(albumResult.pageCount ?? 1);
          setAllAlbumsCount(albumResult.totalItems);
          albumsDataKey.current = requestedAlbumsKey;
        }
        if (fileResult) {
          setFiles(fileResult.items);
          setAllFilesPage(fileResult.page ?? 1);
          setAllFilesPageCount(fileResult.pageCount ?? 1);
          setAllFilesCount(fileResult.totalItems);
          filesDataKey.current = requestedFilesKey;
        }
        if (shouldLoadCounts) {
          const counts = Object.fromEntries(
            DASHBOARD_FILE_CATEGORIES.map(({ label }, index) => [
              label,
              categoryResults[index]?.totalItems ?? 0,
            ]),
          );
          categoryCountsCache.current.set(requestedCountsKey, {
            version: contentVersion,
            counts,
          });
          setFileCategoryCounts(counts);
        }
      } catch (caughtError) {
        if (active) {
          setError(
            caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to load dashboard data.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) {
          setLoading(false);
          setHasLoaded(true);
        }
      }
    }

    void load();
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
    // accountKey changes only when the signed-in identity changes.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [
    accountKey,
    allAlbumsPage,
    allAlbumsSearch,
    allFilesPage,
    allFilesSearch,
    canViewAllContent,
    contentScope,
    contentVersion,
    instance,
  ]);

  return { fileCategoryCounts, loading, hasLoaded, error, setError };
}

import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { FolderOpen, Images } from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import { getFilePreviewUrl } from "@/features/albums";
import { getFilePreviewUrlByPath } from "@/shared/api/previews";
import { FilePreviewModal, useFilePreviews } from "@/features/files";
import { ErrorBanner } from "@/shared/components/ui/ErrorBanner";
import { useAuth } from "@/features/auth";
import { useAlbumCoverPreviews, type AlbumResponse } from "@/features/albums";
import type { UserRole } from "@/features/auth";
import { getAccountKey } from "@/shared/utils/accountKey";
import { getAdminContentScope, setAdminContentScope } from "@/shared/utils/adminContentScope";
import { useDashboardAdminActions } from "@/features/dashboard/hooks/useDashboardAdminActions";
import { useDashboardData } from "@/features/dashboard/hooks/useDashboardData";
import { DASHBOARD_FILE_CATEGORIES } from "@/features/dashboard/constants/dashboard";
import type { DashboardFile } from "@/features/dashboard/types/dashboard";
import { DashboardAlbumsPanel } from "@/features/dashboard/components/DashboardAlbumsPanel";
import { DashboardFilesPanel } from "@/features/dashboard/components/DashboardFilesPanel";

const dashboardStatCardClass =
  "surface group relative flex min-h-[78px] cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] px-3 py-2 text-left transition-[transform,border-color,box-shadow,background] duration-200 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.85)] hover:shadow-[var(--glass-shadow-raised)] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--focus-ring)] max-[760px]:min-h-[74px] max-[760px]:gap-2 max-[760px]:px-2.5 max-[760px]:py-2";

// Keep dashboard access role-driven. Add a role here when the backend grants it
// access to application-wide content management.
const dashboardRoleCapabilities: Record<UserRole, { canViewAllContent: boolean }> = {
  User: { canViewAllContent: false },
  Admin: { canViewAllContent: true },
};

export function Dashboard() {
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const { account, user } = useAuth();
  const accountKey = getAccountKey(account);
  const canViewAllContent = user
    ? (dashboardRoleCapabilities[user.role]?.canViewAllContent ?? false)
    : false;

  const [albums, setAlbums] = useState<AlbumResponse[]>([]);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<DashboardFile[]>([]);
  const [contentScope, setContentScope] = useState<"mine" | "all">(() => getAdminContentScope());
  const [contentVersion, setContentVersion] = useState(0);
  const [allAlbumsPage, setAllAlbumsPage] = useState(1);
  const [allFilesPage, setAllFilesPage] = useState(1);
  const [allAlbumsSearch, setAllAlbumsSearch] = useState("");
  const [allFilesSearch, setAllFilesSearch] = useState("");
  const [allAlbumsPageCount, setAllAlbumsPageCount] = useState(1);
  const [allFilesPageCount, setAllFilesPageCount] = useState(1);
  const [allAlbumsCount, setAllAlbumsCount] = useState(0);
  const [allFilesCount, setAllFilesCount] = useState(0);
  const { fileCategoryCounts, loading, hasLoaded, error, setError } = useDashboardData({
    instance,
    account,
    accountKey,
    canViewAllContent,
    contentScope,
    contentVersion,
    allAlbumsPage,
    allFilesPage,
    allAlbumsSearch,
    allFilesSearch,
    setAlbums,
    setFiles,
    setAllAlbumsPage,
    setAllFilesPage,
    setAllAlbumsPageCount,
    setAllFilesPageCount,
    setAllAlbumsCount,
    setAllFilesCount,
  });

  // Keep these arrays stable while their source data is unchanged. The preview
  // hooks use the array reference as an effect dependency and should not restart
  // their network requests after unrelated dashboard state updates.
  const recentAlbums = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...albums].sort((left, right) => Date.parse(right.createdAt) - Date.parse(left.createdAt)),
    [albums],
  );
  const recentFiles = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...files].sort((left, right) => Date.parse(right.uploadedAt) - Date.parse(left.uploadedAt)),
    [files],
  );

  const albumCoverUrls = useAlbumCoverPreviews(
    recentAlbums,
    instance,
    account,
    user?.userId,
    contentScope === "all" && canViewAllContent,
  );

  const loadFilePreview = useCallback(
    (file: DashboardFile) => {
      if (!account) {
        throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      }

      if (contentScope === "all" && canViewAllContent) {
        const path =
          file.albumId == null
            ? `/api/files/${file.id}/download`
            : `/api/albums/${file.albumId}/files/${file.id}/download`;
        return getFilePreviewUrlByPath(instance, account, path, file.contentType);
      }

      return getFilePreviewUrl(instance, account, file.id);
    },
    // Keep this callback stable when MSAL refreshes the same account object.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [accountKey, canViewAllContent, contentScope, instance],
  );

  const { previewUrls: recentFilePreviewUrls } = useFilePreviews(recentFiles, loadFilePreview);

  const { handleDeleteAlbum, handleDeleteFile } = useDashboardAdminActions({
    instance,
    account,
    canManageContent: canViewAllContent,
    setAlbums,
    setFiles,
    resetAdminPages: () => {
      setAllAlbumsPage(1);
      setAllFilesPage(1);
    },
    refreshContent: () => setContentVersion((version) => version + 1),
    setError,
  });

  const [previewFile, setPreviewFile] = useState<DashboardFile | null>(null);

  // Keep the dashboard layout stable after its initial load.
  if (loading && !hasLoaded) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page !gap-3">
        <section className="skeleton-panel flex min-h-[138px] items-center justify-between rounded-[20px] p-6">
          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <span className="skeleton-button" />
            <span className="skeleton-button skeleton-button-wide" />
          </div>
        </section>
        <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 max-[760px]:gap-[10px]">
          {[1, 2].map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item}
              className="skeleton-panel flex min-h-[76px] items-center gap-3 rounded-[17px] p-[10px]"
            >
              <span className="skeleton-icon" />
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
              <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-value" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </section>
        <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-5 max-[960px]:grid-cols-1 max-[760px]:gap-4">
          <div className="skeleton-panel min-h-[355px] rounded-[18px]" />
          <div className="skeleton-panel min-h-[355px] rounded-[18px]" />
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page !gap-3">
      <section className="surface relative flex min-h-[138px] items-center justify-between gap-4 overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] px-6 py-4 max-[960px]:flex-col max-[960px]:items-start max-[760px]:rounded-[17px] max-[760px]:px-4 max-[760px]:py-4">
        <div className="relative z-[1] flex max-w-[740px] flex-col justify-center">
          <h1 className="m-0 text-[clamp(28px,2.7vw,35px)] font-bold leading-[1.04] tracking-[-0.045em] text-[#173a55] max-[760px]:text-[27px]">
            Welcome back, <span className="text-[#397eaf]">{user?.name ?? "User"}</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="mb-0 mt-2 max-w-[620px] text-[12px] leading-[1.55] text-[#687f91] max-[760px]:mt-2 max-[760px]:text-[11px]">
            Keep your albums organized, manage your files, and access your shared collections from
            one place.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2 max-[760px]:mt-3 max-[760px]:w-full max-[760px]:[&>button]:flex-1">
            <Link
              to="/albums"
              className="primary-action compact-action !min-h-[36px] !rounded-[10px] !px-3 text-[10px]"
            >
              <Images aria-hidden="true" size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <span>Browse albums</span>
            </Link>
            <Link
              to="/files"
              className="secondary-action compact-action !min-h-[36px] !rounded-[10px] !px-3 text-[10px]"
            >
              <FolderOpen aria-hidden="true" size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <span>Open files</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error} />}

      {canViewAllContent && (
        <section
          className="surface flex w-fit max-w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[15px] p-1.5"
          role="group"
          aria-label="Dashboard content scope"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={contentScope === "mine"}
            onClick={() => {
              setAdminContentScope("mine");
              setContentScope("mine");
            }}
            className={`min-h-10 rounded-[10px] px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${contentScope === "mine" ? "bg-white/80 text-[#145a99] shadow-sm" : "text-[#617c91] hover:bg-white/40"}`}
          >
            My Content
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={contentScope === "all"}
            onClick={() => {
              setAdminContentScope("all");
              setContentScope("all");
            }}
            className={`min-h-10 rounded-[10px] px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${contentScope === "all" ? "bg-white/80 text-[#145a99] shadow-sm" : "text-[#617c91] hover:bg-white/40"}`}
          >
            All Content
          </button>
        </section>
      )}

      <section
        aria-label="File counts by type"
        className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-2.5 max-[960px]:grid-cols-3 max-[760px]:grid-cols-2 max-[520px]:gap-2"
      >
        {DASHBOARD_FILE_CATEGORIES.map(({ label, icon: CategoryIcon }) => (
          <div key={label} className={`${dashboardStatCardClass} rounded-[15px]`}>
            <CategoryIcon
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="flex-none text-[#267fe2]"
              size={19}
              strokeWidth={1.8}
            />
            <span className="grid min-w-0 gap-0">
              <span className="overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#71889c] max-[520px]:text-[9px]">
                {label}
              </span>
              <strong className="text-[19px] font-bold leading-none tracking-[-0.04em] text-[#143653]">
                {fileCategoryCounts[label] ?? 0}
              </strong>
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>

      <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-5 max-[960px]:grid-cols-1 max-[760px]:gap-4">
        <DashboardAlbumsPanel
          albums={recentAlbums}
          coverUrls={albumCoverUrls}
          contentScope={contentScope}
          canViewAllContent={canViewAllContent}
          albumCount={allAlbumsCount}
          search={allAlbumsSearch}
          page={allAlbumsPage}
          pageCount={allAlbumsPageCount}
          onSearchChange={(value) => {
            setAllAlbumsSearch(value);
            setAllAlbumsPage(1);
          }}
          onPageChange={setAllAlbumsPage}
          onDelete={(album) => void handleDeleteAlbum(album)}
        />
        <DashboardFilesPanel
          files={recentFiles}
          previewUrls={recentFilePreviewUrls}
          contentScope={contentScope}
          canViewAllContent={canViewAllContent}
          fileCount={allFilesCount}
          search={allFilesSearch}
          page={allFilesPage}
          pageCount={allFilesPageCount}
          onSearchChange={(value) => {
            setAllFilesSearch(value);
            setAllFilesPage(1);
          }}
          onPageChange={setAllFilesPage}
          onPreview={setPreviewFile}
          onDelete={(file) => void handleDeleteFile(file)}
        />
      </section>

      {previewFile && (
        <FilePreviewModal
          file={previewFile}
          src={recentFilePreviewUrls[previewFile.id]}
          onClose={() => setPreviewFile(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { callApi } from "@/shared/api/request";
import { buildPagePath } from "@/shared/api/pagination";
import { downloadResponse, uploadMultipartFile } from "@/shared/api/transfers";
import type { PagedResponse } from "@/shared/types/api";
import type { AdminFileResponse, FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import {
  parseAdminFileResponse,
  parseFileResponse,
  parsePagedResponse,
  parseStorageCleanupResponse,
} from "@/features/files/schemas/fileResponse";
// Personal Files
export async function getFiles(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number,
  search?: string,
  category?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<PagedResponse<FileResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/files", page, pageSize, search, category),
    { signal },
  );

  return parsePagedResponse(await response.json(), parseFileResponse);
}

export async function getFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  fileId: number,
): Promise<FileResponse> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, `/api/files/${fileId}`);

  return parseFileResponse(await response.json());
}

export async function getFavorites(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number,
  search?: string,
  category?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<PagedResponse<FileResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/files/favorites", page, pageSize, search, category),
    { signal },
  );

  return parsePagedResponse(await response.json(), parseFileResponse);
}

export async function addFavorite(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  fileId: number,
): Promise<void> {
  await callApi(instance, account, `/api/files/${fileId}/favorite`, {
    method: "PUT",
  });
}

export async function removeFavorite(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  fileId: number,
): Promise<void> {
  await callApi(instance, account, `/api/files/${fileId}/favorite`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
}

export async function uploadFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  file: File,
): Promise<FileResponse> {
  const value: unknown = await uploadMultipartFile(instance, account, "/api/files", "POST", file);
  return parseFileResponse(value);
}

export async function replaceFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  fileId: number,
  file: File,
): Promise<FileResponse> {
  const value: unknown = await uploadMultipartFile(
    instance,
    account,
    `/api/files/${fileId}`,
    "PUT",
    file,
  );
  return parseFileResponse(value);
}

export async function downloadFileByPath(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  fallbackFileName: string,
): Promise<void> {
  await downloadResponse(instance, account, path, fallbackFileName);
}

export async function deleteFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  fileId: number,
): Promise<{ storageCleanupComplete: boolean }> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, `/api/files/${fileId}`, {
    method: "DELETE",
  });
  if (response.status === 204) return { storageCleanupComplete: true };
  return parseStorageCleanupResponse(await response.json());
}

// Admin API
export async function getAdminFiles(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number,
  search?: string,
  category?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<PagedResponse<AdminFileResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/admin/files", page, pageSize, search, category),
    { signal },
  );

  return parsePagedResponse(await response.json(), parseAdminFileResponse);
}

export async function getMyAdminFiles(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  page = 1,
  pageSize = 10,
  search?: string,
): Promise<PagedResponse<AdminFileResponse>> {
  const response = await callApi(
    instance,
    account,
    buildPagePath("/api/admin/files/mine", page, pageSize, search),
  );

  return parsePagedResponse(await response.json(), parseAdminFileResponse);
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\components\FileCard.tsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  FileArchive,
  FileAudio,
  FileImage,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  FileText,
  FileVideo,
  Presentation,
  Star,
  Play,
} from "lucide-react";

import { FileActionMenu } from "./files/FileActionMenu";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import { getFileCategory } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import { formatFileType } from "@/shared/utils/formatters";
import { PreviewThumbnail } from "./files/PreviewThumbnail";

interface FileCardProps {
  file: FileResponse;
  previewUrl?: string;
  canManage?: boolean;
  favoritePending?: boolean;
  onFavoriteToggle: () => void;
  onReplace: () => void;
  onDetails: () => void;
  onPreview: (file: FileResponse, previewUrl?: string) => void;
  loadPreview?: (file: FileResponse) => Promise<string>;
  onDownload: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  selected?: boolean;
  onSelectionToggle?: () => void;
  selectionDisabled?: boolean;
  focused?: boolean;
}

export function FileCard({
  file,
  previewUrl,
  canManage,
  favoritePending = false,
  onFavoriteToggle,
  onReplace,
  onDetails,
  onPreview,
  loadPreview,
  onDownload,
  onDelete,
  selected = false,
  onSelectionToggle,
  selectionDisabled = false,
  focused = false,
}: FileCardProps) {
  const fileCategory = getFileCategory(file.originalFileName);
  const isImage = fileCategory === "Images";

  const effectiveCanManage = canManage ?? file.canManage;

  const lowerFileName = file.originalFileName.toLocaleLowerCase();

  const Icon =
    fileCategory === "Images"
      ? FileImage
      : fileCategory === "Videos"
        ? FileVideo
        : fileCategory === "Audio"
          ? FileAudio
          : fileCategory === "Archives"
            ? FileArchive
            : lowerFileName.endsWith(".xlsx") || lowerFileName.endsWith(".csv")
              ? FileSpreadsheet
              : lowerFileName.endsWith(".pptx")
                ? Presentation
                : FileText;

  const cardRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const [contentUrl, setContentUrl] = useState<string>();
  const [previewLoading, setPreviewLoading] = useState(false);
  const loadingRef = useRef<Promise<string> | null>(null);
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const fetchPreview = useCallback(() => {
    if (!loadPreview) return Promise.reject(new Error("Preview is unavailable."));
    if (contentUrl) return Promise.resolve(contentUrl);
    if (loadingRef.current) return loadingRef.current;
    setPreviewLoading(true);
    const request = loadPreview(file)
      .then((url) => {
        if (mountedRef.current) setContentUrl(url);
        else URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        return url;
      })
      .finally(() => {
        loadingRef.current = null;
        if (mountedRef.current) setPreviewLoading(false);
      });
    loadingRef.current = request;
    return request;
  }, [contentUrl, file, loadPreview]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    if (isImage || !loadPreview)
      return () => {
        mountedRef.current = false;
      };
    const element = cardRef.current;
    if (!element)
      return () => {
        mountedRef.current = false;
      };
    if (typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined")
      return () => {
        mountedRef.current = false;
      };
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)) {
          void fetchPreview().catch(() => undefined);
          observer.disconnect();
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: "120px" },
    );
    observer.observe(element);
    return () => {
      observer.disconnect();
      mountedRef.current = false;
    };
  }, [fetchPreview, isImage, loadPreview]);

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      if (contentUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(contentUrl);
    },
    [contentUrl],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focused) {
      cardRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
    }
  }, [focused]);

  const openPreview = async () => {
    if (isImage) {
      onPreview(file, previewUrl);
      return;
    }
    try {
      onPreview(file, await fetchPreview());
    } catch {
      onPreview(file);
    }
  };

  const previewArea =
    isImage && previewUrl ? (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="h-full w-full cursor-zoom-in overflow-hidden border-0 bg-transparent p-0"
        aria-label={`Open full-screen preview of ${file.originalFileName}`}
        onClick={() => onPreview(file, previewUrl)}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") {
            event.preventDefault();
            onPreview(file, previewUrl);
          }
        }}
      >
        <img
          src={previewUrl}
          alt=""
          loading="lazy"
          className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-[transform,filter] duration-[350ms] group-hover:scale-[1.04] group-hover:saturate-[1.03]"
        />
      </button>
    ) : fileCategory && !isImage ? (
      <div className="relative h-full w-full overflow-hidden">
        <PreviewThumbnail file={file} src={contentUrl} />
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void openPreview()}
          disabled={previewLoading}
          aria-label={`Open preview of ${file.originalFileName}`}
          className="absolute inset-0 z-[1] grid cursor-pointer place-items-center border-0 bg-transparent text-white focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-inset focus-visible:outline-[var(--focus-ring)] disabled:cursor-wait"
        >
          {previewLoading && (
            <span className="grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/75 bg-[#153b59]/55 shadow-lg backdrop-blur-sm">
              <span
                className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-white/40 border-t-white"
                aria-label="Loading preview"
              />
            </span>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    ) : (
      <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_25%_20%,rgba(255,255,255,0.5),transparent_40%),linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(225,242,255,0.65),rgba(205,230,248,0.5))]">
        <span className="grid h-[57px] w-[57px] place-items-center rounded-[17px] border border-white/80 bg-white/60 text-[var(--accent)] shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(26,54,80,0.06),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.9)] backdrop-blur-md">
          <Icon aria-hidden="true" size={38} strokeWidth={1.45} />
        </span>
        <span className="rounded-full border border-white/60 bg-white/50 px-[7px] py-1 text-[8px] font-bold tracking-[0.08em] text-[#4d6b82] backdrop-blur-sm">
          {formatFileType(file.contentType)}
        </span>
      </div>
    );

  return (
    <article
      ref={cardRef}
      className={`surface group min-w-0 overflow-visible rounded-[17px] transition-[transform,border-color,box-shadow] duration-200 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.85)] hover:shadow-[var(--glass-shadow-raised)] ${focused ? "!border-[var(--focus-ring)] ring-2 ring-[var(--focus-ring)]/35" : ""}`}
    >
      <div className="relative m-2 h-[136px] min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-[12px] border border-white/60 bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_25%_20%,rgba(255,255,255,0.55),transparent_40%),linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(215,238,255,0.65)_0%,rgba(190,222,248,0.45)_50%,rgba(220,238,252,0.60)_100%)] max-[520px]:h-[150px]">
        {previewArea}
        {onSelectionToggle && (
          <label
            className="absolute left-2 top-2 z-[4] grid h-8 w-8 cursor-pointer place-items-center rounded-lg border border-white/80 bg-white/85 shadow-sm backdrop-blur"
            title={`Select ${file.originalFileName}`}
          >
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              className="h-4 w-4 cursor-pointer accent-[#287dd7]"
              checked={selected}
              disabled={selectionDisabled}
              onChange={onSelectionToggle}
              aria-label={`Select ${file.originalFileName}`}
            />
          </label>
        )}
        {!isImage && fileCategory && (
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-[9px] right-[9px] z-[2] rounded-full border border-white/75 bg-white/70 p-1.5 text-[#3b678a] shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm">
            {fileCategory === "Videos" ? (
              <Play size={13} fill="currentColor" />
            ) : fileCategory === "Audio" ? (
              <Icon size={14} />
            ) : fileCategory === "Archives" ? (
              <Icon size={14} />
            ) : (
              <Icon size={14} />
            )}
          </span>
        )}
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(11,42,68,0.06),transparent_30%,rgba(11,42,68,0.14))]" />

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={
            file.isFavorite
              ? `Remove ${file.originalFileName} from favorites`
              : `Add ${file.originalFileName} to favorites`
          }
          aria-pressed={file.isFavorite}
          title={file.isFavorite ? "Remove from favorites" : "Add to favorites"}
          disabled={favoritePending}
          onClick={onFavoriteToggle}
          className={`absolute right-[9px] top-[9px] z-[2] grid h-[34px] w-[34px] cursor-pointer place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/80 bg-white/60 text-[#526f86] shadow-[0_4px_14px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)] backdrop-blur-md transition-[transform,color,background,border-color] duration-[180ms] hover:enabled:scale-[1.06] hover:enabled:bg-white/85 hover:enabled:text-[#d48410] disabled:opacity-[0.55] ${
            file.isFavorite ? "!text-[#d9850e] bg-white/80" : ""
          }`}
        >
          <Star
            aria-hidden="true"
            size={17}
            strokeWidth={1.8}
            className={file.isFavorite ? "fill-current text-[#e79a1b]" : ""}
          />
        </button>

        <div className="absolute bottom-[9px] left-[9px] z-[2] rounded-full border border-white/35 bg-[rgba(10,36,57,0.30)] px-[8px] py-1 text-[8px] font-bold tracking-[0.06em] text-white shadow-sm backdrop-blur-md">
          {formatFileType(file.contentType)}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="min-w-0 px-[13px] pt-px pb-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h3
                className="m-0 overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[12px] leading-[1.3] font-[650] text-[#193c59]"
                title={file.originalFileName}
              >
                {file.originalFileName}
              </h3>

              {/*
              <p className="mt-[5px] flex items-center gap-[5px] text-[8px] text-[#8295a5]">
                {formatFileType(file.contentType)}

                <span aria-hidden="true">
                  {"\u00B7"}
                </span>

                {formatFileSize(file.fileSize)}
              </p>
              */}
            </div>

            <FileActionMenu
              fileName={file.originalFileName}
              canManage={effectiveCanManage}
              canDownload={effectiveCanManage || file.canDownload}
              onDetails={onDetails}
              onReplace={onReplace}
              onDownload={onDownload}
              onDelete={onDelete}
            />
          </div>

          {/*
          <p className="mt-[10px] mb-0 border-t border-[#ebf1f5] pt-[9px] text-[8px] text-[#9aaab7]">
            Added {formatDate(file.uploadedAt)}
          </p>
          */}
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\components\files\FileActionMenu.tsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Download, Info, MoreHorizontal, Replace, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

interface FileActionMenuProps {
  fileName: string;
  canManage: boolean;
  canDownload: boolean;
  onReplace: () => void;
  onDetails: () => void;
  onDownload: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

interface MenuPosition {
  top: number;
  left: number;
}

const MENU_GAP = 6;
const VIEWPORT_MARGIN = 8;

export function FileActionMenu({
  fileName,
  canManage,
  canDownload,
  onReplace,
  onDetails,
  onDownload,
  onDelete,
}: FileActionMenuProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  const [position, setPosition] = useState<MenuPosition>({
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
  });

  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);

  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  const positionMenu = useCallback(() => {
    if (!triggerRef.current || !menuRef.current) {
      return;
    }

    const triggerRect = triggerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();

    const menuRect = menuRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();

    const maxLeft = Math.max(VIEWPORT_MARGIN, window.innerWidth - menuRect.width - VIEWPORT_MARGIN);
    const maxTop = Math.max(
      VIEWPORT_MARGIN,
      window.innerHeight - menuRect.height - VIEWPORT_MARGIN,
    );
    const belowTop = triggerRect.bottom + MENU_GAP;
    const aboveTop = triggerRect.top - menuRect.height - MENU_GAP;
    const fitsBelow = belowTop <= maxTop;
    const top = fitsBelow ? belowTop : Math.max(VIEWPORT_MARGIN, Math.min(aboveTop, maxTop));
    const left = Math.max(VIEWPORT_MARGIN, Math.min(triggerRect.right - menuRect.width, maxLeft));

    setPosition({
      top,
      left,
    });
  }, []);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return;
    }

    menuRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[role="menuitem"]')?.focus();

    positionMenu();
  }, [open, positionMenu]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      return;
    }

    const handleReposition = () => {
      positionMenu();
    };

    const handlePointerDown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      const target = event.target;

      if (
        target instanceof Node &&
        !triggerRef.current?.contains(target) &&
        !menuRef.current?.contains(target)
      ) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        triggerRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener("resize", handleReposition);

    window.addEventListener("scroll", handleReposition, true);

    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);

    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("resize", handleReposition);

      window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleReposition, true);

      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);

      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [open, positionMenu]);

  const runAction = (action: () => void) => {
    setOpen(false);
    action();
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button
        ref={triggerRef}
        type="button"
        className="grid h-[30px] w-[30px] flex-none place-items-center rounded-[9px] border border-transparent bg-transparent p-0 font-[inherit] text-[#71879c] transition-all duration-[180ms] ease-out hover:border-white/70 hover:bg-white/60 hover:text-[#185fa6] hover:shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(20,54,84,0.06),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.85)] aria-expanded:border-white/80 aria-expanded:bg-white/75 aria-expanded:text-[#145a99] aria-expanded:shadow-[0_2px_10px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)]"
        onClick={() => setOpen((current) => !current)}
        aria-label={`Actions for ${fileName}`}
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        title="File actions"
      >
        <MoreHorizontal aria-hidden="true" size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} />
      </button>

      {open &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            ref={menuRef}
            className="glass-popover z-[1000] w-[148px] max-h-[calc(100dvh-16px)] overflow-y-auto rounded-[12px] p-[6px]"
            role="menu"
            style={{
              position: "fixed",
              top: position.top,
              left: position.left,
            }}
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#4d667c] transition-all duration-[160ms] ease-out hover:bg-white/60 hover:text-[#15599c] hover:shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.8)] focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#5a9bd4]"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={() => runAction(onDetails)}
            >
              <Info aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <span>Details</span>
            </button>

            {canManage && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#4d667c] transition-all duration-[160ms] ease-out hover:bg-white/60 hover:text-[#15599c] hover:shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.8)] focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#5a9bd4]"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={() => runAction(onReplace)}
              >
                <Replace aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />

                <span>Replace</span>
              </button>
            )}

            {canDownload && (
              <button
                type="button"
                title="Download file"
                className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#4d667c] transition-all duration-[160ms] ease-out hover:bg-white/60 hover:text-[#15599c] hover:shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.8)] focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#5a9bd4]"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={() => runAction(onDownload)}
              >
                <Download aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />

                <span>Download</span>
              </button>
            )}

            {canManage && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="flex min-h-[34px] w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-0 bg-transparent px-[9px] text-left text-[10px] font-medium text-[#c9454f] transition-all duration-[160ms] ease-out hover:bg-[rgba(255,235,238,0.7)] hover:text-[#b52a35] hover:shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.8)] focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-[#d85059]"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={() => runAction(onDelete)}
              >
                <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />

                <span>Delete</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}
    </>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\components\files\FileCardGrid.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { FileCard } from "../FileCard";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";

interface FileCardGridProps {
  files: FileResponse[];
  previewUrls: Record<number, string>;
  favoritePendingIds: ReadonlySet<number>;
  canManage?: boolean | ((file: FileResponse) => boolean);
  onFavoriteToggle: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onReplace: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onDetails: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onPreview: (file: FileResponse, previewUrl?: string) => void;
  loadPreview?: (file: FileResponse) => Promise<string>;
  onDownload: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  onDelete: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  empty?: ReactNode;
  selectionMode?: boolean;
  selectedIds?: ReadonlySet<number>;
  onSelectionToggle?: (file: FileResponse) => void;
  selectionEligible?: (file: FileResponse) => boolean;
  selectionDisabled?: boolean;
  focusedFileId?: number | null;
}

export function FileCardGrid({
  files,
  previewUrls,
  favoritePendingIds,
  canManage = false,
  onFavoriteToggle,
  onReplace,
  onDetails,
  onPreview,
  loadPreview,
  onDownload,
  onDelete,
  empty,
  selectionMode = false,
  selectedIds,
  onSelectionToggle,
  selectionEligible,
  selectionDisabled = false,
  focusedFileId = null,
}: FileCardGridProps) {
  if (files.length === 0) {
    return <>{empty}</>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3 max-[1200px]:grid-cols-3 max-[900px]:grid-cols-2 max-[520px]:grid-cols-1">
      {files.map((file) => {
        const effectiveCanManage = typeof canManage === "function" ? canManage(file) : canManage;

        return (
          <FileCard
            key={file.id}
            file={file}
            previewUrl={previewUrls[file.id]}
            loadPreview={loadPreview}
            canManage={effectiveCanManage}
            favoritePending={favoritePendingIds.has(file.id)}
            onFavoriteToggle={() => onFavoriteToggle(file)}
            onReplace={() => onReplace(file)}
            onDetails={() => onDetails(file)}
            onPreview={(selectedFile, url) => onPreview(selectedFile, url)}
            onDownload={() => onDownload(file)}
            onDelete={() => onDelete(file)}
            selected={selectedIds?.has(file.id)}
            onSelectionToggle={
              selectionMode && (selectionEligible?.(file) ?? true)
                ? () => onSelectionToggle?.(file)
                : undefined
            }
            selectionDisabled={selectionDisabled}
            focused={file.id === focusedFileId}
          />
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\components\files\FileDetailsDialog.tsx

import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import { formatDate, formatFileSize, formatFileType } from "@/shared/utils/formatters";

interface FileDetailsDialogProps {
  file: FileResponse;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function FileDetailsDialog({ file, onClose }: FileDetailsDialogProps) {
  const closeButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    closeButtonRef.current?.focus();

    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);

          <dt className="text-[#5a778e]">Added</dt>
          <dd className="m-0 font-semibold text-[#163855]">{formatDate(file.uploadedAt)}</dd>
        </dl>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
