import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { FileWarning, Headphones, LoaderCircle, X } from "lucide-react";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import { getFileCategory, getFileExtension } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import { readFilePreview, type PreviewContent } from "@/features/files/utils/filePreview";

function ContentTable({ rows }: { rows: string[][] }) {
  if (!rows.length) return <p className="p-5 text-sm text-[#7890a3]">No tabular content found.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="h-full overflow-auto">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-left text-xs text-[#38566e]">
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((row, i) => (
            <tr
              key={i}
              className={i === 0 ? "bg-[rgba(235,244,252,0.85)] font-semibold text-[#183a57]" : ""}
            >
              {row.map((cell, j) => (
                <td
                  key={j}
                  className="max-w-[360px] whitespace-pre-wrap border-b border-r border-[#e5edf3] px-3 py-2 align-top"
                >
                  {cell}
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

function SlidePreview({ content }: { content: Extract<PreviewContent, { kind: "slide" }> }) {
  return (
    <div
      className="relative mx-auto aspect-video w-full max-w-[1100px] overflow-hidden rounded-[16px] border border-white/50 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.92)] shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.3)] backdrop-blur-md"
      aria-label="First slide preview"
    >
      {content.shapes.map((shape, index) => (
        <div
          key={index}
          className="absolute overflow-hidden whitespace-pre-wrap break-words px-1 text-[clamp(8px,1.1vw,18px)] leading-[1.15] text-[#233f58]"
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            left: `${(shape.x / content.width) * 100}%`,
            top: `${(shape.y / content.height) * 100}%`,
            width: `${(shape.width / content.width) * 100}%`,
            height: `${(shape.height / content.height) * 100}%`,
          }}
        >
          {shape.text}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function ArchiveListing({
  entries,
}: {
  entries: Extract<PreviewContent, { kind: "archive" }>["entries"];
}) {
  return (
    <div className="max-h-[78vh] w-full max-w-[900px] overflow-auto rounded-[16px] border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.88)] p-5 text-[#24425d] shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.25)] backdrop-blur-xl">
      <h3 className="mb-3 mt-0 text-sm font-semibold">
        Archive contents{" "}
        <span className="font-normal text-[#8295a5]">
          ({entries.length}
          {entries.length === 2000 ? "+" : ""} entries)
        </span>
      </h3>
      <ul className="m-0 list-none p-0">
        {entries.map((entry, i) => {
          const extension =
            entry.name.split("/").pop()?.split(".").pop()?.toLocaleUpperCase() ||
            (entry.directory ? "FOLDER" : "FILE");
          const sizeLabel =
            entry.size === undefined
              ? "Size unavailable"
              : entry.size < 1024
                ? `${entry.size} B`
                : `${(entry.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
          return (
            <li
              key={`${entry.name}-${i}`}
              className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-[rgba(200,220,235,0.5)] py-2 text-xs"
            >
              <span className="min-w-0 truncate">
                <span className="mr-2 rounded border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(235,244,252,0.85)] px-1.5 py-1 text-[9px] font-bold text-[#4a6e8f]">
                  {extension}
                </span>
                {entry.name}
              </span>
              <span className="flex-none text-[#8295a5]">
                {entry.directory ? "Folder" : sizeLabel}
              </span>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

export function FilePreviewModal({
  file,
  src,
  onClose,
}: {
  file: FileResponse;
  src?: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const extension = getFileExtension(file.originalFileName);
  const fileCategory = getFileCategory(file.originalFileName);
  const isImage = fileCategory === "Images";
  const isVideo = fileCategory === "Videos";
  const isAudio = fileCategory === "Audio";
  const isPdf = extension === ".pdf";
  const [content, setContent] = useState<PreviewContent | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [mediaError, setMediaError] = useState(false);
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState<number | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const closeOnEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", closeOnEscape);
  }, [onClose]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setContent(null);
    setError(null);
    setMediaError(false);
    if (!src || isImage || isVideo || isAudio || isPdf)
      return () => {
        active = false;
      };
    void readFilePreview(src, extension)
      .then((result) => {
        if (active) setContent(result);
      })
      .catch((caughtError) => {
        if (active)
          setError(
            caughtError instanceof Error
              ? caughtError.message
              : "This file could not be previewed. It may be damaged or use an unsupported feature.",
          );
      });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [src, extension, isImage, isVideo, isAudio, isPdf]);
  const formatDuration = (seconds: number) =>
    `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}:${String(Math.floor(seconds % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-[rgba(10,24,38,0.76)] p-5 backdrop-blur-[24px] saturate-[180%]"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={`Preview ${file.originalFileName}`}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        className="absolute inset-0 cursor-default border-0 bg-transparent"
        aria-label="Close file preview backdrop"
        onClick={onClose}
      />
      <h2
        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-5 top-5 z-[1] max-w-[75vw] overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-semibold text-white"
        title={file.originalFileName}
      >
        {file.originalFileName}
      </h2>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-label="Close file preview"
        className="absolute right-5 top-4 z-[1] rounded-full border border-white/30 bg-white/15 p-2.5 text-white backdrop-blur-md shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2),inset_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] transition-all hover:bg-white/30 hover:border-white/50 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-white"
      >
        <X aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
      {!src ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-white" role="status">
          <FileWarning aria-hidden="true" size={30} />
          <p>Preview unavailable</p>
          <p>You can download the file instead.</p>
        </div>
      ) : isImage ? (
        <img
          src={src}
          alt={file.originalFileName}
          className="max-h-[88vh] max-w-[95vw] object-contain"
        />
      ) : isVideo ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
          {mediaError ? (
            <p role="status" className="text-sm text-white">
              This video could not be played. The codec may not be supported by this browser; you
              can download the file instead.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <video
              src={src}
              controls
              playsInline
              preload="metadata"
              className="max-h-[84vh] max-w-[95vw] rounded-lg"
              aria-label={`Video preview: ${file.originalFileName}`}
              onError={() => setMediaError(true)}
            >
              Video playback is not supported by this browser.
            </video>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : isAudio ? (
        <div className="flex w-full max-w-[560px] flex-col items-center gap-5 rounded-[24px] border border-white/30 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.18)] px-8 py-10 text-center text-white shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.35),inset_0_1px_1.5px_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] backdrop-blur-2xl">
          <span className="grid h-20 w-20 place-items-center rounded-[24px] bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-md">
            <Headphones aria-hidden="true" size={38} />
          </span>
          <p className="m-0 text-base font-semibold">{file.originalFileName}</p>
          <p className="m-0 text-xs text-white/75">Audio preview</p>
          {mediaError ? (
            <p role="status" className="m-0 text-sm">
              Audio could not be played in this browser.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <audio
              src={src}
              controls
              preload="metadata"
              aria-label={`Audio preview: ${file.originalFileName}`}
              onError={() => setMediaError(true)}
              onLoadedMetadata={(event) => setDuration(event.currentTarget.duration)}
              className="w-full"
            />
          )}
          <p className="m-0 text-xs text-white/70">
            {duration !== null && Number.isFinite(duration)
              ? `Duration ${formatDuration(duration)}`
              : "Duration loads when playback is ready"}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : isPdf ? (
        <iframe
          src={src}
          title={`PDF preview: ${file.originalFileName}`}
          className="h-[84vh] w-[min(1050px,95vw)] rounded-[16px] border border-white/40 bg-white/95 shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.3)]"
        />
      ) : error ? (
        <div
          className="flex max-w-[560px] flex-col items-center gap-3 text-center text-white"
          role="alert"
        >
          <FileWarning aria-hidden="true" size={30} />
          <p>{error}</p>
        </div>
      ) : !content ? (
        <div role="status" className="flex items-center gap-3 text-white">
          <LoaderCircle className="animate-spin" />
          Reading file previewâ€¦
        </div>
      ) : content.kind === "text" ? (
        <pre className="max-h-[84vh] w-[min(1050px,95vw)] overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded-[16px] border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.90)] p-6 text-left text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#1d3d5a] shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.25)] backdrop-blur-xl">
          {content.text || "This file is empty."}
        </pre>
      ) : content.kind === "table" ? (
        <div className="h-[84vh] w-[min(1050px,95vw)] overflow-auto rounded-[16px] border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.90)] p-4 shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.25)] backdrop-blur-xl">
          {content.sheetName && (
            <h3 className="px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#1d3d5a]">{content.sheetName}</h3>
          )}
          <ContentTable rows={content.rows} />
        </div>
      ) : content.kind === "slide" ? (
        <div className="w-[min(1050px,95vw)]">
          <SlidePreview content={content} />
          {content.shapes.length === 0 && (
            <p className="text-center text-white">This slide has no extractable text content.</p>
          )}
          <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs text-white/75">Slide 1 text preview</p>
        </div>
      ) : content.kind === "archive" ? (
        <ArchiveListing entries={content.entries} />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useRef, useState, type DragEvent } from "react";
import { Check, FilePlus2, LoaderCircle, Trash2, Upload, X } from "lucide-react";

import { SUPPORTED_FILE_ACCEPT, validateUploadFile } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import { ApiError } from "@/shared/types/api";
import { formatFileSize } from "@/shared/utils/formatters";

type UploadStatus = "queued" | "uploading" | "success" | "failed" | "invalid";

interface UploadItem {
  key: string;
  file: File;
  status: UploadStatus;
  message?: string;
}

interface MultiFileUploadDialogProps {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  onUpload: (file: File) => Promise<unknown>;
  onComplete: (successCount: number, failureCount: number) => void | Promise<void>;
  onUploadingChange?: (uploading: boolean) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

function getFailureMessage(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 401) {
    return "Your session expired. Sign in again, then retry this file.";
  }
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 403) {
    return "You do not have permission to upload this file here.";
  }
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status >= 400 && error.status < 500) {
    return error.message;
  }
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status >= 500) {
    return "The server could not upload this file. Please try again.";
  }
  return "Upload failed. Check your connection and try again.";
}

function fileKey(file: File): string {
  return `${file.name}:${file.size}:${file.lastModified}`;
}

export function MultiFileUploadDialog({
  title,
  description,
  onUpload,
  onComplete,
  onUploadingChange,
  onClose,
}: MultiFileUploadDialogProps) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<UploadItem[]>([]);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false);

  const addFiles = (selected: FileList | File[]) => {
    const incoming = Array.from(selected);
    if (incoming.length === 0) return;
    setFinished(false);
    setItems((current) => {
      const known = new Set(current.map((item) => item.key));
      const additions = incoming.flatMap((file) => {
        const key = fileKey(file);
        if (known.has(key)) return [];
        known.add(key);
        const validationMessage = validateUploadFile(file);
        return [
          {
            key,
            file,
            status: validationMessage ? ("invalid" as const) : ("queued" as const),
            message: validationMessage,
          },
        ];
      });
      return [...current, ...additions];
    });
  };

  const handleDrop = (event: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(false);
    if (!isUploading) addFiles(event.dataTransfer.files);
  };

  const handleUpload = async () => {
    if (isUploading) return;
    const queued = items.filter((item) => item.status === "queued");
    if (queued.length === 0) return;

    setIsUploading(true);
    onUploadingChange?.(true);
    let successCount = 0;
    let failureCount = items.filter((item) => item.status === "invalid").length;

    try {
      for (const item of queued) {
        setItems((current) =>
          current.map((entry) =>
            entry.key === item.key ? { ...entry, status: "uploading", message: undefined } : entry,
          ),
        );
        try {
          await onUpload(item.file);
          successCount += 1;
          setItems((current) =>
            current.map((entry) =>
              entry.key === item.key ? { ...entry, status: "success", message: undefined } : entry,
            ),
          );
        } catch (error) {
          failureCount += 1;
          setItems((current) =>
            current.map((entry) =>
              entry.key === item.key
                ? { ...entry, status: "failed", message: getFailureMessage(error) }
                : entry,
            ),
          );
        }
      }
      setFinished(true);
      await onComplete(successCount, failureCount);
    } finally {
      setIsUploading(false);
      onUploadingChange?.(false);
    }
  };

  const completedCount = items.filter(
    (item) => item.status === "success" || item.status === "failed" || item.status === "invalid",
  ).length;
  const validCount = items.filter((item) => item.status === "queued").length;
  const successCount = items.filter((item) => item.status === "success").length;
  const failureCount = items.filter(
    (item) => item.status === "failed" || item.status === "invalid",
  ).length;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-[rgba(10,24,38,0.62)] p-4 backdrop-blur-md"
      role="presentation"
    >
      <section
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="multi-upload-title"
        className="surface w-full max-w-[620px] overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] border border-white/80 shadow-[0_24px_80px_rgba(9,30,50,0.32)]"
      >
        <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-white/55 px-5 py-4">
          <div>
            <h2 id="multi-upload-title" className="m-0 text-[17px] font-[700] text-[#183b59]">
              {title}
            </h2>
            <p className="mb-0 mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-[#38566f]">{description}</p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            disabled={isUploading}
            aria-label="Close upload dialog"
            className="grid h-8 w-8 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full border border-white/70 bg-white/55 text-[#58738a] disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </header>

        <div className="max-h-[min(65vh,560px)] overflow-y-auto p-5">
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            type="file"
            multiple
            accept={SUPPORTED_FILE_ACCEPT}
            className="hidden"
            aria-label="Choose files to upload"
            disabled={isUploading || finished}
            onChange={(event) => {
              if (event.currentTarget.files) addFiles(event.currentTarget.files);
              event.currentTarget.value = "";
            }}
          />

          {!finished && (
            <div
              onDragEnter={(event) => {
                event.preventDefault();
                setIsDragging(true);
              }}
              onDragOver={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
              onDragLeave={(event) => {
                if (
                  !(event.relatedTarget instanceof Node) ||
                  !event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget)
                )
                  setIsDragging(false);
              }}
              onDrop={handleDrop}
              className={`rounded-[15px] border border-dashed px-4 py-7 text-center transition-colors ${isDragging ? "border-[#4a9be2] bg-[rgba(222,241,255,0.8)]" : "border-[rgba(111,157,190,0.4)] bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.35)]"}`}
            >
              <span className="mx-auto grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-[14px] border border-white/80 bg-white/65 text-[#2879d1]">
                <FilePlus2 size={21} />
              </span>
              <p className="mb-0 mt-3 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#38566f]">
                Drop files here, or choose files
              </p>
              <p className="mb-3 mt-1 text-[11px] text-[#38566f]">up to 10 MB per file</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={isUploading}
                className="secondary-action min-h-[32px] px-3 text-[10px] disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Choose files
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {items.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <h3 className="m-0 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#38566f]">
                  Selected files ({items.length})
                </h3>
                {!isUploading && !finished && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setItems([])}
                    className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#71879a] hover:text-[#285c84]"
                  >
                    Clear all
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
              <ul className="m-0 grid list-none gap-2 p-0">
                {items.map((item) => (
                  <li
                    key={item.key}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-[11px] border border-white/65 bg-white/45 px-3 py-2"
                  >
                    <span className="grid h-8 w-8 flex-none place-items-center rounded-[9px] bg-[rgba(230,241,250,0.75)] text-[#5283aa]">
                      <FilePlus2 size={15} />
                    </span>
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <strong
                        className="block overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#38566f]"
                        title={item.file.name}
                      >
                        {item.file.name}
                      </strong>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-[#8296a6]">
                        {formatFileSize(item.file.size)}
                        {item.message ? ` Â· ${item.message}` : ""}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`flex-none text-[9px] font-semibold ${item.status === "success" ? "text-[#34734b]" : item.status === "failed" || item.status === "invalid" ? "text-[#b2454d]" : "text-[#54738c]"}`}
                      role={
                        item.status === "failed" || item.status === "invalid" ? "status" : undefined
                      }
                    >
                      {item.status === "queued" ? (
                        "Ready"
                      ) : item.status === "uploading" ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                          <LoaderCircle size={12} className="animate-spin" />
                          Uploading
                        </span>
                      ) : item.status === "success" ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Check size={12} />
                          Uploaded
                        </span>
                      ) : item.status === "invalid" ? (
                        "Invalid"
                      ) : (
                        "Failed"
                      )}
                    </span>
                    {!isUploading && !finished && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          setItems((current) => current.filter((entry) => entry.key !== item.key))
                        }
                        aria-label={`Remove ${item.file.name}`}
                        className="grid h-7 w-7 flex-none place-items-center rounded-[8px] text-[#9b6870] hover:bg-[#fff0f1]"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={13} />
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}

          {isUploading && (
            <div className="mt-4" role="status" aria-live="polite">
              <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between text-[9px] font-semibold text-[#54738c]">
                <span>Uploading files</span>
                <span>
                  {completedCount} of {items.length}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div
                role="progressbar"
                aria-label="Files uploaded"
                aria-valuemin={0}
                aria-valuemax={items.length}
                aria-valuenow={completedCount}
                className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[rgba(90,135,170,0.15)]"
              >
                <div
                  className="h-full rounded-full bg-[#438bd0] transition-[width] duration-300"
                  style={{ width: `${items.length ? (completedCount / items.length) * 100 : 0}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <p className="mb-0 mt-1 text-[9px] text-[#8296a6]">
                Each file uploads separately so other files can continue if one fails.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {finished && (
            <p
              role="status"
              className="glass-alert-success mt-4 rounded-[11px] px-3 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium"
            >
              Uploads finished: {successCount} successful, {failureCount} failed.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <footer className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2 border-t border-white/55 px-5 py-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            disabled={isUploading}
            className="secondary-action min-h-[36px] px-4 text-[10px] disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {finished ? "Close" : "Cancel"}
          </button>
          {!finished && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void handleUpload()}
              disabled={isUploading || validCount === 0}
              className="primary-action min-h-[36px] px-4 text-[10px] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-55"
            >
              <Upload size={14} />
              {isUploading
                ? "Uploading..."
                : `Upload ${validCount} file${validCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}`}
            </button>
          )}
        </footer>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  Archive,
  FileText,
  FileWarning,
  Headphones,
  LoaderCircle,
  Play,
  Presentation,
  Table2,
} from "lucide-react";
import type * as PDFJs from "pdfjs-dist";
import { getFileCategory, getFileExtension } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import { readFilePreview, type PreviewContent } from "@/features/files/utils/filePreview";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";

function PdfThumbnail({ src }: { src: string }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    let task: PDFJs.PDFDocumentLoadingTask | undefined;
    let renderTask: PDFJs.RenderTask | undefined;
    void Promise.all([import("pdfjs-dist"), import("pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.min.mjs?url")])
      .then(async ([pdfjs, worker]) => {
        pdfjs.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = worker.default;
        const response = await fetch(src);
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Preview content could not be read.");
        const data = await response.arrayBuffer();
        if (cancelled) return;
        task = pdfjs.getDocument({ data });
        const document = await task.promise;
        if (cancelled) return;
        const page = await document.getPage(1);
        const canvas = canvasRef.current;
        if (!canvas) return;
        const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 0.45 });
        canvas.width = viewport.width;
        canvas.height = viewport.height;
        renderTask = page.render({
          canvas,
          canvasContext: canvas.getContext("2d")!,
          viewport,
        });
        await renderTask.promise;
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setFailed(true);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      renderTask?.cancel();
      void task?.destroy();
    };
  }, [src]);
  return failed ? (
    <PreviewFallback icon={<FileWarning size={31} />} label="PDF preview unavailable" />
  ) : (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      aria-label="First page of PDF"
      className="h-full w-full bg-white object-contain"
    />
  );
}

function PreviewFallback({ icon, label }: { icon: React.ReactNode; label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(235,244,252,0.65),rgba(225,236,246,0.50))] text-[#467498] backdrop-blur-md">
      {icon}
      <span className="max-w-[90%] truncate text-[9px] font-semibold">{label}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PreviewThumbnail({ file, src }: { file: FileResponse; src?: string }) {
  const extension = getFileExtension(file.originalFileName);
  const fileCategory = getFileCategory(file.originalFileName);
  const hasRichPreview =
    (fileCategory === "Documents" && extension !== ".pdf") || fileCategory === "Archives";
  const [content, setContent] = useState<PreviewContent | null>(null);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setContent(null);
    setFailed(false);
    if (!src || !hasRichPreview)
      return () => {
        active = false;
      };
    void readFilePreview(src, extension)
      .then((value) => {
        if (active) setContent(value);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) setFailed(true);
      });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [src, extension, hasRichPreview]);

  if (extension === ".pdf" && src) return <PdfThumbnail src={src} />;
  if (fileCategory === "Videos")
    return failed ? (
      <PreviewFallback icon={<FileWarning size={31} />} label="Video thumbnail unavailable" />
    ) : src ? (
      <video
        src={src}
        muted
        playsInline
        preload="auto"
        aria-label={`Video frame preview of ${file.originalFileName}`}
        className="h-full w-full bg-[#172b3d] object-cover"
        onError={() => setFailed(true)}
      />
    ) : (
      <PreviewFallback icon={<Play size={31} />} label="Video" />
    );
  if (fileCategory === "Audio")
    return <PreviewFallback icon={<Headphones size={35} />} label="Audio preview" />;
  if (failed)
    return <PreviewFallback icon={<FileWarning size={31} />} label="Preview unavailable" />;
  if (!src)
    return (
      <PreviewFallback
        icon={<FileText size={31} />}
        label={`${file.contentType.split("/").pop()?.toLocaleUpperCase() ?? "File"} preview`}
      />
    );
  if (!content)
    return (
      <PreviewFallback
        icon={<LoaderCircle className="animate-spin" size={28} />}
        label="Loading previewâ€¦"
      />
    );
  if (content.kind === "text")
    return (
      <div className="h-full w-full overflow-hidden bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.75)] p-3 text-left backdrop-blur-md">
        <pre className="m-0 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-[8px] leading-[1.45] text-[#536b7e]">
          {content.text.slice(0, 1800)}
        </pre>
      </div>
    );
  if (content.kind === "table")
    return (
      <div className="h-full w-full overflow-hidden bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.75)] p-2 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded border border-white/60 bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.6)]">
          <table className="w-full border-collapse text-left text-[7px] text-[#536b7e]">
            <tbody>
              {content.rows.slice(0, 8).map((row, i) => (
                <tr
                  key={i}
                  className={
                    i === 0 ? "bg-[rgba(235,244,252,0.85)] font-semibold text-[#183a57]" : ""
                  }
                >
                  {row.slice(0, 5).map((cell, j) => (
                    <td
                      key={j}
                      className="max-w-[80px] truncate border-b border-r border-[#e8eef3] px-1 py-[3px]"
                    >
                      {cell}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  if (content.kind === "slide")
    return (
      <div
        className="relative h-full w-full overflow-hidden bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.85)] shadow-inner backdrop-blur-md"
        aria-label="First PowerPoint slide text preview"
      >
        {content.shapes.slice(0, 15).map((shape, index) => (
          <span
            key={index}
            className="absolute overflow-hidden text-ellipsis text-[6px] leading-tight text-[#28445b]"
            style={{
              left: `${(shape.x / content.width) * 100}%`,
              top: `${(shape.y / content.height) * 100}%`,
              width: `${(shape.width / content.width) * 100}%`,
              height: `${(shape.height / content.height) * 100}%`,
            }}
          >
            {shape.text}
          </span>
        ))}
        {content.shapes.length === 0 && (
          <PreviewFallback icon={<Presentation size={31} />} label="Slide text unavailable" />
        )}
      </div>
    );
  if (content.kind === "archive")
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(255,248,238,0.75),rgba(250,238,222,0.65))] text-[#a66a23] backdrop-blur-md">
        <Archive size={36} strokeWidth={1.5} />
        <span className="rounded-full border border-white/70 bg-white/80 px-2 py-1 text-[8px] font-bold shadow-sm">
          {content.entries.length} entries
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  return <PreviewFallback icon={<Table2 size={31} />} label="File preview" />;
}

export const MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024;

export const FILE_EXTENSIONS = {
  Images: [".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"],
  Documents: [".pdf", ".txt", ".csv", ".docx", ".xlsx", ".pptx"],
  Videos: [".mp4", ".mov", ".webm"],
  Audio: [".mp3", ".wav", ".m4a"],
  Archives: [".zip"],
} as const;

export type FileCategory = keyof typeof FILE_EXTENSIONS;
export type FileCategoryFilter = "All" | FileCategory;

export const FILE_CATEGORY_FILTERS: FileCategoryFilter[] = [
  "All",
  "Images",
  "Documents",
  "Videos",
  "Audio",
  "Archives",
];

export const SUPPORTED_FILE_ACCEPT = Object.values(FILE_EXTENSIONS).flat().join(",");

export function getFileExtension(fileName: string): string {
  const lastDot = fileName.lastIndexOf(".");
  return lastDot >= 0 ? fileName.slice(lastDot).toLocaleLowerCase() : "";
}

export function getFileCategory(fileName: string): FileCategory | undefined {
  const extension = getFileExtension(fileName);

  return (Object.entries(FILE_EXTENSIONS) as [FileCategory, readonly string[]][]).find(
    ([, extensions]) => extensions.includes(extension),
  )?.[0];
}

export function validateUploadFile(file: File): string | undefined {
  if (!getFileCategory(file.name)) {
    return "Unsupported file format.";
  }

  if (file.size <= 0) {
    return "The selected file is empty.";
  }

  if (file.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES) {
    return "Files must be 10 MB or smaller.";
  }

  return undefined;
}

export function isPreviewableFile(fileName: string): boolean {
  return getFileCategory(fileName) !== undefined;
}

export function isImagePreviewableFile(fileName: string): boolean {
  return FILE_EXTENSIONS.Images.includes(
    getFileExtension(fileName) as (typeof FILE_EXTENSIONS.Images)[number],
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\hooks\fileQueries.ts

import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { addFavorite, getAdminFiles, getFavorites, getFiles, removeFavorite } from "@/features/files/api/filesApi";
import { queryClient } from "@/shared/api/queryClient";

export const fileKeys = {
  all: ["files"] as const,
  lists: () => [...fileKeys.all, "list"] as const,
  list: (accountId: string | null, view: string, page: number, pageSize: number, search: string, category?: string, adminAll = false) =>
    [...fileKeys.lists(), accountId, view, page, pageSize, search, category ?? "", adminAll] as const,
  details: () => [...fileKeys.all, "detail"] as const,
  detail: (id: number) => [...fileKeys.details(), id] as const,
};

interface FileQueryContext { instance: IPublicClientApplication; account: AccountInfo | null; accountKey: string | null }

export function useFileListQuery(context: FileQueryContext & { view: "all" | "favorites"; page: number; pageSize: number; search?: string; category?: string; adminAll?: boolean }) {
  const { instance, account, accountKey, view, page, pageSize, search, category, adminAll } = context;
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: fileKeys.list(accountKey, view, page, pageSize, search ?? "", category, adminAll),
    enabled: !!account,
    queryFn: async ({ signal }) => {
      if (!account) throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      if (view === "favorites") return getFavorites(instance, account, page, pageSize, search, category, signal);
      if (adminAll) return getAdminFiles(instance, account, page, pageSize, search, category, signal);
      return getFiles(instance, account, page, pageSize, search, category, signal);
    },
  });
}

export function useToggleFavoriteMutation(context: FileQueryContext) {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ fileId, favorite }: { fileId: number; favorite: boolean }) => {
      if (!context.account) return Promise.reject(new Error("An authenticated account is required."));
      return favorite ? addFavorite(context.instance, context.account, fileId) : removeFavorite(context.instance, context.account, fileId);
    },
    onMutate: async ({ fileId, favorite }) => {
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: fileKeys.lists() });
      const previous = queryClient.getQueriesData({ queryKey: fileKeys.lists() });
      queryClient.setQueriesData({ queryKey: fileKeys.lists() }, (data: unknown) => {
        if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !("items" in data) || !Array.isArray(data.items)) return data;
        return { ...data, items: data.items.map((item: unknown) => item && typeof item === "object" && "id" in item && item.id === fileId ? { ...item, isFavorite: favorite } : item) };
      });
      return { previous };
    },
    onError: (_error, _variables, context) => context?.previous.forEach(([key, data]) => queryClient.setQueryData(key, data)),
    onSettled: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: fileKeys.lists() }),
  }, queryClient);
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\hooks\useFileActions.ts
============================================================

import type { ChangeEvent, RefObject } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import {
  deleteFile,
  downloadFileByPath,
  getFile,
  replaceFile,
} from "@/features/files/api/filesApi";
import { validateUploadFile } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import type { FileResponse, FileView } from "@/features/files/types/file";

interface UseFileActionsOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  view: FileView;
  loadFiles: (view: FileView) => Promise<void>;
  removeSelected: (ids: number[]) => void;
  replaceInputRef: RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>;
  replaceTargetIdRef: RefObject<number | null>;
  setError: (message: string | null) => void;
  setSuccessMessage: (message: string | null) => void;
  setFileDetails: (file: FileResponse | null) => void;
}

export function useFileActions({
  instance,
  account,
  view,
  loadFiles,
  removeSelected,
  replaceInputRef,
  replaceTargetIdRef,
  setError,
  setSuccessMessage,
  setFileDetails,
}: UseFileActionsOptions) {
  const handleReplace = async (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): Promise<void> => {
    const selectedFile = event.target.files?.[0];
    const fileId = replaceTargetIdRef.current;

    if (!selectedFile || !account || fileId === null) return;

    const validationError = validateUploadFile(selectedFile);
    if (validationError) {
      setError(validationError);
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    try {
      setError(null);
      setSuccessMessage(null);
      await replaceFile(instance, account, fileId, selectedFile);
      setSuccessMessage(`${selectedFile.name} replaced successfully.`);
      await loadFiles(view);
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Replace failed.");
    } finally {
      replaceTargetIdRef.current = null;
      event.target.value = "";
    }
  };

  const handleDownload = async (file: FileResponse): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account) return;

    try {
      setError(null);
      await downloadFileByPath(instance, account, file.downloadUrl, file.originalFileName);
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Download failed.");
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (fileId: number): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account) return;

    try {
      setError(null);
      const result = await deleteFile(instance, account, fileId);
      removeSelected([fileId]);
      if (result?.storageCleanupComplete === false) {
        setSuccessMessage(
          "The file record was deleted, but its stored content needs cleanup reconciliation.",
        );
      }
      await loadFiles(view);
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(
        caughtError instanceof Error
          ? `${caughtError.message} The file remains listed, so you can retry after resolving the issue.`
          : "Delete failed. You can safely retry.",
      );
    }
  };

  const handleShowFileDetails = async (file: FileResponse): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account) return;

    try {
      setError(null);
      setFileDetails(await getFile(instance, account, file.id));
    } catch (caughtError) {
      setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to load file details.");
    }
  };

  const openReplace = (file: FileResponse): void => {
    replaceTargetIdRef.current = file.id;
    if (replaceInputRef.current) {
      replaceInputRef.current.value = "";
      replaceInputRef.current.click();
    }
  };

  return { handleReplace, handleDownload, handleDelete, handleShowFileDetails, openReplace };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\hooks\useFileBulkActions.ts
============================================================

import { useState } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { deleteFile, removeFavorite } from "@/features/files/api/filesApi";
import type { FileResponse, FileView } from "@/features/files/types/file";

export type BulkAction = "delete" | "unfavorite" | null;

interface UseFileBulkActionsOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  selectedFiles: FileResponse[];
  bulkAction: BulkAction;
  view: FileView;
  currentPage: number;
  loadFiles: (view: FileView, page?: number) => Promise<void>;
  removeSelected: (ids: number[]) => void;
  cancelSelection: () => void;
  setError: (message: string | null) => void;
  setSuccessMessage: (message: string | null) => void;
  clearBulkAction: () => void;
}

export function useFileBulkActions({
  instance,
  account,
  selectedFiles,
  bulkAction,
  view,
  currentPage,
  loadFiles,
  removeSelected,
  cancelSelection,
  setError,
  setSuccessMessage,
  clearBulkAction,
}: UseFileBulkActionsOptions) {
  const [bulkPending, setBulkPending] = useState(false);

  const runBulkAction = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (!account || bulkPending || !selectedFiles.length || !bulkAction) return;

    setBulkPending(true);
    setError(null);
    const results = await Promise.all(
      selectedFiles.map(async (file) => {
        try {
          const deletion =
            bulkAction === "unfavorite" ? undefined : await deleteFile(instance, account, file.id);
          if (bulkAction === "unfavorite") await removeFavorite(instance, account, file.id);
          return {
            id: file.id,
            ok: true as const,
            cleanupComplete: deletion?.storageCleanupComplete !== false,
          };
        } catch (failure) {
          return {
            id: file.id,
            ok: false as const,
            message: failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : "Request failed.",
          };
        }
      }),
    );
    const succeeded = results.filter((result) => result.ok).map((result) => result.id);
    const failed = results.filter((result) => !result.ok);
    const cleanupWarnings = results.filter((result) => result.ok && !result.cleanupComplete).length;
    const failedNames = failed
      .map((result) => selectedFiles.find((file) => file.id === result.id)?.originalFileName)
      .filter(Boolean);
    removeSelected(succeeded);
    const failureSummary = failed.length
      ? `${succeeded.length} completed; ${failed.length} failed (${failedNames.join(", ")}). Failed files that remain available are selected for review or retry.`
      : null;
    const cleanupNames = results
      .filter((result) => result.ok && !result.cleanupComplete)
      .map((result) => selectedFiles.find((file) => file.id === result.id)?.originalFileName)
      .filter(Boolean);
    setSuccessMessage(
      succeeded.length
        ? `${succeeded.length} ${bulkAction === "unfavorite" ? `file${succeeded.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} removed from Favorites` : `file${succeeded.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} permanently deleted`}.${cleanupWarnings ? ` Storage cleanup needs reconciliation for: ${cleanupNames.join(", ")}. The file records are already removed.` : ""}`
        : null,
    );
    clearBulkAction();
    if (!failed.length) cancelSelection();
    await loadFiles(view, currentPage);
    if (failureSummary) setError(failureSummary);
    setBulkPending(false);
  };

  return { bulkPending, runBulkAction };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\hooks\useFileFavorites.ts
============================================================

import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";

import { useToggleFavoriteMutation } from "@/features/files/hooks/fileQueries";
import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";

interface UseFileFavoritesOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  accountKey?: string | null;
  onSuccess: (file: FileResponse, nextFavorite: boolean) => void;
  onError: (message: string) => void;
}

export function useFileFavorites({
  instance,
  account,
  accountKey = account?.homeAccountId ?? null,
  onSuccess,
  onError,
}: UseFileFavoritesOptions) {
  const [pendingIds, setPendingIds] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const favoriteMutation = useToggleFavoriteMutation({ instance, account, accountKey });

  const setPending = useCallback((fileId: number, pending: boolean) => {
    setPendingIds((previous) => {
      const next = new Set(previous);

      if (pending) {
        next.add(fileId);
      } else {
        next.delete(fileId);
      }

      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const toggleFavorite = useCallback(
    async (file: FileResponse) => {
      if (!account || pendingIds.has(file.id)) {
        return;
      }

      const nextFavorite = !file.isFavorite;

      setPending(file.id, true);

      try {
        await favoriteMutation.mutateAsync({ fileId: file.id, favorite: nextFavorite });

        onSuccess(file, nextFavorite);
      } catch (caughtError) {
        onError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to update favorite.");
      } finally {
        setPending(file.id, false);
      }
    },
    [account, favoriteMutation, onError, onSuccess, pendingIds, setPending],
  );

  return {
    pendingIds,
    toggleFavorite,
  };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\hooks\useFilePreviews.ts

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

import type { FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import { isImagePreviewableFile } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";

export type FilePreviewLoader<TFile extends FileResponse = FileResponse> = (
  file: TFile,
) => Promise<string>;

interface UseFilePreviewsResult {
  previewUrls: Record<number, string>;
  loading: boolean;
}

export function useFilePreviews<TFile extends FileResponse>(
  files: TFile[],
  loadPreview: FilePreviewLoader<TFile>,
): UseFilePreviewsResult {
  const [previewUrls, setPreviewUrls] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const cache = useRef(new Map<number, { signature: string; url: string }>());
  const pending = useRef(new Map<string, Promise<string>>());
  const currentSignatures = useRef(new Map<number, string>());

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    const imageFiles = files.filter(
      (file) =>
        file.contentType.startsWith("image/") && isImagePreviewableFile(file.originalFileName),
    );

    const signatureFor = (file: FileResponse) =>
      `${file.contentType}:${file.fileSize}:${file.uploadedAt}`;
    currentSignatures.current = new Map(imageFiles.map((file) => [file.id, signatureFor(file)]));
    cache.current.forEach((entry, id) => {
      const currentFile = imageFiles.find((file) => file.id === id);
      if (!currentFile || entry.signature !== signatureFor(currentFile)) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url);
        cache.current.delete(id);
      }
    });
    setPreviewUrls(
      Object.fromEntries(
        imageFiles.flatMap((file) => {
          const entry = cache.current.get(file.id);
          return entry?.signature === signatureFor(file) ? [[file.id, entry.url]] : [];
        }),
      ),
    );
    setLoading(imageFiles.length > 0);

    if (imageFiles.length === 0) {
      return () => {
        active = false;
      };
    }

    const load = async () => {
      const results = await Promise.all(
        imageFiles.map(async (file) => {
          try {
            const signature = signatureFor(file);
            const pendingKey = `${file.id}:${signature}`;
            const cached = cache.current.get(file.id);
            let url: string;
            if (cached?.signature === signature) {
              url = cached.url;
            } else {
              let request = pending.current.get(pendingKey);
              if (!request) {
                request = loadPreview(file);
                pending.current.set(pendingKey, request);
              }
              url = await request;
            }
            const isCurrent = active && currentSignatures.current.get(file.id) === signature;
            if (!isCurrent) {
              // Requests shared with a newer effect may still be consumed there.
              if (currentSignatures.current.get(file.id) !== signature) {
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
              }
              return null;
            }

            const previous = cache.current.get(file.id);
            if (previous && previous.url !== url && previous.signature !== signature) {
              URL.revokeObjectURL(previous.url);
            }
            cache.current.set(file.id, { signature, url });

            return [file.id, url] as const;
          } catch {
            return null;
          } finally {
            pending.current.delete(`${file.id}:${signatureFor(file)}`);
          }
        }),
      );

      if (!active) {
        return;
      }

      setPreviewUrls(
        Object.fromEntries(
          results.filter((entry): entry is readonly [number, string] => entry !== null),
        ),
      );
      setLoading(false);
    };

    void load();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [files, loadPreview]);

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      currentSignatures.current.clear();
      cache.current.forEach((entry) => URL.revokeObjectURL(entry.url));
      cache.current.clear();
    },
    [],
  );

  return {
    previewUrls,
    loading,
  };
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\features\files\hooks\useFilesData.ts
============================================================

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { Dispatch, SetStateAction } from "react";
import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { getAdminFiles, getFavorites, getFiles } from "@/features/files/api/filesApi";
import type { FileCategoryFilter } from "@/features/files/constants/fileSupport";
import type { AdminFileResponse, FileResponse, FileView } from "@/features/files/types/file";
import type { PagedResponse } from "@/shared/types/api";

const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

interface UseFilesDataOptions {
  instance: IPublicClientApplication;
  account: AccountInfo | null;
  view: FileView;
  currentPage: number;
  searchQuery: string;
  categoryFilter: FileCategoryFilter;
  isAdminAllContent: boolean;
  routeFileId: number | null;
  retain: (ids: number[]) => void;
  setFocusedFileId: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number | null>>;
  setCurrentPage: Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>;
}

export function useFilesData({
  instance,
  account,
  view,
  currentPage,
  searchQuery,
  categoryFilter,
  isAdminAllContent,
  routeFileId,
  retain,
  setFocusedFileId,
  setCurrentPage,
}: UseFilesDataOptions) {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<FileResponse[]>([]);
  const [totalItems, setTotalItems] = useState(0);
  const [pageCount, setPageCount] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [hasLoaded, setHasLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const loadRequestVersion = useRef(0);
  const focusedFileResolved = useRef(false);

  const fetchAdminPage = useCallback(
    async (page: number): Promise<PagedResponse<FileResponse>> => {
      if (!account) throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      const result = await getAdminFiles(
        instance,
        account,
        page,
        PAGE_SIZE,
        searchQuery,
        categoryFilter,
      );
      return {
        ...result,
        items: result.items.map((file: AdminFileResponse): FileResponse => ({
          ...file,
          url: "",
          downloadUrl:
            file.albumId === null
              ? `/api/files/${file.id}/download`
              : `/api/albums/${file.albumId}/files/${file.id}/download`,
          isFavorite: false,
          canManage: true,
          canDownload: true,
        })),
      };
    },
    [account, categoryFilter, instance, searchQuery],
  );

  const loadFiles = useCallback(
    async (
      nextView: FileView = view,
      requestedPage: number = currentPage,
      requestedCategory: FileCategoryFilter = categoryFilter,
    ): Promise<void> => {
      const requestVersion = ++loadRequestVersion.current;
      if (!account) {
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        const nextPage =
          nextView === "favorites"
            ? await getFavorites(
                instance,
                account,
                requestedPage,
                PAGE_SIZE,
                searchQuery,
                requestedCategory,
              )
            : isAdminAllContent
              ? await fetchAdminPage(requestedPage)
              : await getFiles(
                  instance,
                  account,
                  requestedPage,
                  PAGE_SIZE,
                  searchQuery,
                  requestedCategory,
                );

        if (
          nextView === "all" &&
          routeFileId &&
          !focusedFileResolved.current &&
          requestedPage === 1
        ) {
          let targetPage = nextPage;
          const fetchTargetPage = (page: number) =>
            isAdminAllContent
              ? fetchAdminPage(page)
              : getFiles(instance, account, page, PAGE_SIZE, searchQuery, requestedCategory);
          while (
            !targetPage.items.some((file) => file.id === routeFileId) &&
            targetPage.page < targetPage.pageCount
          ) {
            targetPage = await fetchTargetPage(targetPage.page + 1);
          }
          if (targetPage.items.some((file) => file.id === routeFileId)) {
            nextPage.items = targetPage.items;
            nextPage.page = targetPage.page;
            setFocusedFileId(routeFileId);
          }
          focusedFileResolved.current = true;
        }

        if (requestVersion !== loadRequestVersion.current) return;
        setFiles(nextPage.items);
        retain(
          nextPage.items
            .filter((file) => nextView === "favorites" || file.canManage)
            .map((file) => file.id),
        );
        setTotalItems(nextPage.totalItems);
        setPageCount(nextPage.pageCount);
        setCurrentPage(nextPage.page);
      } catch (caughtError) {
        if (requestVersion !== loadRequestVersion.current) return;
        setFiles([]);
        retain([]);
        setTotalItems(0);
        setPageCount(1);
        setError(caughtError instanceof Error ? caughtError.message : "Unable to load files.");
      } finally {
        if (requestVersion === loadRequestVersion.current) {
          setLoading(false);
          setHasLoaded(true);
        }
      }
    },
    [
      account,
      categoryFilter,
      currentPage,
      fetchAdminPage,
      instance,
      isAdminAllContent,
      retain,
      routeFileId,
      searchQuery,
      setCurrentPage,
      setFocusedFileId,
      view,
    ],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadFiles(view, currentPage);
    return () => {
      loadRequestVersion.current += 1;
    };
  }, [loadFiles, view, currentPage]);

  return { files, setFiles, totalItems, pageCount, loading, hasLoaded, error, setError, loadFiles };
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { FilePlus2, FolderOpen, ListChecks, Search, Star, Upload } from "lucide-react";
import { useMsal } from "@azure/msal-react";

import { uploadFile } from "../api/filesApi";

const fileToolbarClass =
  "flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2 max-[960px]:w-full max-[960px]:justify-start max-[760px]:grid max-[760px]:grid-cols-1";

export function FilesPage() {
  const { instance } = useMsal();
  const { account, user } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  const routeParams = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const isAdminAllContent =
    user?.role === "Admin" &&
    (routeParams.get("scope") === "all" || getAdminContentScope() === "all");
  const routeFileId = Number(routeParams.get("fileId")) || null;
  const accountKey = getAccountKey(account);

  // Hidden replacement input opens the native file picker from a file card.
  const replaceInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  // Keep the replacement target while the native file picker is open.
  const replaceTargetIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const [view, setView] = useState<FileView>("all");
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [showUploadDialog, setShowUploadDialog] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState(() => routeParams.get("search") ?? "");
  const [focusedFileId, setFocusedFileId] = useState<number | null>(routeFileId);
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<FileCategoryFilter>("All");
  const [fileDetails, setFileDetails] = useState<FileResponse | null>(null);
  const [previewFile, setPreviewFile] = useState<FileResponse | null>(null);
  const [previewSource, setPreviewSource] = useState<string | undefined>();
  const {
    selectionMode,
    selectedIds,
    startSelection,
    cancelSelection,
    toggle,
    selectAll,
    remove,
    retain,
  } = useBulkSelection<number>();
  const [bulkAction, setBulkAction] = useState<BulkAction>(null);

  const { files, setFiles, totalItems, pageCount, loading, hasLoaded, error, setError, loadFiles } =
    useFilesData({
      instance,
      account,
      view,
      currentPage,
      searchQuery,
      categoryFilter,
      isAdminAllContent,
      routeFileId,
      retain,
      setFocusedFileId,
      setCurrentPage,
    });

  const loadPreview = useCallback(
    async (file: FileResponse) => {
      if (!account) {
        throw new Error("An authenticated account is required.");
      }

      // Shared album viewers use the preview route instead of the download route.
      const previewPath =
        file.downloadUrl.startsWith("/api/albums/") && !file.canManage
          ? file.downloadUrl.replace(/\/download$/, "/preview")
          : file.downloadUrl;

      return getFilePreviewUrlByPath(instance, account, previewPath, file.contentType);
    },
    // Keep preview references stable across MSAL object refreshes for the same identity.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [accountKey, instance],
  );

  const { previewUrls } = useFilePreviews(files, loadPreview);

  useEffect(() => {
    cancelSelection();
  }, [accountKey, cancelSelection]);

  const selectableFiles = view === "favorites" ? files : files.filter((file) => file.canManage);
  const selectedFiles = selectableFiles.filter((file) => selectedIds.has(file.id));
  const allSelectedFilesManageable =
    selectedFiles.length > 0 && selectedFiles.every((file) => file.canManage);

  const { handleReplace, handleDownload, handleDelete, handleShowFileDetails, openReplace } =
    useFileActions({
      instance,
      account,
      view,
      loadFiles,
      removeSelected: remove,
      replaceInputRef,
      replaceTargetIdRef,
      setError,
      setSuccessMessage,
      setFileDetails,
    });

  const { bulkPending, runBulkAction } = useFileBulkActions({
    instance,
    account,
    selectedFiles,
    bulkAction,
    view,
    currentPage,
    loadFiles,
    removeSelected: remove,
    cancelSelection,
    setError,
    setSuccessMessage,
    clearBulkAction: () => setBulkAction(null),
  });

  const handleFavoriteSuccess = useCallback(
    (file: FileResponse, nextFavorite: boolean) => {
      setFiles((previous) => {
        // Unfavoriting a file removes it immediately from the Favorites view.
        if (view === "favorites" && !nextFavorite) {
          return previous.filter((item) => item.id !== file.id);
        }

        return previous.map((item) =>
          item.id === file.id
            ? {
                ...item,
                isFavorite: nextFavorite,
              }
            : item,
        );
      });

      if (view === "favorites" && !nextFavorite) {
        remove([file.id]);
        // Refresh the list and pagination totals after removing the favorite.
        void loadFiles(view, currentPage);
      }
    },
    [view, currentPage, remove, loadFiles, setFiles],
  );

  const handleFavoriteError = useCallback((message: string) => setError(message), [setError]);

  const { pendingIds: favoritePendingIds, toggleFavorite } = useFileFavorites({
    instance,
    account,
    onSuccess: handleFavoriteSuccess,
    onError: handleFavoriteError,
  });

  // Show skeletons only during the initial load.
  if (loading && !hasLoaded) {
    return (
      <div className="app-page">
        <section className={pageHeaderSkeletonClass}>
          <div className="skeleton-content">
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="skeleton-field skeleton-field-lg" />
            <span className="skeleton-field" />
            <span className="skeleton-button skeleton-button-wide" />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3 max-[1200px]:grid-cols-3 max-[900px]:grid-cols-2 max-[520px]:grid-cols-1">
          {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((item) => (
            <div key={item} className="surface overflow-hidden rounded-[17px]">
              <div className="relative mx-[8px] mt-[8px] h-[136px] w-[calc(100%-16px)] overflow-hidden rounded-[11px] bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,#e3edf5,#d7e5ef)] after:absolute after:top-0 after:bottom-0 after:w-[35%] after:translate-x-[-130%] after:bg-[linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,rgba(255,255,255,0.56),transparent)] after:animate-[skeleton-shimmer_1.55s_infinite_ease-in-out] after:content-['']" />

              <div className="grid gap-1 p-4">
                <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-md" />
                <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-sm" />
                <span className="skeleton-line skeleton-line-xs" />
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-page">
      <section className={pageHeaderClass}>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h1 className="page-heading flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="page-kicker-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
            {view === "favorites" ? "Favorites" : isAdminAllContent ? "All Files" : "Files"}
          </h1>

          <p className="page-description">
            {isAdminAllContent
              ? "Browse and manage files across all users."
              : "Browse, search, and manage your files from one place."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={fileToolbarClass}>
          {!isAdminAllContent && (
            <div className={viewSwitcherClass} role="group" aria-label="File view">
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={view === "all"}
                onClick={() => {
                  cancelSelection();
                  setView("all");
                  setCurrentPage(1);
                }}
                className={`${viewButtonClass} ${view === "all" ? "border-white/80 bg-white/70 text-[#145a99] shadow-[0_3px_12px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)]" : "border-transparent bg-transparent text-[#557086] hover:bg-white/30 hover:text-[#194b75]"}`}
              >
                <FolderOpen aria-hidden="true" size={15} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                All files
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={view === "favorites"}
                onClick={() => {
                  cancelSelection();
                  setView("favorites");
                  setCurrentPage(1);
                }}
                className={`${viewButtonClass} ${view === "favorites" ? "border-white/80 bg-white/70 text-[#ad6f12] shadow-[0_3px_12px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)]" : "border-transparent bg-transparent text-[#557086] hover:bg-white/30 hover:text-[#194b75]"}`}
              >
                <Star
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  size={15}
                  strokeWidth={1.8}
                  className={view === "favorites" ? "fill-current text-[#e79a1b]" : ""}
                />
                Favorites
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          <SearchField
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(value) => {
              cancelSelection();
              setSearchQuery(value);
              setCurrentPage(1);
            }}
            placeholder="Search files..."
            ariaLabel="Search files by name, type, or ID"
            className={searchFieldClass}
            inputClassName="w-full min-w-0 border-0 bg-transparent text-[10px] text-[#24445d] outline-none placeholder:text-[#98aab7]"
            iconSize={17}
            iconStrokeWidth={1.8}
            clearLabel="Clear file search"
            onClear={() => {
              cancelSelection();
              setSearchQuery("");
              setCurrentPage(1);
            }}
          />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowUploadDialog(true)}
            disabled={uploading}
            className="primary-action flex-none disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-wait max-[760px]:min-h-[42px] max-[760px]:w-full"
          >
            <Upload aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            <span>{uploading ? "Uploading..." : "Upload files"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <input
          ref={replaceInputRef}
          type="file"
          accept={SUPPORTED_FILE_ACCEPT}
          className="hidden"
          onChange={handleReplace}
        />
      </section>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-2">
        <div
          className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"
          role="group"
          aria-label={
            view === "favorites" ? "Filter favorite files by category" : "Filter files by category"
          }
        >
          {FILE_CATEGORY_FILTERS.map((category) => (
            <button
              key={category}
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={categoryFilter === category}
              onClick={() => {
                cancelSelection();
                setCategoryFilter(category);
                setCurrentPage(1);
              }}
              className={`secondary-action min-h-[32px] px-3 py-1 text-[9px] ${categoryFilter === category ? "!border-white/80 !bg-white/70 !text-[#145a99] shadow-[0_3px_12px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)]" : ""}`}
            >
              {category}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {!selectionMode && selectableFiles.length > 0 && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={startSelection}
            className="secondary-action inline-flex min-h-[32px] flex-none items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[9px] border border-white/75 bg-white/45 px-3 py-1 text-[9px] shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.85)] backdrop-blur-md transition-colors hover:bg-white/75 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-1 focus-visible:outline-[var(--focus-ring)]"
          >
            <ListChecks aria-hidden="true" size={14} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            Select files
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {selectionMode && (
        <BulkSelectionToolbar
          selectedCount={selectedIds.size}
          visibleCount={selectableFiles.length}
          disabled={bulkPending}
          onSelectAll={() => selectAll(selectableFiles.map((file) => file.id))}
          onCancel={cancelSelection}
          actions={
            view === "favorites" ? (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={!selectedIds.size || bulkPending}
                  onClick={() => setBulkAction("unfavorite")}
                  className="primary-action disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Remove from Favorites
                </button>
                {selectedIds.size > 0 && allSelectedFilesManageable && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={bulkPending}
                    onClick={() => setBulkAction("delete")}
                    className="danger-gradient-action inline-flex min-h-[36px] items-center rounded-[10px] px-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Delete permanently
                  </button>
                )}
              </>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={!selectedIds.size || bulkPending}
                onClick={() => setBulkAction("delete")}
                className="danger-gradient-action inline-flex min-h-[36px] items-center rounded-[10px] px-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Delete selected
              </button>
            )
          }
        />
      )}

      {error && <ErrorBanner message={error} />}

      {successMessage && (
        <p
          role="status"
          className="glass-alert-success rounded-[11px] px-3 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium text-[#34734b]"
        >
          {successMessage}
        </p>
      )}

      <section className="min-w-0" aria-label={view === "favorites" ? "Favorite files" : "Files"}>
        {files.length === 0 ? (
          <section className="surface flex min-h-[300px] flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-[18px] px-[22px] py-[38px] text-center">
            <span className="grid h-[62px] w-[62px] place-items-center rounded-[19px] border border-white/80 bg-white/50 text-[var(--accent)] shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.9),0_6px_18px_rgba(26,54,80,0.06)] backdrop-blur-md">
              {view === "favorites" ? (
                <Star aria-hidden="true" size={29} strokeWidth={1.45} />
              ) : searchQuery ? (
                <Search aria-hidden="true" size={29} strokeWidth={1.45} />
              ) : (
                <FilePlus2 aria-hidden="true" size={29} strokeWidth={1.45} />
              )}
            </span>

            <h3 className="mt-4 mb-0 text-[15px] font-[650] text-[#29445d]">
              {view === "favorites"
                ? searchQuery
                  ? "No favorite files found"
                  : "No favorite files yet"
                : totalItems === 0 && !searchQuery.trim() && categoryFilter === "All"
                  ? "No files uploaded yet"
                  : categoryFilter !== "All" && searchQuery.trim()
                    ? "No files match these filters"
                    : categoryFilter !== "All"
                      ? `No ${categoryFilter.toLocaleLowerCase()} found`
                      : "No files match your search"}
            </h3>

            <p className="mt-[7px] mb-[18px] max-w-[390px] text-[10px] leading-[1.6] text-[#8497a7]">
              {view === "favorites"
                ? searchQuery
                  ? "Try a different filename, type, or ID."
                  : "Favorite a file to keep it close at hand."
                : totalItems === 0 && !searchQuery.trim() && categoryFilter === "All"
                  ? "Upload a supported image, document, video, audio file, or ZIP archive to start building your library."
                  : "Try changing your search terms."}
            </p>

            {view === "favorites" && !searchQuery && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setView("all");
                  setCurrentPage(1);
                }}
                className="secondary-action"
              >
                <FolderOpen aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                Browse all files
              </button>
            )}

            {totalItems === 0 &&
              !searchQuery.trim() &&
              categoryFilter === "All" &&
              view === "all" && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowUploadDialog(true)}
                  className="primary-action"
                >
                  <Upload aria-hidden="true" size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                  Upload your first file
                </button>
              )}
          </section>
        ) : (
          <FileCardGrid
            files={files}
            previewUrls={previewUrls}
            favoritePendingIds={favoritePendingIds}
            loadPreview={loadPreview}
            canManage={(file) => file.canManage}
            onFavoriteToggle={(file) => void toggleFavorite(file)}
            onReplace={openReplace}
            onDetails={(file) => void handleShowFileDetails(file)}
            onPreview={(file, source) => {
              setPreviewFile(file);
              setPreviewSource(source);
            }}
            onDownload={(file) => void handleDownload(file)}
            onDelete={(file) => void handleDelete(file.id)}
            selectionMode={selectionMode}
            selectionDisabled={bulkPending}
            focusedFileId={focusedFileId}
            selectedIds={selectedIds}
            selectionEligible={(file) => view === "favorites" || file.canManage}
            onSelectionToggle={(file) => toggle(file.id)}
          />
        )}

        {totalItems > 0 && (
          <Pagination
            currentPage={currentPage}
            pageCount={pageCount}
            onPageChange={(page) => {
              cancelSelection();
              setCurrentPage(page);
            }}
          />
        )}
      </section>

      {fileDetails && <FileDetailsDialog file={fileDetails} onClose={() => setFileDetails(null)} />}

      {bulkAction && (
        <BulkDeleteDialog
          count={selectedFiles.length}
          title={
            bulkAction === "unfavorite"
              ? `Remove ${selectedFiles.length} ${selectedFiles.length === 1 ? "file" : "files"} from Favorites?`
              : `Permanently delete ${selectedFiles.length} ${selectedFiles.length === 1 ? "file" : "files"}?`
          }
          description={
            bulkAction === "unfavorite"
              ? "This removes only your Favorites entries. The underlying files will remain available."
              : "This permanently deletes the selected files and their stored content. This action cannot be undone."
          }
          confirmLabel={
            bulkAction === "unfavorite" ? "Remove from Favorites" : "Delete permanently"
          }
          pendingLabel={bulkAction === "unfavorite" ? "Removingâ€¦" : "Deletingâ€¦"}
          destructive={bulkAction === "delete"}
          pending={bulkPending}
          onCancel={() => setBulkAction(null)}
          onConfirm={() => void runBulkAction()}
        />
      )}

      {previewFile && (
        <FilePreviewModal
          file={previewFile}
          src={previewSource ?? previewUrls[previewFile.id]}
          onClose={() => {
            setPreviewFile(null);
            setPreviewSource(undefined);
          }}
        />
      )}
      {showUploadDialog && (
        <MultiFileUploadDialog
          title="Upload files"
          description="Add multiple supported files to your personal library"
          onUpload={(file) => {
            if (!account) throw new Error("Your session is unavailable. Sign in again.");
            return uploadFile(instance, account, file);
          }}
          onUploadingChange={setUploading}
          onComplete={async (successCount, failureCount) => {
            setError(null);
            setSuccessMessage(
              `Uploads finished: ${successCount} successful, ${failureCount} failed.`,
            );
            if (successCount > 0) {
              setView("all");
              setCategoryFilter("All");
              setCurrentPage(1);
              await loadFiles("all", 1, "All");
            }
          }}
          onClose={() => setShowUploadDialog(false)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
