import { z } from "zod";
import { ApiError, type PagedResponse } from "@/shared/types/api";
import type { AdminFileResponse, FileResponse } from "@/features/files/types/file";

const positiveId = z.number().int().safe().positive();
const validDate = z.string().min(1).refine((value) => Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)));

export const FileResponseSchema = z.object({
  id: positiveId,
  originalFileName: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  contentType: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  fileSize: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
  uploadedAt: validDate,
  url: z.string(),
  downloadUrl: z.string(),
  isFavorite: z.boolean(),
  canManage: z.boolean(),
  canDownload: z.boolean(),
});

export const AdminFileResponseSchema = z.object({
  id: positiveId,
  originalFileName: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  contentType: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  fileSize: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
  uploadedAt: validDate,
  uploadedBy: positiveId,
  uploaderName: z.string().refine((value) => value.trim().length > 0),
  albumId: positiveId.nullable(),
  albumName: z.string().nullable(),
});

export const StorageCleanupResponseSchema = z.object({ storageCleanupComplete: z.boolean() });
export const PaginationMetadataSchema = z.object({
  totalItems: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
  page: z.number().int().safe().positive(),
  pageSize: z.number().int().safe().positive(),
  pageCount: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
});

function invalidFileResponse(): never {
  throw new ApiError(
    "WinCapture received invalid file information. Refresh and try again.",
    502,
    undefined,
    undefined,
    "INVALID_FILE_RESPONSE",
  );
}

function parse<T>(schema: z.ZodType<T>, value: unknown): T {
  const result = schema.safeParse(value);
  if (!result.success) invalidFileResponse();
  return result.data;
}

export function parseFileResponse(value: unknown): FileResponse {
  return parse(FileResponseSchema, value);
}

export function parseAdminFileResponse(value: unknown): AdminFileResponse {
  return parse(AdminFileResponseSchema, value);
}

export function parsePagedResponse<T>(value: unknown, parseItem: (item: unknown) => T): PagedResponse<T> {
  const result = z.object({
    items: z.array(z.unknown()),
    ...PaginationMetadataSchema.shape,
  }).safeParse(value);
  if (!result.success) invalidFileResponse();
  return { ...result.data, items: result.data.items.map(parseItem) };
}

export function parseStorageCleanupResponse(value: unknown): { storageCleanupComplete: boolean } {
  return parse(StorageCleanupResponseSchema, value);
}

import type JSZip from "jszip";

export type PreviewContent =
  | { kind: "text"; text: string }
  | { kind: "table"; rows: string[][]; sheetName?: string }
  | {
      kind: "slide";
      width: number;
      height: number;
      shapes: {
        text: string;
        x: number;
        y: number;
        width: number;
        height: number;
      }[];
    }
  | {
      kind: "archive";
      entries: { name: string; size?: number; directory: boolean }[];
    };

const MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES = 2000;
const MAX_PREVIEW_XML_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024;

function uncompressedSize(file: JSZip.JSZipObject): number | undefined {
  const data: unknown = Reflect.get(file, "_data");
  if (typeof data !== "object" || data === null) return undefined;
  const size: unknown = Reflect.get(data, "uncompressedSize");
  return typeof size === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(size) && size >= 0 ? size : undefined;
}

async function readZipText(
  zip: JSZip,
  path: string,
  maxBytes = MAX_PREVIEW_XML_BYTES,
): Promise<string | undefined> {
  const file = zip.file(path);
  if (!file) return undefined;
  const size = uncompressedSize(file);
  if (size === undefined || size > maxBytes)
    throw new Error("This file contains an oversized preview section.");
  return file.async("string");
}

export function parseDelimitedText(text: string, delimiter = ","): string[][] {
  const rows: string[][] = [];
  let row: string[] = [];
  let cell = "";
  let quoted = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const character = text[i];
    if (character === '"') {
      if (quoted && text[i + 1] === '"') {
        cell += '"';
        i++;
      } else quoted = !quoted;
    } else if (character === delimiter && !quoted) {
      row.push(cell);
      cell = "";
    } else if ((character === "\n" || character === "\r") && !quoted) {
      if (character === "\r" && text[i + 1] === "\n") i++;
      row.push(cell);
      cell = "";
      if (row.some((value) => value.length > 0)) rows.push(row);
      row = [];
    } else cell += character;
  }
  if (cell.length || row.length) {
    row.push(cell);
    rows.push(row);
  }
  return rows;
}

async function getArrayBuffer(url: string): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  const response = await fetch(url);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("The preview content could not be read.");
  return response.arrayBuffer();
}

function xmlText(element: Element, localName: string): string {
  return Array.from(element.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", localName))
    .map((item) => item.textContent ?? "")
    .join("");
}

async function parsePowerPoint(buffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<PreviewContent> {
  const JSZip = (await import("jszip")).default;
  const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buffer, { checkCRC32: false });
  const slideFile = zip.file("ppt/slides/slide1.xml");
  if (!slideFile) throw new Error("The first slide could not be found.");
  const [slideXml, presentationXml] = await Promise.all([
    readZipText(zip, "ppt/slides/slide1.xml"),
    readZipText(zip, "ppt/presentation.xml", 1024 * 1024),
  ]);
  if (!slideXml) throw new Error("The first slide could not be read.");
  const parser = new DOMParser();
  const slide = parser.parseFromString(slideXml, "application/xml");
  const presentation = parser.parseFromString(presentationXml ?? "", "application/xml");
  const dimensions = presentation.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "sldSz")[0];
  const width = Number(dimensions?.getAttribute("cx")) || 9144000;
  const height = Number(dimensions?.getAttribute("cy")) || 5143500;
  const shapes = Array.from(slide.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "sp"))
    .map((shape) => {
      const transform = shape.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "xfrm")[0];
      const offset = transform?.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "off")[0];
      const extent = transform?.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "ext")[0];
      const text = xmlText(shape, "t").trim();
      if (!text) return null;
      return {
        text,
        x: Number(offset?.getAttribute("x")) || 0,
        y: Number(offset?.getAttribute("y")) || 0,
        width: Number(extent?.getAttribute("cx")) || width,
        height: Number(extent?.getAttribute("cy")) || height,
      };
    })
    .filter((shape): shape is NonNullable<typeof shape> => shape !== null);
  return { kind: "slide", width, height, shapes };
}

function resolveZipPath(base: string, target: string): string {
  const parts = (target.startsWith("/") ? target.slice(1) : `${base}/${target}`).split("/");
  const resolved: string[] = [];
  for (const part of parts) {
    if (!part || part === ".") continue;
    if (part === "..") resolved.pop();
    else resolved.push(part);
  }
  return resolved.join("/");
}

async function parseWorkbook(buffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<PreviewContent> {
  const JSZip = (await import("jszip")).default;
  const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buffer, { checkCRC32: false });
  const [workbookXml, relationshipsXml, sharedXml] = await Promise.all([
    readZipText(zip, "xl/workbook.xml", 1024 * 1024),
    readZipText(zip, "xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels", 1024 * 1024),
    readZipText(zip, "xl/sharedStrings.xml", 8 * 1024 * 1024),
  ]);
  if (!workbookXml) throw new Error("This workbook could not be read.");
  const parser = new DOMParser();
  const workbook = parser.parseFromString(workbookXml, "application/xml");
  const firstSheet = workbook.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "sheet")[0];
  if (!firstSheet) throw new Error("This workbook contains no worksheets.");
  const relationshipId = firstSheet.getAttribute("r:id") ?? firstSheet.getAttributeNS("*", "id");
  const relationships = parser.parseFromString(relationshipsXml ?? "", "application/xml");
  const relationship = Array.from(relationships.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "Relationship")).find(
    (item) => item.getAttribute("Id") === relationshipId,
  );
  const sheetPath = resolveZipPath(
    "xl",
    relationship?.getAttribute("Target") ?? "worksheets/sheet1.xml",
  );
  const sheetXml = await readZipText(zip, sheetPath);
  if (!sheetXml) throw new Error("The first worksheet could not be found.");
  const sheet = parser.parseFromString(sheetXml, "application/xml");
  const shared = sharedXml ? parser.parseFromString(sharedXml, "application/xml") : undefined;
  const sharedStrings = shared
    ? Array.from(shared.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "si"), (item) => xmlText(item, "t"))
    : [];
  const columnIndex = (reference: string) => {
    let value = 0;
    for (const character of reference.match(/^[A-Z]+/i)?.[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? "")
      value = value * 26 + character.charCodeAt(0) - 64;
    return Math.max(0, value - 1);
  };
  const rows = Array.from(sheet.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "row"))
    .slice(0, 500)
    .map((row) => {
      const cells = Array.from(row.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "c"))
        .map((cell) => {
          const reference = cell.getAttribute("r") ?? "A1";
          const type = cell.getAttribute("t");
          const value = cell.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "v")[0]?.textContent ?? "";
          const inlineText = cell.getElementsByTagNameNS("*", "is")[0];
          const text =
            type === "s"
              ? (sharedStrings[Number(value)] ?? "")
              : type === "inlineStr" && inlineText
                ? xmlText(inlineText, "t")
                : type === "b"
                  ? value === "1"
                    ? "TRUE"
                    : "FALSE"
                  : value;
          return { column: columnIndex(reference), text };
        })
        .filter((cell) => cell.column < 30);
      const maxColumn = Math.max(-1, ...cells.map((cell) => cell.column));
      const values = Array.from({ length: maxColumn + 1 }, () => "");
      for (const cell of cells) values[cell.column] = cell.text;
      return values;
    });
  return {
    kind: "table",
    sheetName: firstSheet.getAttribute("name") ?? "Sheet 1",
    rows,
  };
}

export async function readFilePreview(url: string, extension: string): Promise<PreviewContent> {
  const buffer = await getArrayBuffer(url);
  if (extension === ".txt") return { kind: "text", text: new TextDecoder().decode(buffer) };
  if (extension === ".csv")
    return {
      kind: "table",
      rows: parseDelimitedText(new TextDecoder().decode(buffer)),
    };
  if (extension === ".docx") {
    const JSZip = (await import("jszip")).default;
    const docx = await JSZip.loadAsync(buffer, { checkCRC32: false });
    const totalExpandedSize = Object.values(docx.files).reduce(
      (sum, entry) => sum + (uncompressedSize(entry) ?? 0),
      0,
    );
    if (totalExpandedSize > 32 * 1024 * 1024)
      throw new Error("This document expands beyond the safe preview limit.");
    if (!(await readZipText(docx, "word/document.xml")))
      throw new Error("The main document content could not be found.");
    const mammoth = (await import("mammoth/mammoth.browser.js")).default;
    const result = await mammoth.extractRawText({ arrayBuffer: buffer });
    if (!result.value.trim()) throw new Error("This document contains no readable text.");
    return { kind: "text", text: result.value };
  }
  if (extension === ".xlsx") {
    return parseWorkbook(buffer);
  }
  if (extension === ".pptx") return parsePowerPoint(buffer);
  if (extension === ".zip") {
    const JSZip = (await import("jszip")).default;
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buffer, { checkCRC32: false });
    const entries = Object.values(zip.files)
      .slice(0, MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES)
      .map((entry) => ({
        name: entry.name,
        directory: entry.dir,
        size: uncompressedSize(entry),
      }));
    return { kind: "archive", entries };
  }
  throw new Error("This file type does not have a readable preview.");
}

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import { PublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";

import { App } from "./App";
import { AppProviders } from "@/app/providers/AppProviders";
import { appShellClass } from "@/shared/components/ui/uiClasses";
import { msalConfig, validateEnvironment } from "@/config/auth";

import "@/styles/index.css";

const rootElement = document.getElementById("root");

if (!rootElement) {
  throw new Error("Could not find the root element.");
}

const root = ReactDOM.createRoot(rootElement);

function showStartupError(error: unknown) {
  const missingConfiguration =
    error instanceof Error &&
    /^Missing environment variables: VITE_[A-Z0-9_, ]+$/.test(error.message)
      ? error.message
      : null;
  const message =
    missingConfiguration ??
    "WinCapture could not initialize Microsoft sign-in. Check the app configuration and retry.";

  // Show a readable error screen if environment validation or MSAL startup fails.
  root.render(
    <main className={`${appShellClass} min-h-screen px-5 py-8 sm:px-8 sm:py-12`}>
      <section className="mx-auto flex min-h-[calc(100vh-4rem)] w-full max-w-2xl items-center justify-center">
        <div className="surface w-full overflow-hidden rounded-[24px] p-6 sm:p-8">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <span className="grid h-11 w-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-[13px] border border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-600 shadow-sm">
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-lg font-bold">
                !
              </span>
            </span>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">
                WinCapture
              </p>

              <h1 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold tracking-[-0.025em] text-slate-800 sm:text-2xl">
                Could not start the app
              </h1>

              <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                WinCapture could not complete itsstartup configuration. Check themessage below and
                correct the environment configuration before starting the application again.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 rounded-[15px] border border-red-100 bg-red-50/75 p-4">
            <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6 text-red-700">
              {message}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>,
  );
}

async function startApp() {
  try {
    const missingEnvironment = validateEnvironment();

    if (missingEnvironment.length > 0) {
      throw new Error(`Missing environment variables: ${missingEnvironment.join(", ")}`);
    }

    const msalInstance = new PublicClientApplication(msalConfig);

    // Initialize MSAL before rendering components that use its context.
    await msalInstance.initialize();

    root.render(
      <React.StrictMode>
        <AppProviders msalInstance={msalInstance}>
          <App />
        </AppProviders>
      </React.StrictMode>,
    );
  } catch (error) {
    showStartupError(error);
  }
}

void startApp();

export function buildPagePath(
  path: string,
  page: number,
  pageSize: number,
  search?: string,
  category?: string,
): string {
  const query = new URLSearchParams({
    page: String(page),
    pageSize: String(pageSize),
  });

  const normalizedSearch = search?.trim();

  if (normalizedSearch) {
    query.set("search", normalizedSearch);
  }

  // "All" means no category restriction, so omit it from the query string.
  if (category && category !== "All") {
    query.set("category", category);
  }

  return `${path}?${query.toString()}`;
}

import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { callApi } from "@/shared/api/request";

export async function getFilePreviewUrlByPath(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  contentType?: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, path);

  const responseBlob = await response.blob();

  // Preserve the known MIME type so the browser can render the preview correctly.
  const previewBlob = contentType ? new Blob([responseBlob], { type: contentType }) : responseBlob;

  return URL.createObjectURL(previewBlob);
}

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 30_000,
      gcTime: 5 * 60_000,
      retry: (failureCount, error) => {
        const status = (error as { status?: number }).status;
        if (status === 0 || status === 401 || status === 403 || status === 404) return false;
        return failureCount < 2;
      },
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
    },
    mutations: { retry: false },
  },
});

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\shared\api\request.ts
============================================================

import {
  InteractionRequiredAuthError,
  type AccountInfo,
  type IPublicClientApplication,
} from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { apiBaseUrl, apiScope } from "@/config/auth";
import { ApiError } from "@/shared/types/api";

const interactiveTokenRequests = new WeakMap<IPublicClientApplication, Promise<void>>();

function beginInteractiveTokenAcquisition(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
): Promise<void> {
  const existingRequest = interactiveTokenRequests.get(instance);
  if (existingRequest) return existingRequest;

  const request = instance.acquireTokenRedirect({
    account,
    scopes: [apiScope],
    redirectStartPage: window.location.href,
  });
  interactiveTokenRequests.set(instance, request);
  void request.catch(() => {
    if (interactiveTokenRequests.get(instance) === request) {
      interactiveTokenRequests.delete(instance);
    }
  });
  return request;
}

async function acquireAccessToken(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
): Promise<string> {
  try {
    const result = await instance.acquireTokenSilent({ account, scopes: [apiScope] });
    if (!result.accessToken) {
      throw new ApiError(
        "Microsoft Entra did not return an access token for the WinCapture API.",
        401,
      );
    }
    return result.accessToken;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError) throw error;
    if (error instanceof InteractionRequiredAuthError) {
      try {
        await beginInteractiveTokenAcquisition(instance, account);
      } catch (interactionError) {
        throw new ApiError(
          interactionError instanceof Error
            ? `Microsoft sign-in could not be completed: ${interactionError.message}`
            : "Microsoft sign-in could not be completed. Please try signing in again.",
          401,
        );
      }
      interactiveTokenRequests.delete(instance);
      throw new ApiError(
        "Microsoft sign-in or consent is required. Complete the sign-in prompt and retry.",
        401,
      );
    }
    throw new ApiError("Unable to acquire the Microsoft access token for the WinCapture API.", 401);
  }
}

function getUserErrorMessage(status: number): string {
  if (status === 400)
    return "Some of the submitted information is invalid. Review it and try again.";
  if (status === 401) return "Your Microsoft session has expired. Sign in again and retry.";
  if (status === 403) return "You do not have permission to complete this action.";
  if (status === 404) return "The requested item could not be found.";
  if (status === 409) return "This change conflicts with the current data. Refresh and try again.";
  if (status === 413) return "The selected file is too large to upload.";
  if (status >= 500) return "The service encountered a problem. Please try again later.";
  return "The request could not be completed. Please try again.";
}

export async function callApi(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  options: RequestInit = {},
): Promise<Response> {
  const accessToken = await acquireAccessToken(instance, account);
  const headers = new Headers(options.headers);
  headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${accessToken}`);
  if (options.body && !(options.body instanceof FormData)) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...options, headers });
  } catch (error) {
    if (options.signal?.aborted) throw error;
    throw new ApiError(
      "The WinCapture API could not be reached. Ensure the backend is running and reachable.",
      0,
    );
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    const correlationId =
      response.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? response.headers.get("request-id") ?? undefined;
    throw new ApiError(
      getUserErrorMessage(response.status),
      response.status,
      undefined,
      correlationId,
    );
  }
  return response;
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\shared\api\transfers.ts
============================================================

import type { AccountInfo, IPublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { callApi } from "@/shared/api/request";
// Shared helper for uploading and replacing files in personal and album endpoints.
export async function uploadMultipartFile(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  method: "POST" | "PUT",
  file: File,
): Promise<unknown> {
  const formData = new FormData();
  formData.append("file", file);

  // Do not set Content-Type manually; fetch adds the required multipart boundary.
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, path, {
    method,
    body: formData,
  });

  const payload: unknown = await response.json();
  return payload;
}

export async function downloadResponse(
  instance: IPublicClientApplication,
  account: AccountInfo,
  path: string,
  fallbackFileName: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const response = await callApi(instance, account, path);

  const blob = await response.blob();
  const fileName = extractFileNameFromHeaders(response, fallbackFileName);

  triggerBrowserDownload(blob, fileName);
}

// Binary Download Utilities
function extractFileNameFromHeaders(response: Response, fallback: string): string {
  const disposition = response.headers.get("content-disposition");

  if (!disposition) {
    return fallback;
  }

  const match = disposition.match(/filename\*?=(?:UTF-8'')?["']?([^"';]+)["']?/i);

  return match?.[1] ? decodeURIComponent(match[1]) : fallback;
}

function triggerBrowserDownload(blob: Blob, filename: string): void {
  const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const anchor = document.createElement("a");

  anchor.href = url;
  anchor.download = filename;

  document.body.appendChild(anchor);
  anchor.click();
  document.body.removeChild(anchor);

  // Release the temporary URL created for this download.
  window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

          Previous

        >
          Next
        </button>

  );
}

  inputClassName: string;

}

export function SearchField({
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  ariaLabel,
  className,
  inputClassName,
  iconSize = 16,
  iconStrokeWidth,
  clearLabel,
  onClear,
}: SearchFieldProps) {

    <label className={className}>
      <Search aria-hidden="true" size={iconSize} strokeWidth={iconStrokeWidth} />

        type="text"
        value={value}

        placeholder={placeholder}
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        className={inputClassName}
      />
      {onClear && clearLabel && value && (
        <button
          type="button"

          onClick={onClear}
        >
          <X aria-hidden="true" size={15} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        </button>
      )}
    </label>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\shared\hooks\useBulkSelection.ts
============================================================

import { useCallback, useState } from "react";

export function useBulkSelection<T extends string | number>() {
  const [selectionMode, setSelectionMode] = useState(false);
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<T>>(() => new Set());
  const startSelection = useCallback(() => setSelectionMode(true), []);
  const cancelSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectionMode(false);
    setSelectedIds(new Set());
  }, []);
  const toggle = useCallback(
    (id: T) =>
      setSelectedIds((current) => {
        const next = new Set(current);
        if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
        else next.add(id);
        return next;
      }),
    [],
  );
  const selectAll = useCallback((ids: readonly T[]) => setSelectedIds(new Set(ids)), []);
  const remove = useCallback(
    (ids: readonly T[]) =>
      setSelectedIds((current) => {
        const next = new Set(current);
        ids.forEach((id) => next.delete(id));
        return next;
      }),
    [],
  );
  const retain = useCallback(
    (ids: readonly T[]) =>
      setSelectedIds((current) => new Set([...current].filter((id) => ids.includes(id)))),
    [],
  );
  return {
    selectionMode,
    selectedIds,
    startSelection,
    cancelSelection,
    toggle,
    selectAll,
    remove,
    retain,
  };
}

export interface PagedResponse<T> {
  items: T[];
  totalItems: number;
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  public readonly code: string;

  constructor(
    message: string,
    public readonly status: number,
    public readonly details?: unknown,
    public readonly correlationId?: string,
    code?: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.code = code ?? (status === 0 ? "NETWORK_ERROR" : `HTTP_${status}`);
  }
}

import type { AccountInfo } from "@azure/msal-browser";

// MSAL may provide a new AccountInfo object for the same signed-in identity.
// Effects that fetch account-scoped data should depend on this stable key.
export function getAccountKey(account: AccountInfo | null): string | null {
  if (!account) return null;
  return `${account.homeAccountId}|${account.tenantId}|${account.localAccountId}`;
}

export type AdminContentScope = "mine" | "all";

const ADMIN_CONTENT_SCOPE_KEY = "wincapture.admin-content-scope";

export function getAdminContentScope(): AdminContentScope {
  try {
    return sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_CONTENT_SCOPE_KEY) === "all" ? "all" : "mine";
  } catch {
    return "mine";
  }
}

export function setAdminContentScope(scope: AdminContentScope): void {
  try {
    sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_CONTENT_SCOPE_KEY, scope);
  } catch {
    // Keep the dashboard usable if browser storage is unavailable.
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend\src\shared\utils\formatters.ts
============================================================

export function formatDate(value: string): string {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

export function formatFileSize(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes >= 1024 * 1024) {
    return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
  }

  if (bytes >= 1024) {
    return `${Math.round(bytes / 1024)} KB`;
  }

  return `${bytes} B`;
}

export function formatFileType(contentType: string): string {
  const [type, subtype] = contentType.split("/");

  return subtype ? subtype.toUpperCase() : (type ?? "Unknown file type").toUpperCase();
}

export function getInitials(name?: string): string {
  if (!name?.trim()) {
    return "U";
  }

  const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/);

  if (parts.length === 1) {
    return (parts[0] ?? "U").slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  }

  return `${parts[0]?.[0] ?? "U"}${parts.at(-1)?.[0] ?? ""}`.toUpperCase();
}

const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  resolve: {
    alias: { "@": path.resolve(__dirname, "src") },

  server: {
    host: "localhost",

      "/angular": {

  selector: "app-root",

  template: "<router-outlet />",
})

============================================================
FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend-angular\src\app\app.config.ts
============================================================

import {
  ApplicationConfig,
  importProvidersFrom,
  provideAppInitializer,
  inject,
} from "@angular/core";
import { provideHttpClient } from "@angular/common/http";
import { provideRouter } from "@angular/router";
import { MsalModule, MsalService } from "@azure/msal-angular";
import { InteractionType, PublicClientApplication } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { routes } from "./app.routes";
import { msalConfig, apiScopes } from "./core/auth/msal.config";

const msalInstance = new PublicClientApplication(msalConfig);

export const appConfig: ApplicationConfig = {
  providers: [
    provideRouter(routes),
    provideHttpClient(),
    importProvidersFrom(
      MsalModule.forRoot(
        msalInstance,
        { interactionType: InteractionType.Popup, authRequest: { scopes: apiScopes } },
        { interactionType: InteractionType.Popup, protectedResourceMap: new Map() },
      ),
    ),
    provideAppInitializer(() => {
      const msal = inject(MsalService);
      return msal.instance.initialize();
    }),
  ],
};

import { Routes } from "@angular/router";
import { AppShellComponent } from "./app-shell.component";
import { AlbumsPageComponent } from "./features/albums/albums-page.component";

export const routes: Routes = [
  {
    path: "",
    component: AppShellComponent,
    children: [
      { path: "albums", component: AlbumsPageComponent },
      { path: "", pathMatch: "full", redirectTo: "albums" },
      { path: "**", redirectTo: "albums" },
    ],
  },
];

import { Component, ElementRef, HostListener, ViewChild, inject, signal } from "@angular/core";
import { RouterLink, RouterLinkActive, RouterOutlet } from "@angular/router";
import { AuthService } from "./core/auth/auth.service";
import { environment } from "../environments/environment";

@Component({
  selector: "app-shell",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterLink, RouterLinkActive, RouterOutlet],
  templateUrl: "./app-shell.component.html",
})
export class AppShellComponent {
  @ViewChild("profileContainer") private profileElement?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>;
  @ViewChild("profileTrigger") private profileTrigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>;
  readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  readonly mobileOpen = signal(false);
  readonly profileOpen = signal(false);
  private readonly reactOrigin = new URL(environment.reactAlbumsUrl).origin;

  reactPage(path: string): string {
    return new URL(path, this.reactOrigin).toString();
  }

  initials(name: string | undefined): string {
    const parts = name?.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean) ?? [];
    return parts.length === 0
      ? "U"
      : parts
          .slice(0, 2)
          .map((part) => part[0])
          .join("")
          .toUpperCase();
  }

  closeMobileNavigation(): void {
    this.mobileOpen.set(false);
  }

  toggleMobileNavigation(): void {
    this.mobileOpen.update((open) => !open);
  }

  toggleProfileMenu(): void {
    this.profileOpen.update((open) => !open);
  }

  @HostListener("document:pointerdown", ["$event"])
  closeProfileOnOutsideClick(event: PointerEvent): void {
    const target = event.target;
    if (target instanceof Node && !this.profileElement?.nativeElement.contains(target)) {
      this.profileOpen.set(false);
    }
  }

  @HostListener("document:keydown.escape")
  closeProfileOnEscape(): void {
    if (!this.profileOpen()) return;
    this.profileOpen.set(false);
    this.profileTrigger?.nativeElement.focus();
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend-angular\src\app\core\api\api.service.ts
============================================================

import { HttpClient, HttpErrorResponse, HttpHeaders, HttpParams } from "@angular/common/http";
import { Injectable, inject } from "@angular/core";
import { MsalService } from "@azure/msal-angular";
import { InteractionRequiredAuthError, type AccountInfo } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { firstValueFrom } from "rxjs";
import { environment } from "../../../environments/environment";
import { apiScopes } from "../auth/msal.config";
import type {
  AlbumFilter,
  AlbumResponse,
  CurrentUser,
  CreateAlbumRequest,
  FileCategory,
  FileResponse,
  PagedResponse,
} from "../models";

@Injectable({ providedIn: "root" })
export class ApiService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);
  private readonly msal = inject(MsalService);
  private readonly baseUrl = environment.apiBaseUrl.replace(/\/$/, "");

  getAlbums(
    account: AccountInfo,
    filter: AlbumFilter,
    page: number,
    pageSize: number,
    search: string,
  ): Promise<PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>> {
    const suffix = filter === "mine" ? "/mine" : filter === "shared" ? "/shared" : "";
    return this.getPaged<AlbumResponse>(`/api/albums${suffix}`, account, page, pageSize, search);
  }

  createAlbum(account: AccountInfo, request: CreateAlbumRequest): Promise<AlbumResponse> {
    return this.send<AlbumResponse>("/api/albums", account, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(request),
    });
  }

  getAlbumImages(account: AccountInfo, albumId: number): Promise<PagedResponse<FileResponse>> {
    return this.getPaged<FileResponse>(
      `/api/albums/${albumId}/files`,
      account,
      1,
      100,
      "",
      "Images",
    );
  }

  async getAlbumCover(account: AccountInfo, albumId: number, file: FileResponse): Promise<string> {
    const canManage = file.canManage;
    const action = canManage ? "download" : "preview";
    const headers = await this.authorizationHeaders(account);
    const blob = await firstValueFrom(
      this.http.get(`${this.baseUrl}/api/albums/${albumId}/files/${file.id}/${action}`, {
        headers,
        responseType: "blob",
      }),
    );
    return URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([blob], { type: file.contentType }));
  }

  getCurrentUser(account: AccountInfo): Promise<CurrentUser> {
    return this.send<CurrentUser>("/api/auth/me", account);
  }

  private getPaged<T>(
    path: string,
    account: AccountInfo,
    page: number,
    pageSize: number,
    search: string,
    category?: FileCategory,
  ): Promise<PagedResponse<T>> {
    let params = new HttpParams().set("page", page).set("pageSize", pageSize);
    if (search.trim()) params = params.set("search", search.trim());
    if (category && category !== "All") params = params.set("category", category);
    return this.send<PagedResponse<T>>(`${path}?${params.toString()}`, account);
  }

  private async send<T>(
    path: string,
    account: AccountInfo,
    options: {
      method?: string;
      body?: string;
    } = {},
  ): Promise<T> {
    const headers = await this.authorizationHeaders(account);
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}${path}`;
    try {
      if (options.method === "POST") {
        const jsonHeaders = headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
        return await firstValueFrom(
          this.http.post<T>(url, options.body ?? "", { headers: jsonHeaders }),
        );
      }
      return await firstValueFrom(this.http.get<T>(url, { headers }));
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof InteractionRequiredAuthError) {
        throw new Error(
          "Microsoft sign-in or consent is required. Choose Sign in again and retry.",
        );
      }
      if (error instanceof HttpErrorResponse) {
        const body = this.getErrorBody(error.error);
        if (error.status === 0) {
          throw new Error(
            "The WinCapture API is unreachable. Check that https://localhost:7106 is running and its certificate is trusted. Browser API calls use the React origin https://localhost:5173.",
          );
        }
        if (error.status === 401) {
          throw new Error(
            "Microsoft sign-in or consent is required. Choose Sign in again and retry.",
          );
        }
        if (error.status === 403) {
          throw new Error(
            body?.message ??
              body?.Message ??
              "Your Microsoft account does not have a WinCapture User or Admin role. Ask an administrator to assign access.",
          );
        }
        throw new Error(
          body?.message ??
            body?.Message ??
            body?.error ??
            body?.Error ??
            `WinCapture API returned HTTP ${error.status}.`,
        );
      }
      if (error instanceof Error) throw error;
      throw new Error("The WinCapture API request failed. Check the backend and try again.");
    }
  }

  private getErrorBody(value: unknown): {
    message?: string;
    Message?: string;
    error?: string;
    Error?: string;
  } | null {
    if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return null;
    return value;
  }

  private async authorizationHeaders(account: AccountInfo): Promise<HttpHeaders> {
    try {
      const result = await this.msal.instance.acquireTokenSilent({ account, scopes: apiScopes });
      return new HttpHeaders({ Authorization: `Bearer ${result.accessToken}` });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof InteractionRequiredAuthError) {
        throw new Error(
          "Microsoft sign-in or consent is required. Choose Sign in again and retry.",
        );
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\ShodhanVemulapalli\Downloads\WinCapture\frontend-angular\src\app\core\auth\auth.service.ts
============================================================

import { Injectable, inject, signal } from "@angular/core";
import { MsalService } from "@azure/msal-angular";
import type { AccountInfo } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { firstValueFrom } from "rxjs";
import { ApiService } from "../api/api.service";
import type { CurrentUser } from "../models";
import { apiErrorMessage } from "../../shared/api/api-error-message";
import { environment } from "../../../environments/environment";

@Injectable({ providedIn: "root" })
export class AuthService {
  private readonly msal = inject(MsalService);
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  readonly account = signal<AccountInfo | null>(null);
  readonly user = signal<CurrentUser | null>(null);
  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly error = signal<string | null>(null);
  private initialized = false;

  async initialize(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.initialized) return;
    this.initialized = true;

    try {
      const redirectResult = await firstValueFrom(this.msal.handleRedirectObservable());
      const account =
        redirectResult?.account ??
        this.msal.instance.getActiveAccount() ??
        this.msal.instance.getAllAccounts()[0] ??
        null;

      if (!account) {
        this.error.set(
          "No signed-in React session was found in this browser tab. Sign in through React, then open Albums again.",
        );
        return;
      }

      this.msal.instance.setActiveAccount(account);
      this.account.set(account);
      await this.loadProfile(account);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.error.set(
        apiErrorMessage(
          error,
          "The shared Microsoft session could not be read. Return to React and sign in again.",
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      this.loading.set(false);
    }
  }

  async signOut(): Promise<void> {
    const account = this.account();
    this.user.set(null);
    this.account.set(null);
    this.error.set(null);
    try {
      await firstValueFrom(
        this.msal.logoutPopup({
          account: account ?? undefined,
          postLogoutRedirectUri: this.msal.instance.getConfiguration().auth?.redirectUri,
        }),
      );
      try {
        sessionStorage.setItem("wincapture.pick-account-after-logout", "true");
      } catch {
        // The React app will still open if session storage is unavailable.
      }
      window.location.assign(new URL("/", environment.reactAlbumsUrl).toString());
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.error.set(apiErrorMessage(error, "Microsoft sign-out could not be completed."));
    }
  }

  private async loadProfile(account: AccountInfo): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const user = await this.api.getCurrentUser(account);
      this.user.set(user);
      this.error.set(null);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.error.set(
        apiErrorMessage(
          error,
          "Microsoft sign-in succeeded, but the WinCapture API could not load your account.",
        ),
      );
    }
  }
}

import type { Configuration } from "@azure/msal-browser";
import { environment } from "../../../environments/environment";

export const msalConfig: Configuration = {
  auth: {
    clientId: environment.msalClientId,
    authority: `https://login.microsoftonline.com/${environment.msalTenantId}`,
    redirectUri: environment.redirectUri,
    postLogoutRedirectUri: environment.redirectUri,
  },
  cache: { cacheLocation: "sessionStorage" },
};

export const apiScopes = [environment.msalApiScope];

export type AlbumFilter = "all" | "mine" | "shared";
export type FileCategory = "All" | "Images" | "Documents" | "Videos" | "Audio" | "Archives";

export interface AlbumResponse {
  id: number;
  albumName: string;
  ownerId: number;
  ownerName: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string | null;
}

export interface CreateAlbumRequest {
  albumName: string;
}

export interface PagedResponse<T> {
  items: T[];
  totalItems: number;
  page: number;
  pageSize: number;
  pageCount: number;
}

export interface FileResponse {
  id: number;
  originalFileName: string;
  contentType: string;
  fileSize: number;
  uploadedAt: string;
  canManage: boolean;
}

export interface CurrentUser {
  userId: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: "User" | "Admin";
}

============================================================

import { Component, DestroyRef, OnInit, computed, inject, signal } from "@angular/core";
import { FormsModule } from "@angular/forms";
import { AuthService } from "../../core/auth/auth.service";
import { ApiService } from "../../core/api/api.service";
import type { AlbumFilter, AlbumResponse, PagedResponse } from "../../core/models";
import { apiErrorMessage } from "../../shared/api/api-error-message";
import { formatDate } from "../../shared/utils/format-date";
import { environment } from "../../../environments/environment";

const PAGE_SIZE = 9;

@Component({
  selector: "app-albums-page",
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule],
  templateUrl: "./albums-page.component.html",
})
export class AlbumsPageComponent implements OnInit {
  readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly destroyRef = inject(DestroyRef);

  readonly albums = signal<AlbumResponse[]>([]);
  readonly currentPage = signal(1);
  readonly pageCount = signal(1);
  readonly totalItems = signal(0);
  readonly filter = signal<AlbumFilter>("all");
  readonly searchInput = signal("");
  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly hasLoaded = signal(false);
  readonly creating = signal(false);
  readonly error = signal<string | null>(null);
  readonly albumName = signal("");
  readonly covers = signal<Record<number, string>>({});
  readonly pages = computed(() =>
    Array.from({ length: this.pageCount() }, (_, index) => index + 1),
  );
  readonly formatDate = formatDate;
  readonly reactAlbumsUrl = environment.reactAlbumsUrl;
  readonly reactDashboardUrl = new URL("/dashboard", environment.reactAlbumsUrl).toString();
  private debounceId: number | undefined;
  private requestVersion = 0;
  private readonly objectUrls = new Set<string>();

  constructor() {
    this.destroyRef.onDestroy(() => {
      if (this.debounceId !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(this.debounceId);
      this.requestVersion += 1;
      this.objectUrls.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
      this.objectUrls.clear();
    });
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    void this.initializePage();
  }

  private async initializePage(): Promise<void> {
    await this.auth.initialize();
    const account = this.auth.account();
    if (account && this.auth.user()) await this.loadAlbums();
    else this.loading.set(false);
  }

  async loadAlbums(): Promise<void> {
    const account = this.auth.account();
    if (!account) return;
    const version = ++this.requestVersion;
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.error.set(null);
    try {
      const result = await this.api.getAlbums(
        account,
        this.filter(),
        this.currentPage(),
        PAGE_SIZE,
        this.searchInput().trim(),
      );
      if (version !== this.requestVersion) return;
      this.applyPage(result);
      void this.loadCovers(result.items, version);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (version === this.requestVersion)
        this.error.set(apiErrorMessage(error, "Unable to load albums."));
    } finally {
      if (version === this.requestVersion) {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.hasLoaded.set(true);
      }
    }
  }

  setFilter(filter: AlbumFilter): void {
    if (this.filter() === filter) return;
    this.filter.set(filter);
    this.currentPage.set(1);
    void this.loadAlbums();
  }

  setSearch(value: string): void {
    this.searchInput.set(value);
    if (this.debounceId !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(this.debounceId);
    this.debounceId = window.setTimeout(() => {
      this.currentPage.set(1);
      void this.loadAlbums();
    }, 300);
  }

  setPage(page: number): void {
    if (page < 1 || page > this.pageCount() || page === this.currentPage()) return;
    this.currentPage.set(page);
    void this.loadAlbums();
  }

  focusCreateField(): void {
    document.getElementById("new-album-name")?.focus();
  }

  filterButtonClass(filter: AlbumFilter): string {
    const base =
      "min-h-[33px] rounded-[8px] border px-3 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-[color,background,box-shadow,border-color]";
    return this.filter() === filter
      ? `${base} border-white/80 bg-white/70 text-[#145a99] shadow-[0_3px_12px_rgba(20,54,84,0.08),inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.95)]`
      : `${base} border-transparent bg-transparent text-[#557086] hover:bg-white/30 hover:text-[#194b75]`;
  }

  async createAlbum(): Promise<void> {
    const account = this.auth.account();
    const albumName = this.albumName().trim();
    if (!account || !albumName) {
      this.error.set("Album name is required.");
      return;
    }
    if (albumName.length > 200) {
      this.error.set("Album name cannot exceed 200 characters.");
      return;
    }
    this.creating.set(true);
    this.error.set(null);
    try {
      const created = await this.api.createAlbum(account, { albumName });
      this.albumName.set("");
      this.openReactAlbum(created);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.error.set(apiErrorMessage(error, "Unable to create album."));
    } finally {
      this.creating.set(false);
    }
  }

  openReactAlbum(album: AlbumResponse): void {
    const destination = new URL(
      `/albums/${encodeURIComponent(String(album.id))}`,
      this.reactAlbumsUrl,
    );
    window.location.assign(destination.toString());
  }

  private applyPage(page: PagedResponse<AlbumResponse>): void {
    this.albums.set(page.items);
    this.currentPage.set(page.page);
    this.pageCount.set(Math.max(page.pageCount, 1));
    this.totalItems.set(page.totalItems);
  }

  private async loadCovers(albums: AlbumResponse[], version: number): Promise<void> {
    const account = this.auth.account();
    if (!account) return;
    this.objectUrls.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
    this.objectUrls.clear();
    this.covers.set({});
    const urls: Record<number, string> = {};
    await Promise.all(
      albums.map(async (album) => {
        try {
          const page = await this.api.getAlbumImages(account, album.id);
          const file = page.items[0];
          if (!file) return;
          const url = await this.api.getAlbumCover(account, album.id, file);
          if (version !== this.requestVersion) {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            return;
          }
          this.objectUrls.add(url);
          urls[album.id] = url;
        } catch {
          // A missing image, restricted preview, or network failure leaves the fallback cover visible.
        }
      }),
    );
    if (version === this.requestVersion) this.covers.set(urls);
  }
}

============================================================

export function apiErrorMessage(error: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "error" in error) {
    const body: unknown = error.error;
    if (typeof body === "object" && body !== null) {
      const message = getStringProperty(body, "message");
      const upperMessage = getStringProperty(body, "Message");
      const errorMessage = getStringProperty(body, "error");
      if (message || upperMessage || errorMessage) {
        return message ?? upperMessage ?? errorMessage ?? fallback;
      }
    }
  }
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    if ("errorCode" in error && error.errorCode === "interaction_in_progress") {
      return "Microsoft sign-in is already in progress. Finish or close that sign-in window and retry.";
    }
    return error.message;
  }
  return fallback;
}

function getStringProperty(value: object, key: string): string | undefined {
  if (!(key in value)) return undefined;
  const property: unknown = Reflect.get(value, key);
  return typeof property === "string" ? property : undefined;
}

============================================================

export function formatDate(value: string): string {
  return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
    month: "short",
    day: "numeric",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}
